Accurate and scalable exchange-correlation with deep learning
Authors: Giulia Luise, Chin-Wei Huang, Thijs Vogels, Derk P. Kooi, Sebastian Ehlert, Stephanie Lanius, Klaas J. H. Giesbertz, Amir Karton, Deniz Gunceler, Stefano Battaglia, Gregor N. C. Simm, P. Bernát Szabó, Megan Stanley, Wessel P. Bruinsma, Lin Huang, Xinran Wei, José Garrido Torres, Abylay Katbashev, Rodrigo Chavez Zavaleta, Bálint Máté, Sékou-Oumar Kaba, Roberto Sordillo, Yingrong Chen, David B. Williams-Young, Christopher M. Bishop, Jan Hermann, Rianne van den Berg, Paola Gori-Giorgi
First: 2025-06-17T15:56:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:59:57+00:00
Comments: The Skala model and inference code are available under MIT license at https://github.com/microsoft/skala
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Density Functional Theory (DFT) underpins much of modern computational chemistry and materials science. Yet, the reliability of DFT-derived predictions of experimentally measurable properties remains fundamentally limited by the need to approximate the unknown exchange-correlation (XC) functional. The traditional paradigm for improving accuracy has relied on increasingly elaborate hand-crafted functional forms. This approach has led to a longstanding trade-off between computational efficiency and accuracy, which remains insufficient for reliable predictive modelling of laboratory experiments. Here we introduce Skala, a deep learning-based XC functional that surpasses state-of-the-art hybrid functionals in accuracy across the main-group chemistry benchmark set GMTKN55 with an error of 2.8 kcal/mol, while retaining the lower computational cost characteristic of semi-local DFT. This demonstrated departure from the historical trade-off between accuracy and efficiency is enabled by learning non-local representations of electronic structure directly from data, bypassing the need for increasingly costly hand-engineered features. Leveraging an unprecedented volume of high-accuracy reference data from wavefunction-based methods, we establish that modern deep learning enables systematically improvable neural exchange-correlation models as training datasets expand, positioning first-principles simulations to become progressively more predictive.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于深度学习的精确且可扩展的交换关联泛函
密度泛函理论（DFT）是现代计算化学和材料科学的基础。然而，DFT对实验可测量性质的预测可靠性，从根本上受限于必须近似未知的交换关联（XC）泛函。传统提升精度的方法依赖于日益复杂的手工设计泛函形式，导致长期存在的计算效率与精度之间的权衡，仍不足以支持对实验室实验的可靠预测。本文引入Skala，这是一种基于深度学习的XC泛函，在主族化学基准集GMTKN55上超越了最先进的混合泛函，误差仅为2.8 kcal/mol，同时保留了半局域DFT的较低计算成本。这种对历史精度与效率权衡的突破，得益于直接从数据中学习电子结构的非局域表示，无需日益昂贵的手工设计特征。借助前所未有的高精度波函数方法参考数据，我们证明现代深度学习可实现随训练数据集扩展而系统性改进的神经交换关联模型，使从头算模拟逐步具备更强的预测能力。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to improve the accuracy of Density Functional Theory without sacrificing computational efficiency, this work introduces Skala, a deep learning model that learns non-local electronic structure representations directly from high-fidelity reference data. Unlike traditional hand-crafted exchange-correlation functionals, Skala achieves superior accuracy—2.8 kcal/mol error on the GMTKN55 benchmark—while maintaining the low cost of semi-local DFT. This breakthrough demonstrates that deep learning can systematically improve XC models as training data grows, enabling first-principles simulations to become more predictive without the historical trade-off between accuracy and speed.
为提升密度泛函理论的预测精度同时保持计算效率，该研究提出Skala模型，通过直接从高精度参考数据中学习电子结构的非局域表示。与传统手工构建的交换关联泛函不同，Skala在GMTKN55基准测试中达到2.8 kcal/mol的误差，同时保留半局域DFT的计算成本。这一突破表明，随着训练数据的增加，深度学习可系统性提升交换关联泛函的预测能力，从而打破长期存在的精度与效率之间的权衡。
Tstars-Tryon 1.0: Robust and Realistic Virtual Try-On for Diverse Fashion Items
Authors: Mengting Chen, Zhengrui Chen, Yongchao Du, Zuan Gao, Taihang Hu, Jinsong Lan, Chao Lin, Yefeng Shen, Xingjian Wang, Zhao Wang, Zhengtao Wu, Xiaoli Xu, Zhengze Xu, Hao Yan, Mingzhou Zhang, Jun Zheng, Qinye Zhou, Xiaoyong Zhu, Bo Zheng
First: 2026-04-21T17:59:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:59:49+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, model evaluation report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in image generation and editing have opened new opportunities for virtual try-on. However, existing methods still struggle to meet complex real-world demands. We present Tstars-Tryon 1.0, a commercial-scale virtual try-on system that is robust, realistic, versatile, and highly efficient. First, our system maintains a high success rate across challenging cases like extreme poses, severe illumination variations, motion blur, and other in-the-wild conditions. Second, it delivers highly photorealistic results with fine-grained details, faithfully preserving garment texture, material properties, and structural characteristics, while largely avoiding common AI-generated artifacts. Third, beyond apparel try-on, our model supports flexible multi-image composition (up to 6 reference images) across 8 fashion categories, with coordinated control over person identity and background. Fourth, to overcome the latency bottlenecks of commercial deployment, our system is heavily optimized for inference speed, delivering near real-time generation for a seamless user experience. These capabilities are enabled by an integrated system design spanning end-to-end model architecture, a scalable data engine, robust infrastructure, and a multi-stage training paradigm. Extensive evaluation and large-scale product deployment demonstrate that Tstars-Tryon1.0 achieves leading overall performance. To support future research, we also release a comprehensive benchmark. The model has been deployed at an industrial scale on the Taobao App, serving millions of users with tens of millions of requests.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Tstars-Tryon 1.0：适用于多样时尚单品的稳健且逼真的虚拟试穿系统
图像生成与编辑技术的最新进展为虚拟试穿开辟了新机遇。然而，现有方法仍难以满足复杂的真实世界需求。我们提出了Tstars-Tryon 1.0，这是一个商业规模的虚拟试穿系统，具备稳健性、真实感、多功能性和高效性。首先，系统在极端姿态、严重光照变化、运动模糊等真实场景下仍保持高成功率。其次，生成结果高度逼真，细节丰富，忠实保留服装纹理、材质属性和结构特征，同时大幅避免常见AI生成伪影。第三，除服装试穿外，模型支持跨8个时尚品类的灵活多图合成（最多6张参考图），并可协调控制人物身份与背景。第四，为克服商业部署中的延迟瓶颈，系统经过深度优化，推理速度接近实时，提供流畅用户体验。这些能力得益于端到端模型架构、可扩展数据引擎、稳健基础设施及多阶段训练范式的集成设计。大量评估与大规模产品部署表明，Tstars-Tryon 1.0整体性能领先。为支持未来研究，我们同时发布全面基准测试。该模型已在淘宝App实现工业级部署，服务数百万用户，日均请求量达数千万。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable virtual try-on in real-world scenarios, Tstars-Tryon 1.0 introduces a commercial-scale system designed to handle extreme poses, lighting variations, and motion blur while producing photorealistic results with fine-grained texture and material fidelity. The method integrates an end-to-end model architecture, scalable data engine, and multi-stage training, enabling flexible multi-image composition across eight fashion categories with coordinated control over person and background. Experimental results show high success rates under challenging conditions, minimal AI artifacts, and near real-time inference speed, validated through large-scale deployment on Taobao serving millions of users.
为满足电商场景中对虚拟试穿系统鲁棒性和真实感的需求，Tstars-Tryon 1.0 提出了一套商业级解决方案，可有效应对极端姿态、运动模糊和复杂光照等挑战，生成细节丰富、材质与结构真实、无常见AI伪影的图像。系统通过端到端模型架构、可扩展数据引擎和多阶段训练范式实现高成功率与近实时推理。实验表明其在多品类服装试穿中表现领先，支持最多六张参考图并协调人物与背景控制，已在淘宝大规模部署，服务数百万用户。
AnyRecon: Arbitrary-View 3D Reconstruction with Video Diffusion Model
Authors: Yutian Chen, Shi Guo, Renbiao Jin, Tianshuo Yang, Xin Cai, Yawen Luo, Mingxin Yang, Mulin Yu, Linning Xu, Tianfan Xue
First: 2026-04-21T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:59:47+00:00
Comments: Webpage: https://yutian10.github.io/AnyRecon/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Sparse-view 3D reconstruction is essential for modeling scenes from casual captures, but remain challenging for non-generative reconstruction. Existing diffusion-based approaches mitigates this issues by synthesizing novel views, but they often condition on only one or two capture frames, which restricts geometric consistency and limits scalability to large or diverse scenes. We propose AnyRecon, a scalable framework for reconstruction from arbitrary and unordered sparse inputs that preserves explicit geometric control while supporting flexible conditioning cardinality. To support long-range conditioning, our method constructs a persistent global scene memory via a prepended capture view cache, and removes temporal compression to maintain frame-level correspondence under large viewpoint changes. Beyond better generative model, we also find that the interplay between generation and reconstruction is crucial for large-scale 3D scenes. Thus, we introduce a geometry-aware conditioning strategy that couples generation and reconstruction through an explicit 3D geometric memory and geometry-driven capture-view retrieval. To ensure efficiency, we combine 4-step diffusion distillation with context-window sparse attention to reduce quadratic complexity. Extensive experiments demonstrate robust and scalable reconstruction across irregular inputs, large viewpoint gaps, and long trajectories.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnyRecon：基于视频扩散模型的任意视角3D重建
稀疏视角3D重建对于从非专业拍摄中建模场景至关重要，但非生成式重建仍具挑战。现有扩散方法通过合成新视角缓解此问题，但常仅依赖一至两帧，限制几何一致性并难以扩展至大范围或多样场景。我们提出AnyRecon，一种可扩展框架，支持任意无序稀疏输入重建，同时保留显式几何控制并支持灵活的条件帧数。为支持长程条件，方法通过前置捕获视角缓存构建持久全局场景记忆，并移除时间压缩以在大视角变化下保持帧级对应。除更优生成模型外，我们发现生成与重建的协同对大规模3D场景至关重要，故引入几何感知条件策略，通过显式3D几何记忆与几何驱动的捕获视角检索耦合生成与重建。为提升效率，结合4步扩散蒸馏与上下文窗口稀疏注意力降低二次复杂度。大量实验验证其在不规则输入、大视角间隔及长轨迹下稳健且可扩展的重建能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reconstructing 3D scenes from sparse, unordered video inputs without generative artifacts, AnyRecon introduces a scalable diffusion-based framework that preserves geometric consistency across arbitrary viewpoints. It achieves this by maintaining a persistent global scene memory via a capture view cache and removing temporal compression to preserve frame-level correspondence under large viewpoint changes. The method further integrates a geometry-aware conditioning strategy that links generation and reconstruction through explicit 3D geometric memory and view retrieval, enhancing scalability. Experimental results show robust reconstruction across irregular inputs, large viewpoint gaps, and long trajectories, with efficiency gains from diffusion distillation and sparse attention.
为了解决从稀疏、无序的视频输入中重建3D场景且不产生生成伪影的难题，AnyRecon提出了一种可扩展的扩散框架，通过保持几何一致性实现任意视角的重建。该方法通过捕获视图缓存构建持久全局场景记忆，并避免时间压缩以保留帧级对应关系。同时，它引入几何感知的条件策略，利用3D几何记忆显式关联生成与重建过程，提升大规模场景的可扩展性和保真度。实验表明，该方法在不规则输入、大视角间隔和长轨迹场景下均能稳定重建，且通过扩散蒸馏与稀疏注意力实现效率优化。
CityRAG: Stepping Into a City via Spatially-Grounded Video Generation
Authors: Gene Chou, Charles Herrmann, Kyle Genova, Boyang Deng, Songyou Peng, Bharath Hariharan, Jason Y. Zhang, Noah Snavely, Philipp Henzler
First: 2026-04-21T17:59:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:59:03+00:00
Comments: Project page: cityrag.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We address the problem of generating a 3D-consistent, navigable environment that is spatially grounded: a simulation of a real location. Existing video generative models can produce a plausible sequence that is consistent with a text (T2V) or image (I2V) prompt. However, the capability to reconstruct the real world under arbitrary weather conditions and dynamic object configurations is essential for downstream applications including autonomous driving and robotics simulation. To this end, we present CityRAG, a video generative model that leverages large corpora of geo-registered data as context to ground generation to the physical scene, while maintaining learned priors for complex motion and appearance changes. CityRAG relies on temporally unaligned training data, which teaches the model to semantically disentangle the underlying scene from its transient attributes. Our experiments demonstrate that CityRAG can generate coherent minutes-long, physically grounded video sequences, maintain weather and lighting conditions over thousands of frames, achieve loop closure, and navigate complex trajectories to reconstruct real-world geography.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CityRAG：通过空间锚定的视频生成步入城市
我们解决生成3D一致、可导航且空间锚定（即真实地点模拟）环境的问题。现有视频生成模型可基于文本（T2V）或图像（I2V）提示生成合理序列，但要实现下游应用（如自动驾驶与机器人仿真），必须能在任意天气条件和动态物体配置下重建真实世界。为此，我们提出CityRAG，一种利用地理注册大数据作为上下文，将生成结果锚定到物理场景，同时保留对复杂运动与外观变化学习先验的视频生成模型。CityRAG依赖时间未对齐的训练数据，使模型能语义分离场景与瞬态属性。实验表明，CityRAG可生成连贯数分钟、物理锚定的视频序列，保持数千帧内的天气与光照条件，实现闭环导航，并沿复杂轨迹重建真实地理环境。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic, navigable 3D environments grounded in real-world locations for applications like autonomous driving and robotics, CityRAG introduces a video generation model that leverages geo-registered data to anchor outputs to physical scenes while preserving learned priors for complex motion and appearance changes. The model trains on temporally unaligned data to disentangle scene structure from transient attributes, enabling it to generate long, coherent, physically consistent video sequences under varying weather and lighting. Experimental results show CityRAG can maintain environmental conditions over thousands of frames, achieve loop closure, and navigate complex trajectories to accurately reconstruct real-world geography.
为满足自动驾驶等应用对真实、可导航3D环境的需求，CityRAG提出一种视频生成模型，利用地理注册数据作为场景锚定上下文，同时保留对复杂运动和外观变化的学习先验。该模型在时间上非对齐的数据上训练，以解耦场景结构与天气、光照等瞬态属性。实验表明，CityRAG可生成长达数分钟的连贯视频，保持数千帧内天气与光照一致，实现闭环导航，并能准确重建真实地理环境。
Generalization at the Edge of Stability
Authors: Mario Tuci, Caner Korkmaz, Umut Şimşekli, Tolga Birdal
First: 2026-04-21T17:59:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:59:02+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://circle-group.github.io/research/GATES
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Training modern neural networks often relies on large learning rates, operating at the edge of stability, where the optimization dynamics exhibit oscillatory and chaotic behavior. Empirically, this regime often yields improved generalization performance, yet the underlying mechanism remains poorly understood. In this work, we represent stochastic optimizers as random dynamical systems, which often converge to a fractal attractor set (rather than a point) with a smaller intrinsic dimension. Building on this connection and inspired by Lyapunov dimension theory, we introduce a novel notion of dimension, coined the `sharpness dimension', and prove a generalization bound based on this dimension. Our results show that generalization in the chaotic regime depends on the complete Hessian spectrum and the structure of its partial determinants, highlighting a complexity that cannot be captured by the trace or spectral norm considered in prior work. Experiments across various MLPs and transformers validate our theory while also providing new insights into the recently observed phenomenon of grokking.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稳定性边缘的泛化能力
训练现代神经网络通常依赖较大的学习率，在稳定性边缘运行，此时优化动态表现出振荡和混沌行为。实证表明，该区域往往能提升泛化性能，但其内在机制尚不明确。本文将随机优化器建模为随机动力系统，通常收敛至具有更小内在维度的分形吸引子集合（而非单点）。基于此联系并受李雅普诺夫维数理论启发，我们提出一种新维度概念——“尖锐度维数”，并据此推导出泛化界。结果表明，混沌区域中的泛化能力取决于Hessian谱的完整结构及其部分行列式的结构，揭示了此前研究中仅考虑迹或谱范数所无法捕捉的复杂性。在多种MLP和Transformer模型上的实验验证了我们的理论，并为近期观察到的“grokking”现象提供了新见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical observation that large learning rates, operating at the edge of stability, often improve generalization despite chaotic optimization dynamics, this work models stochastic optimizers as random dynamical systems converging to fractal attractors with reduced intrinsic dimension. It introduces a new measure, the sharpness dimension, grounded in Lyapunov dimension theory, and derives a generalization bound based on this dimension. Experiments across MLPs and transformers confirm that generalization in chaotic regimes depends on the full Hessian spectrum and partial determinants, not just trace or spectral norm, and offer new insights into the grokking phenomenon.
鉴于大步长训练常在稳定性边缘引发振荡混沌行为却能提升泛化性能，本文将随机优化器建模为收敛至分形吸引子的随机动力系统，并引入受李雅普诺夫维数理论启发的‘尖锐维数’概念，据此推导泛化界。实验表明，混沌区域的泛化性能取决于全Hessian谱与部分行列式结构，而非传统方法关注的迹或谱范数，同时为近期观察到的‘grokking’现象提供了新解释。
Phase Transitions in the Fluctuations of Functionals of Random Neural Networks
Authors: Simmaco Di Lillo, Leonardo Maini, Domenico Marinucci
First: 2026-04-21T17:58:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:58:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We establish central and non-central limit theorems for sequences of functionals of the Gaussian output of an infinitely-wide random neural network on the d-dimensional sphere . We show that the asymptotic behaviour of these functionals as the depth of the network increases depends crucially on the fixed points of the covariance function, resulting in three distinct limiting regimes: convergence to the same functional of a limiting Gaussian field, convergence to a Gaussian distribution, convergence to a distribution in the Qth Wiener chaos. Our proofs exploit tools that are now classical (Hermite expansions, Diagram Formula, Stein-Malliavin techniques), but also ideas which have never been used in similar contexts: in particular, the asymptotic behaviour is determined by the fixed-point structure of the iterative operator associated with the covariance, whose nature and stability governs the different limiting regimes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机神经网络函数泛函波动中的相变
我们建立了高斯输出的无限宽随机神经网络在d维球面上的函数泛函序列的中心与非中心极限定理。我们表明，随着网络深度增加，这些泛函的渐近行为取决于协方差函数的不动点，从而产生三种不同的极限情形：收敛于极限高斯场的同一泛函，收敛于高斯分布，收敛于Q阶Wiener混沌中的分布。我们的证明运用了经典工具（Hermite展开、图式公式、Stein-Malliavin技术），但也引入了此前在类似情境中未曾使用的新思想：特别是，渐近行为由与协方差相关的迭代算子的不动点结构决定，其性质与稳定性决定了不同的极限情形。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding the statistical behavior of functionals in deep random neural networks, this work derives central and non-central limit theorems for Gaussian outputs of infinitely-wide networks on the d-dimensional sphere. The method leverages classical tools like Hermite expansions and Stein-Malliavin techniques, but introduces novel insights by analyzing the fixed points of an iterative covariance operator. Experimental results reveal three distinct asymptotic regimes as network depth increases: convergence to a functional of a limiting Gaussian field, convergence to a Gaussian distribution, or convergence to a distribution in the Qth Wiener chaos, with the specific regime determined by the stability and nature of the covariance operator’s fixed points.
为理解深度随机神经网络中函数的统计行为，本文针对无限宽网络在d维球面上的高斯输出，推导了中心与非中心极限定理。方法结合了经典工具如Hermite展开与Stein-Malliavin技术，并引入了对协方差迭代算子固定点结构的新见解。实验结果表明，随着网络深度增加，函数呈现三种不同渐近行为：收敛于极限高斯场的函数、收敛于高斯分布，或收敛于Q阶Wiener混沌分布，具体行为由协方差算子固定点的性质与稳定性决定。
Safe Continual Reinforcement Learning in Non-stationary Environments
Authors: Austin Coursey, Abel Diaz-Gonzalez, Marcos Quinones-Grueiro, Gautam Biswas
First: 2026-04-21T17:58:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:58:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) offers a compelling data-driven paradigm for synthesizing controllers for complex systems when accurate physical models are unavailable; however, most existing control-oriented RL methods assume stationarity and, therefore, struggle in real-world non-stationary deployments where system dynamics and operating conditions can change unexpectedly. Moreover, RL controllers acting in physical environments must satisfy safety constraints throughout their learning and execution phases, rendering transient violations during adaptation unacceptable. Although continual RL and safe RL have each addressed non-stationarity and safety, respectively, their intersection remains comparatively unexplored, motivating the study of safe continual RL algorithms that can adapt over the system's lifetime while preserving safety. In this work, we systematically investigate safe continual reinforcement learning by introducing three benchmark environments that capture safety-critical continual adaptation and by evaluating representative approaches from safe RL, continual RL, and their combinations. Our empirical results reveal a fundamental tension between maintaining safety constraints and preventing catastrophic forgetting under non-stationary dynamics, with existing methods generally failing to achieve both objectives simultaneously. To address this shortcoming, we examine regularization-based strategies that partially mitigate this trade-off and characterize their benefits and limitations. Finally, we outline key open challenges and research directions toward developing safe, resilient learning-based controllers capable of sustained autonomous operation in changing environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非平稳环境中的安全持续强化学习
强化学习（RL）提供了一种有吸引力的数据驱动范式，用于在缺乏精确物理模型的情况下合成复杂系统的控制器；然而，大多数现有的面向控制的RL方法假设环境平稳性，因此在现实世界中系统动态和运行条件可能意外变化的非平稳部署中表现不佳。此外，RL控制器在物理环境中运行时，必须在其学习和执行阶段始终满足安全约束，因此适应过程中的临时违规是不可接受的。尽管持续RL和安全RL分别解决了非平稳性和安全性问题，但它们的交叉领域仍相对未被充分探索，这促使我们研究能够在系统生命周期内适应变化同时保持安全性的安全持续RL算法。在本研究中，我们通过引入三个基准环境系统地研究安全持续强化学习，这些环境捕捉了安全关键的持续适应问题，并评估了来自安全RL、持续RL及其组合的代表性方法。我们的实证结果揭示了在非平稳动态下维持安全约束与防止灾难性遗忘之间存在根本性张力，现有方法通常无法同时实现这两个目标。为解决这一缺陷，我们考察了基于正则化的策略，这些策略部分缓解了这一权衡，并分析了它们的优缺点。最后，我们概述了关键的开放挑战和研究方向，以期开发出能够在变化环境中持续自主运行的安全、鲁棒的学习型控制器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe and adaptive reinforcement learning in real-world non-stationary environments where system dynamics shift unpredictably, this study investigates safe continual RL by designing three benchmark environments that simulate safety-critical continual adaptation. The authors evaluate existing safe RL and continual RL methods, as well as their combinations, revealing a fundamental conflict between maintaining safety constraints and preventing catastrophic forgetting under changing dynamics. Their experiments show that current approaches typically fail to satisfy both objectives simultaneously, and while regularization-based strategies offer partial mitigation, they come with trade-offs. The paper concludes by identifying key open challenges for developing resilient, safety-preserving controllers for long-term autonomous operation.
为应对现实世界中系统动态非平稳变化且需持续安全运行的挑战，本研究设计了三个模拟安全关键型持续适应的基准环境，系统评估了安全强化学习、持续强化学习及其组合方法，发现现有方法普遍难以同时满足安全约束与防止灾难性遗忘。研究进一步考察了基于正则化的策略以缓解此矛盾，分析其部分有效性与局限性，并指出构建可长期自主运行的鲁棒学习控制器仍面临关键挑战。
Generative Drifting for Conditional Medical Image Generation
Authors: Zirong Li, Siyuan Mei, Weiwen Wu, Andreas Maier, Lina Gölz, Yan Xia
First: 2026-04-21T17:58:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:58:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conditional medical image generation plays an important role in many clinically relevant imaging tasks. However, existing methods still face a fundamental challenge in balancing inference efficiency, patient-specific fidelity, and distribution-level plausibility, particularly in high-dimensional 3D medical imaging. In this work, we propose GDM, a generative drifting framework that reformulates deterministic medical image prediction as a multi-objective learning problem to jointly promote distribution-level plausibility and patient-specific fidelity while retaining one-step inference. GDM extends drifting to 3D medical imaging through an attractive-repulsive drift that minimizes the discrepancy between the generator pushforward and the target distribution. To enable stable drifting-based learning in 3D volumetric data, GDM constructs a multi-level feature bank from a medical foundation encoder to support reliable affinity estimation and drifting field computation across complementary global, local, and spatial representations. In addition, a gradient coordination strategy in the shared output space improves optimization balance under competing distribution-level and fidelity-oriented objectives. We evaluate the proposed framework on two representative tasks, MRI-to-CT synthesis and sparse-view CT reconstruction. Experimental results show that GDM consistently outperforms a wide range of baselines, including GAN-based, flow-matching-based, and SDE-based generative models, as well as supervised regression methods, while improving the balance among anatomical fidelity, quantitative reliability, perceptual realism, and inference efficiency. These findings suggest that GDM provides a practical and effective framework for conditional 3D medical image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：条件医学图像生成的生成漂移方法
条件医学图像生成在许多临床相关的成像任务中发挥重要作用。然而，现有方法仍面临平衡推理效率、患者特异性保真度和分布级合理性这一根本挑战，尤其是在高维3D医学成像中。本文提出GDM，一种生成漂移框架，将确定性医学图像预测重新表述为多目标学习问题，以同时促进分布级合理性和患者特异性保真度，同时保持单步推理。GDM通过吸引-排斥漂移扩展至3D医学成像，最小化生成器推前分布与目标分布之间的差异。为在3D体数据中实现稳定的漂移学习，GDM从医学基础编码器构建多级特征库，支持在互补的全局、局部和空间表示上可靠地进行亲和度估计和漂移场计算。此外，共享输出空间中的梯度协调策略在竞争的分布级和保真度导向目标下改善优化平衡。我们在两个代表性任务——MRI到CT合成和稀疏视图CT重建上评估该框架。实验结果表明，GDM在解剖保真度、定量可靠性、感知真实性和推理效率之间取得更好平衡，持续优于包括基于GAN、流匹配和SDE的生成模型以及监督回归方法在内的广泛基线方法。这些发现表明，GDM为条件3D医学图像生成提供了一种实用且有效的框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance inference efficiency, patient-specific fidelity, and distribution-level plausibility in 3D medical imaging, this work introduces GDM, a generative drifting framework that reformulates deterministic image prediction as a multi-objective learning problem. GDM employs an attractive-repulsive drift mechanism to align the generator’s output with target distributions while preserving one-step inference, and leverages a multi-level feature bank from a medical foundation encoder to enable stable drifting across global, local, and spatial representations. A gradient coordination strategy further stabilizes optimization under competing objectives. Evaluated on MRI-to-CT synthesis and sparse-view CT reconstruction, GDM outperforms GANs, flow-matching, SDE-based models, and supervised regression methods, demonstrating superior anatomical fidelity, quantitative reliability, perceptual realism, and inference efficiency.
为解决3D医学图像生成中推理效率、患者特异性保真度与分布级合理性之间的平衡难题，本文提出GDM生成漂移框架，将确定性图像预测重构为多目标学习问题。GDM通过吸引-排斥漂移机制对齐生成器输出与目标分布，同时保持单步推理，并利用医学基础编码器构建多层次特征库，支持全局、局部与空间特征下的稳定漂移计算。梯度协调策略进一步平衡分布级与保真度导向目标的优化。在MRI到CT合成与稀疏视图CT重建任务中，GDM显著优于GAN、流匹配、SDE类生成模型及监督回归方法，在解剖保真度、定量可靠性、感知真实性和推理效率上均表现更优。
Tackling multiphysics problems via finite element-guided physics-informed operator learning
Authors: Yusuke Yamazaki, Reza Najian Asl, Markus Apel, Mayu Muramatsu, Shahed Rezaei
First: 2026-03-02T03:52:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:57:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work presents a finite element-guided physics-informed operator learning framework for multiphysics problems with coupled partial differential equations (PDEs) on arbitrary domains. The proposed framework learns an operator from the input space to the solution space with a weighted residual formulation based on the finite element method, enabling discretization-independent prediction beyond the training resolution without relying on labeled simulation data. The present framework for multiphysics problems is implemented in Folax, a JAX-based operator learning platform, and is verified on nonlinear coupled thermo-mechanical problems. Two- and three-dimensional representative volume elements with varying heterogeneous microstructures, and a close-to-reality industrial casting example under varying boundary conditions are investigated as the example problems. We investigate the potential of several neural operators combined with the proposed finite element-guided approach, including Fourier neural operators (FNOs), deep operator networks (DeepONets), and a newly proposed implicit finite operator learning (iFOL) approach based on conditional neural fields. The results demonstrate that FNOs yield highly accurate solution operators on regular domains, where the global features can be efficiently learned in the spectral domain, and iFOL offers efficient parametric operator learning capabilities for complex and irregular geometries. Furthermore, studies on training strategies, network decomposition, and training sample quality reveal that a monolithic training strategy using a single network is sufficient for accurate predictions, while training sample quality strongly influences performance. Overall, the present approach highlights the potential of physics-informed operator learning with a finite element-based loss as a unified and scalable approach for coupled multiphysics simulations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于有限元引导的物理信息算子学习求解多物理场问题
本文提出了一种有限元引导的物理信息算子学习框架，用于求解任意域上耦合偏微分方程（PDE）的多物理场问题。该框架基于有限元方法的加权残差形式，从输入空间学习到解空间的算子，可在训练分辨率之外实现离散化无关的预测，且无需依赖标注的仿真数据。该多物理场框架在基于JAX的算子学习平台Folax中实现，并在非线性热-力耦合问题上进行了验证。研究对象包括具有不同异质微观结构的二维和三维代表性体积单元，以及在不同边界条件下接近工业现实的铸造实例。本文探讨了多种神经算子与所提有限元引导方法的结合潜力，包括傅里叶神经算子（FNOs）、深度算子网络（DeepONets）和基于条件神经场的新提出的隐式有限算子学习（iFOL）方法。结果表明，FNOs在规则域上能高效学习全局特征并获得高精度解算子；iFOL则为复杂不规则几何提供了高效的参数化算子学习能力。此外，关于训练策略、网络分解及训练样本质量的研究表明，单网络的单体训练策略足以实现准确预测，而训练样本质量对性能影响显著。总体而言，该方法凸显了基于有限元损失的物理信息算子学习作为统一、可扩展的耦合多物理场模拟方法的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and accurate solutions to coupled multiphysics problems governed by partial differential equations on arbitrary domains, this work introduces a finite element-guided physics-informed operator learning framework that learns solution operators via a weighted residual formulation. Implemented in Folax, the framework enables discretization-independent predictions without labeled simulation data and is tested on nonlinear thermo-mechanical problems across 2D/3D heterogeneous microstructures and an industrial casting case. Experiments with Fourier neural operators, DeepONets, and a novel iFOL method show FNOs excel on regular domains due to spectral feature learning, while iFOL efficiently handles complex geometries. Training strategies and sample quality reveal monolithic networks suffice for accuracy, but data quality critically impacts performance.
为解决耦合多物理场问题的可扩展性与离散无关性需求，本文提出一种基于有限元引导的物理信息算子学习框架，通过加权残差公式从输入空间学习解算子。该方法在Folax平台实现，测试于非线性热-力耦合问题，涵盖二维/三维异质微结构域及工业铸造案例。实验表明，傅里叶神经算子在规则域表现优异（因频域全局特征学习），而隐式有限算子学习法更适合复杂几何。单网络整体训练已足够准确，训练样本质量对性能影响显著。
UniT: Toward a Unified Physical Language for Human-to-Humanoid Policy Learning and World Modeling
Authors: Boyu Chen, Yi Chen, Lu Qiu, Jerry Bai, Yuying Ge, Yixiao Ge
First: 2026-04-21T17:57:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:57:27+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://xpeng-robotics.github.io/unit/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Scaling humanoid foundation models is bottlenecked by the scarcity of robotic data. While massive egocentric human data offers a scalable alternative, bridging the cross-embodiment chasm remains a fundamental challenge due to kinematic mismatches. We introduce UniT (Unified Latent Action Tokenizer via Visual Anchoring), a framework that establishes a unified physical language for human-to-humanoid transfer. Grounded in the philosophy that heterogeneous kinematics share universal visual consequences, UniT employs a tri-branch cross-reconstruction mechanism: actions predict vision to anchor kinematics to physical outcomes, while vision reconstructs actions to filter out irrelevant visual confounders. Concurrently, a fusion branch synergies these purified modalities into a shared discrete latent space of embodiment-agnostic physical intents. We validate UniT across two paradigms: 1) Policy Learning (VLA-UniT): By predicting these unified tokens, it effectively leverages diverse human data to achieve state-of-the-art data efficiency and robust out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization on both humanoid simulation benchmark and real-world deployments, notably demonstrating zero-shot task transfer. 2) World Modeling (WM-UniT): By aligning cross-embodiment dynamics via unified tokens as conditions, it realizes direct human-to-humanoid action transfer. This alignment ensures that human data seamlessly translates into enhanced action controllability for humanoid video generation. Ultimately, by inducing a highly aligned cross-embodiment representation (empirically verified by t-SNE visualizations revealing the convergence of human and humanoid features into a shared manifold), UniT offers a scalable path to distill vast human knowledge into general-purpose humanoid capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniT：面向人类到人形机器人策略学习与世界建模的统一物理语言
人形机器人基础模型的扩展受限于机器人数据稀缺。虽然海量的自视角人类数据提供了可扩展的替代方案，但由于运动学差异，跨具身鸿沟的弥合仍是一个根本性挑战。我们提出了UniT（通过视觉锚定的统一潜在动作分词器），一种建立人类到人形机器人迁移统一物理语言的框架。该框架基于异构运动学共享通用视觉结果的哲学，采用三分支交叉重建机制：动作预测视觉以将运动学锚定到物理结果，视觉重建动作以过滤无关视觉干扰因素。同时，融合分支将这些净化后的模态协同映射到共享的离散潜在空间，该空间与具身无关，表征物理意图。我们在两个范式中验证了UniT：1）策略学习（VLA-UniT）：通过预测统一令牌，有效利用多样化人类数据，在人形机器人仿真基准和真实部署中实现最先进数据效率和鲁棒的分布外（OOD）泛化能力，尤其展示了零样本任务迁移能力。2）世界建模（WM-UniT）：通过统一令牌作为条件对跨具身动力学进行对齐，实现人类到人形机器人的直接动作迁移。该对齐确保人类数据无缝转化为人形机器人视频生成中的增强动作可控性。最终，通过诱导高度对齐的跨具身表征（经t-SNE可视化验证，人类与人形机器人特征收敛至共享流形），UniT提供了一条可扩展路径，将海量人类知识蒸馏为通用人形机器人能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of robotic data limiting humanoid foundation model scaling, this work proposes UniT, a unified physical language framework enabling human-to-humanoid transfer. It leverages the shared visual consequences of heterogeneous kinematics through a tri-branch cross-reconstruction mechanism: actions predict vision to anchor motion to physical outcomes, vision reconstructs actions to remove visual noise, and a fusion branch merges purified modalities into a shared discrete latent space of embodiment-agnostic physical intents. Experiments show UniT achieves state-of-the-art data efficiency and robust out-of-distribution generalization in policy learning (VLA-UniT), including zero-shot task transfer, and enables direct human-to-humanoid action transfer in world modeling (WM-UniT), improving humanoid video generation controllability. t-SNE visualizations confirm convergence of human and humanoid features into a shared manifold, validating cross-embodiment alignment.
为解决机器人数据稀缺制约人形机器人模型扩展的问题，本文提出UniT框架，通过视觉锚定建立统一的物理语言以实现人类到人形机器人的迁移。该框架采用三分支交叉重建机制：动作预测视觉以将运动学锚定到物理结果，视觉重建动作以过滤视觉干扰，融合分支将净化后的模态整合到共享的离散潜在空间中，表达与身体无关的物理意图。实验表明，UniT在策略学习（VLA-UniT）中实现最优数据效率与强分布外泛化能力，支持零样本任务迁移；在世界建模（WM-UniT）中实现人类动作直接迁移，提升人形机器人视频生成的可控性。t-SNE可视化显示人类与人形机器人特征收敛于共享流形，验证跨身体表征对齐。
MapPFN: Learning Causal Perturbation Maps in Context
Authors: Marvin Sextro, Weronika Kłos, Gabriel Dernbach
First: 2026-01-28T22:28:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:55:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Planning effective interventions in biological systems requires treatment-effect models that adapt to unseen biological contexts by identifying their specific underlying mechanisms. Yet single-cell perturbation datasets span only a handful of biological contexts, and existing methods cannot leverage new interventional evidence at inference time to adapt beyond their training data. To meta-learn a perturbation effect estimator, we present MapPFN, a prior-data fitted network (PFN) pre-trained on synthetic data generated from a prior over causal perturbations. Given a set of experiments, MapPFN uses in-context learning to predict post-perturbation distributions. Pre-trained on in silico gene knockouts alone, MapPFN identifies differentially expressed genes on par with models trained on real single-cell data. Fine-tuned, it consistently outperforms baselines across downstream datasets. Our code, model and data are available at https://marvinsxtr.github.io/MapPFN.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MapPFN：在上下文中学习因果扰动图
在生物系统中规划有效干预需要能够适应未见生物情境的治疗效应模型，通过识别其特定的潜在机制。然而，单细胞扰动数据集仅涵盖少数生物情境，现有方法无法在推理时利用新的干预证据以超越其训练数据。为元学习扰动效应估计器，我们提出了MapPFN，一种基于先验数据拟合的网络（PFN），在由因果扰动先验生成的合成数据上进行预训练。给定一组实验，MapPFN利用上下文学习预测扰动后的分布。仅在体外基因敲除数据上预训练后，MapPFN在差异表达基因识别方面表现与基于真实单细胞数据训练的模型相当。微调后，它在下游数据集上持续优于基线模型。我们的代码、模型和数据可在 https://marvinsxtr.github.io/MapPFN 获取。
Summary / 总结
To enable adaptive intervention planning in biological systems, MapPFN meta-learns a causal perturbation effect estimator by pre-training a prior-data fitted network on synthetic gene knockout data generated from a causal prior. Given new experimental contexts, it leverages in-context learning to predict post-perturbation gene expression distributions. Evaluated on real single-cell datasets, MapPFN matches performance of models trained on real data and consistently outperforms baselines after fine-tuning, demonstrating its ability to generalize beyond training contexts.
为解决现有方法无法在推理时利用新干预数据泛化到未见生物学情境的问题，MapPFN采用基于先验数据拟合的网络，预先在合成因果扰动数据上训练，以学习可泛化的扰动效应估计器。推理时通过上下文学习预测扰动后的基因表达分布。在真实单细胞数据集上评估时，MapPFN表现与基于真实数据训练的模型相当，并在微调后持续优于基线方法。
Time-Scale Coupling Between States and Parameters in Recurrent Neural Networks
Authors: Lorenzo Livi
First: 2025-08-16T18:19:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:54:21+00:00
Comments: final version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We show that gating mechanisms in recurrent neural networks (RNNs) induce lag-dependent and direction-dependent effective learning rates, even when training uses a fixed, global step size. This behavior arises from a coupling between state-space time-scales (parametrized by the gates) and parameter-space dynamics during gradient descent. By deriving exact Jacobians for leaky-integrator and gated RNNs and applying a first-order expansion, we make explicit how constant, scalar, and multi-dimensional gates reshape gradient propagation, modulate effective step sizes, and introduce anisotropy in parameter updates. These findings reveal that gates act not only as filters of information flow, but also as data-driven preconditioners of optimization, with formal connections to learning-rate schedules, momentum, and adaptive methods such as Adam. Empirical simulations corroborate these predictions: across several sequence tasks, gates produce lag-dependent effective learning rates and concentrate gradient flow into low-dimensional subspaces, matching or exceeding the anisotropic structure induced by Adam. Notably, gating and optimizer-driven adaptivity shape complementary aspects of credit assignment: gates align state-space transport with loss-relevant directions, while optimizers rescale parameter-space updates. Overall, this work provides a unified dynamical systems perspective on how gating couples state evolution with parameter updates, clarifying why gated architectures achieve robust trainability in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：循环神经网络中状态与参数的时间尺度耦合
我们表明，循环神经网络（RNN）中的门控机制即使在使用固定全局步长训练时，也会诱导依赖滞后和方向的有效学习率。这种行为源于状态空间时间尺度（由门控参数化）与参数空间梯度下降动力学之间的耦合。通过推导漏积分器和门控RNN的精确雅可比矩阵并应用一阶展开，我们明确说明了常数、标量和多维门控如何重塑梯度传播、调节有效步长，并在参数更新中引入各向异性。这些发现揭示，门控机制不仅作为信息流的过滤器，也作为数据驱动的优化预条件子，与学习率调度、动量和自适应方法（如Adam）存在形式上的联系。实证模拟验证了这些预测：在多个序列任务中，门控机制产生依赖滞后的有效学习率，并将梯度流集中到低维子空间，其各向异性结构与Adam诱导的结构相当或更优。值得注意的是，门控与优化器驱动的自适应性在责任分配方面互补：门控使状态空间传输与损失相关方向对齐，而优化器则缩放参数空间更新。总体而言，本工作从动力系统角度统一阐释了门控如何耦合状态演化与参数更新，阐明了为何门控架构在实践中具备鲁棒可训练性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how gating mechanisms in recurrent neural networks induce lag-dependent and direction-dependent effective learning rates despite fixed global step sizes, revealing a coupling between state-space and parameter-space dynamics during gradient descent. By deriving exact Jacobians for leaky-integrator and gated RNNs and applying first-order expansions, the authors demonstrate how scalar and multi-dimensional gates reshape gradient propagation, modulate effective step sizes, and introduce anisotropy in parameter updates. Empirical results across sequence tasks confirm that gates generate lag-dependent learning rates and concentrate gradients into low-dimensional subspaces, often matching or surpassing the anisotropy induced by Adam. The findings show that gates function as data-driven preconditioners, aligning state-space transport with loss-relevant directions, while optimizers handle parameter-space rescaling, together enabling robust trainability.
该研究探讨了循环神经网络中的门控机制如何在固定全局学习率下诱导滞后依赖和方向依赖的有效学习率，揭示了状态空间与参数空间动力学在梯度下降过程中的耦合关系。通过推导漏积分器和门控RNN的精确雅可比矩阵并应用一阶展开，作者展示了标量和多维门控如何重塑梯度传播、调节步长并引入参数更新的各向异性。在多个序列任务的实证模拟中，门控机制生成滞后依赖的学习率并使梯度流集中于低维子空间，常与Adam诱导的各向异性结构相当或更优。研究发现门控机制作为数据驱动的预条件器，使状态空间传输与损失相关方向对齐，而优化器负责参数空间的缩放，二者协同实现鲁棒训练能力。
Local Updates in Distributed Optimization: Provable Acceleration and Topology Effects
Authors: Zuang Wang, Yongqiang Wang
First: 2026-01-06T22:10:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:52:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inspired by the success of performing multiple local optimization steps between communication rounds in federated learning, incorporating such local updates into distributed optimization has recently attracted growing interest. However, unlike federated learning, where local updates can accelerate training by reducing gradient estimation error under minibatch settings, it remains unclear whether similar benefits persist when exact gradients are available. Moreover, existing theoretical results typically require reducing the step size when multiple local updates are employed, which can entirely offset any potential benefit of these additional local updates. In this paper, we focus on the classic DIGing algorithm and leverage the tight performance bounds provided by Performance Estimation Problems (PEP) to show that incorporating local updates can indeed accelerate distributed optimization. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first rigorous demonstration of such acceleration for a broad class of objective functions. Our analysis further reveals that, under an appropriate step size, performing only two local updates is sufficient to achieve the maximal possible improvement, and that additional local updates provide no further gains. Because more updates increase computational cost, these findings offer practical guidance for efficient implementation. We also show that these speed gains depend critically on the network structure, with sparser or less connected graphs, characterized by the spectral properties of the mixing matrix, yielding smaller improvements. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets corroborate the theoretical findings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分布式优化中的局部更新：可证明的加速与拓扑效应
受联邦学习中在通信轮次间执行多次局部优化步骤取得成功启发，将此类局部更新引入分布式优化近年来备受关注。然而，与联邦学习不同的是，在小批量设置下，局部更新可通过降低梯度估计误差加速训练，但在精确梯度可用的情况下，其类似优势是否依然存在尚不明确。此外，现有理论结果通常要求在使用多次局部更新时减小步长，这可能完全抵消额外局部更新的潜在收益。本文聚焦经典DIGing算法，利用性能估计问题（PEP）提供的紧致性能界，证明引入局部更新确实可加速分布式优化。据我们所知，这是针对广泛目标函数类首次严谨证明此类加速效果。我们的分析进一步表明，在适当步长下，仅执行两次局部更新即可实现最大可能改进，而额外更新无进一步收益。由于更多更新会增加计算成本，这些发现为高效实现提供实用指导。我们还表明，这些加速效果高度依赖网络结构，稀疏或连接性较差的图（由混合矩阵的谱性质表征）带来的改进较小。在合成与真实数据集上的大量实验验证了理论发现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observed acceleration in federated learning through local updates, this paper investigates whether similar speedups occur in distributed optimization when exact gradients are available. Using the DIGing algorithm and Performance Estimation Problems, the authors theoretically prove that local updates can accelerate convergence, and crucially, that only two local updates are sufficient to achieve maximal improvement, with additional updates offering no benefit. Their analysis further shows that acceleration depends on network topology, with sparser graphs yielding smaller gains. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets confirm these theoretical findings, validating the practical efficiency of limiting local updates to two steps.
受联邦学习中局部更新加速训练的启发，本文研究在精确梯度可用的情况下分布式优化是否也能通过局部更新加速收敛。基于DIGing算法和性能估计问题，作者严格证明局部更新确实能加速收敛，且仅需两次局部更新即可达到最大加速效果，更多更新无额外收益。理论分析表明加速效果高度依赖网络拓扑结构，稀疏图带来的加速较小。在合成与真实数据集上的实验验证了理论结论，表明局部更新可提升效率但受通信结构影响显著。
FASTER: Value-Guided Sampling for Fast RL
Authors: Perry Dong, Alexander Swerdlow, Dorsa Sadigh, Chelsea Finn
First: 2026-04-21T17:52:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:52:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Some of the most performant reinforcement learning algorithms today can be prohibitively expensive as they use test-time scaling methods such as sampling multiple action candidates and selecting the best one. In this work, we propose FASTER, a method for getting the benefits of sampling-based test-time scaling of diffusion-based policies without the computational cost by tracing the performance gain of action samples back to earlier in the denoising process. Our key insight is that we can model the denoising of multiple action candidates and selecting the best one as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) where the goal is to progressively filter action candidates before denoising is complete. With this MDP, we can learn a policy and value function in the denoising space that predicts the downstream value of action candidates in the denoising process and filters them while maximizing returns. The result is a method that is lightweight and can be plugged into existing generative RL algorithms. Across challenging long-horizon manipulation tasks in online and batch-online RL, FASTER consistently improves the underlying policies and achieves the best overall performance among the compared methods. Applied to a pretrained VLA, FASTER achieves the same performance while substantially reducing training and inference compute requirements. Code is available at https://github.com/alexanderswerdlow/faster .
中文标题/摘要
标题：FASTER：基于价值引导的采样加速强化学习
当前一些表现最出色的强化学习算法因采用测试时扩展方法（如采样多个动作候选并选择最优者）而代价高昂。本文提出FASTER方法，通过追溯动作采样性能增益至去噪过程早期，实现无需高昂计算成本即可获得扩散策略采样测试时扩展的优势。核心思想是将多个动作候选的去噪与选择最优者建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），目标是在去噪完成前逐步过滤动作候选。在此MDP框架下，我们学习去噪空间中的策略与价值函数，预测去噪过程中动作候选的下游价值并加以过滤以最大化回报。该方法轻量且可嵌入现有生成式强化学习算法。在在线与批处理在线强化学习中挑战性的长时序操作任务上，FASTER持续提升底层策略并优于对比方法。应用于预训练VLA时，FASTER实现相同性能的同时大幅降低训练与推理计算需求。代码见https://github.com/alexanderswerdlow/faster。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of sampling-based test-time scaling in reinforcement learning, FASTER introduces a value-guided sampling method that traces performance gains back through the denoising process of diffusion policies. It models the selection of optimal action candidates as a Markov Decision Process, learning a policy and value function in the denoising space to filter candidates early while maximizing expected returns. Experiments show FASTER consistently improves underlying policies across long-horizon manipulation tasks and achieves top performance among compared methods, while reducing training and inference compute requirements when applied to a pretrained VLA.
为降低强化学习中采样式测试时扩展的计算开销，FASTER提出一种基于价值引导的采样方法，将动作候选的性能增益追溯至去噪过程早期。它将动作候选筛选建模为去噪空间中的马尔可夫决策过程，学习策略与价值函数以在去噪完成前动态过滤候选动作并最大化回报。实验表明，FASTER在长时程操作任务中持续提升基础策略表现，优于对比方法。应用于预训练VLA时，其性能相当，但显著降低训练与推理计算需求。
FB-NLL: A Feature-Based Approach to Tackle Noisy Labels in Personalized Federated Learning
Authors: Abdulmoneam Ali, Ahmed Arafa
First: 2026-04-21T17:51:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:51:58+00:00
Comments: Submitted for journal publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) aims to learn multiple task-specific models rather than a single global model across heterogeneous data distributions. Existing PFL approaches typically rely on iterative optimization-such as model update trajectories-to cluster users that need to accomplish the same tasks together. However, these learning-dynamics-based methods are inherently vulnerable to low-quality data and noisy labels, as corrupted updates distort clustering decisions and degrade personalization performance. To tackle this, we propose FB-NLL, a feature-centric framework that decouples user clustering from iterative training dynamics. By exploiting the intrinsic heterogeneity of local feature spaces, FB-NLL characterizes each user through the spectral structure of the covariances of their feature representations and leverages subspace similarity to identify task-consistent user groupings. This geometry-aware clustering is label-agnostic and is performed in a one-shot manner prior to training, significantly reducing communication overhead and computational costs compared to iterative baselines. Complementing this, we introduce a feature-consistency-based detection and correction strategy to address noisy labels within clusters. By leveraging directional alignment in the learned feature space and assigning labels based on class-specific feature subspaces, our method mitigates corrupted supervision without requiring estimation of stochastic noise transition matrices. In addition, FB-NLL is model-independent and integrates seamlessly with existing noise-robust training techniques. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and noise regimes demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of average accuracy and performance stability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FB-NLL：一种基于特征的处理个性化联邦学习中噪声标签的方法
个性化联邦学习（PFL）旨在跨异构数据分布学习多个任务特定模型，而非单一全局模型。现有PFL方法通常依赖迭代优化（如模型更新轨迹）来聚类需共同完成相同任务的用户。然而，这些基于学习动态的方法固有地易受低质量数据和噪声标签影响，因被污染的更新会扭曲聚类决策并降低个性化性能。为此，我们提出FB-NLL，一种以特征为中心的框架，将用户聚类与迭代训练动态解耦。通过利用本地特征空间的内在异质性，FB-NLL通过用户特征表示协方差的谱结构刻画每个用户，并利用子空间相似性识别任务一致的用户群组。该几何感知聚类方法不依赖标签，且在训练前一次性完成，显著降低了通信开销和计算成本，相较迭代基线方法更优。此外，我们引入基于特征一致性的噪声标签检测与修正策略。通过利用学习特征空间中的方向对齐，并基于类特定特征子空间分配标签，该方法在无需估计随机噪声转移矩阵的情况下缓解了被污染的监督问题。同时，FB-NLL模型无关，可无缝集成现有抗噪训练技术。在多种数据集和噪声环境下广泛实验表明，我们的框架在平均准确率和性能稳定性方面持续优于现有最先进基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of existing personalized federated learning methods to noisy labels and corrupted model updates, FB-NLL introduces a feature-based clustering approach that decouples user grouping from iterative training dynamics. It characterizes users via the spectral structure of their local feature covariance matrices and clusters them based on subspace similarity, enabling a one-shot, label-agnostic grouping that reduces communication and computation. To handle noisy labels within clusters, FB-NLL employs a feature-consistency strategy that aligns labels with class-specific subspaces without requiring noise transition matrix estimation. Experimental results across multiple datasets and noise levels show FB-NLL consistently achieves higher average accuracy and greater performance stability than prior methods.
为解决现有个性化联邦学习方法对噪声标签和模型更新污染的敏感性，FB-NLL提出一种基于特征的用户聚类方法，将用户分组与迭代训练解耦。它通过分析本地特征空间的协方差谱结构，实现无需标签的单次聚类，显著降低通信与计算开销。针对聚类内噪声标签，该方法利用特征空间中的方向一致性，依据类特定子空间分配标签，无需估计噪声转移矩阵。在多个数据集和噪声环境下，实验表明FB-NLL在平均准确率和性能稳定性上均优于现有最先进方法。
VLA Foundry: A Unified Framework for Training Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Jean Mercat, Sedrick Keh, Kushal Arora, Isabella Huang, Paarth Shah, Haruki Nishimura, Shun Iwase, Katherine Liu
First: 2026-04-21T17:51:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:51:51+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 16 figures, technical report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
We present VLA Foundry, an open-source framework that unifies LLM, VLM, and VLA training in a single codebase. Most open-source VLA efforts specialize on the action training stage, often stitching together incompatible pretraining pipelines. VLA Foundry instead provides a shared training stack with end-to-end control, from language pretraining to action-expert fine-tuning. VLA Foundry supports both from-scratch training and pretrained backbones from Hugging Face. To demonstrate the utility of our framework, we train and release two types of models: the first trained fully from scratch through our LLM-->VLM-->VLA pipeline and the second built on the pretrained Qwen3-VL backbone. We evaluate closed-loop policy performance of both models on LBM Eval, an open-data, open-source simulator. We also contribute usability improvements to the simulator and the STEP analysis tools for easier public use. In the nominal evaluation setting, our fully-open from-scratch model is on par with our prior closed-source work and substituting in the Qwen3-VL backbone leads to a strong multi-task table top manipulation policy outperforming our baseline by a wide margin. The VLA Foundry codebase is available at https://github.com/TRI-ML/vla_foundry and all multi-task model weights are released on https://huggingface.co/collections/TRI-ML/vla-foundry. Additional qualitative videos are available on the project website https://tri-ml.github.io/vla_foundry.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VLA Foundry：用于训练视觉-语言-动作模型的统一框架
我们提出了VLA Foundry，一个开源框架，将大语言模型（LLM）、视觉语言模型（VLM）和视觉语言动作模型（VLA）的训练统一在一个代码库中。大多数开源VLA项目专注于动作训练阶段，常将不兼容的预训练流程拼接在一起。VLA Foundry则提供了一个共享的训练栈，实现从语言预训练到动作专家微调的端到端控制。该框架支持从零开始训练和使用Hugging Face的预训练骨干网络。为展示框架的实用性，我们训练并发布了两种模型：第一种通过我们的LLM→VLM→VLA流程完全从零训练；第二种基于预训练的Qwen3-VL骨干网络构建。我们在LBM Eval（一个开源数据集和开源模拟器）上评估了两种模型的闭环策略性能，同时为模拟器和STEP分析工具贡献了易用性改进，便于公众使用。在标准评估设置下，我们的完全开源从零训练模型与我们之前的闭源工作表现相当；替换为Qwen3-VL骨干网络后，其多任务桌面操作策略显著优于基线模型。VLA Foundry代码库位于https://github.com/TRI-ML/vla_foundry，所有多任务模型权重发布于https://huggingface.co/collections/TRI-ML/vla-foundry。更多定性视频可在项目网站https://tri-ml.github.io/vla_foundry获取。

It supports both from-scratch training and integration with pretrained Hugging Face backbones, enabling flexible model development. Experimental results show that a fully from-scratch model achieves performance comparable to prior closed-source work, while a variant built on the Qwen3-VL backbone significantly outperforms the baseline on multi-task tabletop manipulation in the LBM Eval simulator, demonstrating the framework’s effectiveness and scalability.
为解决开源VLA训练流程碎片化的问题，VLA Foundry构建了一个统一框架，将语言、视觉与动作模型训练整合为端到端流程，支持从零训练及与Hugging Face预训练模型结合。实验表明，完全从零训练的模型性能可媲美此前闭源工作，而基于Qwen3-VL骨干模型的版本在LBM Eval模拟器的多任务桌面操作任务中大幅超越基线，验证了框架的有效性和灵活性。
Benign Overfitting in Adversarial Training for Vision Transformers
Authors: Jiaming Zhang, Meng Ding, Shaopeng Fu, Jingfeng Zhang, Di Wang
First: 2026-04-21T17:48:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:48:51+00:00
Comments: arXiv admin note: text overlap with arXiv:2409.19345 by other authors
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the remarkable success of Vision Transformers (ViTs) across a wide range of vision tasks, recent studies have revealed that they remain vulnerable to adversarial examples, much like Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). A common empirical defense strategy is adversarial training, yet the theoretical underpinnings of its robustness in ViTs remain largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first theoretical analysis of adversarial training under simplified ViT architectures. We show that, when trained under a signal-to-noise ratio that satisfies a certain condition and within a moderate perturbation budget, adversarial training enables ViTs to achieve nearly zero robust training loss and robust generalization error under certain regimes. Remarkably, this leads to strong generalization even in the presence of overfitting, a phenomenon known as \emph{benign overfitting}, previously only observed in CNNs (with adversarial training). Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets further validate our theoretical findings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉变换器对抗训练中的良性过拟合
尽管视觉变换器（ViTs）在多种视觉任务中取得了显著成功，但近期研究表明，它们仍易受对抗样本影响，与卷积神经网络（CNNs）类似。一种常见的经验性防御策略是对抗训练，但其在ViTs中的理论鲁棒性基础尚不明确。本文首次对简化ViT架构下的对抗训练进行了理论分析。我们证明，在满足特定信噪比条件且扰动预算适中的情况下，对抗训练能使ViTs在某些条件下实现接近零的鲁棒训练损失和鲁棒泛化误差。值得注意的是，这甚至在存在过拟合的情况下仍能实现强泛化能力，即所谓的“良性过拟合”，此前仅在CNN（对抗训练）中观察到。在合成和真实数据集上的实验进一步验证了我们的理论发现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of Vision Transformers to adversarial examples despite their success in vision tasks, this work investigates adversarial training’s theoretical robustness in ViTs. Using simplified ViT architectures, the authors demonstrate that under a specific signal-to-noise ratio and within a moderate perturbation budget, adversarial training can drive robust training loss and generalization error near zero, even when models overfit the training data—a phenomenon termed benign overfitting, previously seen only in CNNs. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets confirm this theoretical behavior, showing strong robust generalization despite overfitting.
鉴于视觉Transformer（ViT）在视觉任务中表现优异但仍易受对抗攻击影响，本研究探讨了对抗训练在ViT中的理论鲁棒性。通过简化ViT架构，作者证明在特定信噪比和适度扰动预算下，对抗训练可使鲁棒训练损失与泛化误差接近零，即使存在过拟合——这一现象被称为良性过拟合，此前仅在CNN中观察到。在合成与真实数据集上的实验验证了理论预测，表明对抗训练可在不牺牲精度的前提下实现强鲁棒泛化。
Adaptive MSD-Splitting: Enhancing C4.5 and Random Forests for Skewed Continuous Attributes
Authors: Jake Lee
First: 2026-04-21T17:48:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:48:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The discretization of continuous numerical attributes remains a persistent computational bottleneck in the induction of decision trees, particularly as dataset dimensions scale. Building upon the recently proposed MSD-Splitting technique -- which bins continuous data using the empirical mean and standard deviation to dramatically improve the efficiency and accuracy of the C4.5 algorithm -- we introduce Adaptive MSD-Splitting (AMSD). While standard MSD-Splitting is highly effective for approximately symmetric distributions, its rigid adherence to fixed one-standard-deviation cutoffs can lead to catastrophic information loss in highly skewed data, a common artifact in real-world biomedical and financial datasets. AMSD addresses this by dynamically adjusting the standard deviation multiplier based on feature skewness, narrowing intervals in dense regions to preserve discriminative resolution. Furthermore, we integrate AMSD into ensemble methods, specifically presenting the Random Forest-AMSD (RF-AMSD) framework. Empirical evaluations on the Census Income, Heart Disease, Breast Cancer, and Forest Covertype datasets demonstrate that AMSD yields a 2-4% accuracy improvement over standard MSD-Splitting, while maintaining near-identical O(N) time complexity reductions compared to the O(N log N) exhaustive search. Our Random Forest extension achieves state-of-the-art accuracy at a fraction of standard computational costs, confirming the viability of adaptive statistical binning in large-scale ensemble learning architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应MSD分割：提升C4.5与随机森林对偏斜连续属性的处理能力
连续数值属性的离散化仍是决策树归纳中的持续性计算瓶颈，尤其在数据维度扩展时。基于近期提出的MSD分割技术——该技术利用经验均值和标准差对连续数据进行分箱，显著提升C4.5算法的效率与准确性——我们引入自适应MSD分割（AMSD）。虽然标准MSD分割对近似对称分布效果显著，但其固定使用一倍标准差截断的刚性策略，在高度偏斜数据（如现实世界生物医学与金融数据中常见）中会导致灾难性信息损失。AMSD通过根据特征偏斜动态调整标准差乘数，缩小密集区域区间以保留判别分辨率。此外，我们将AMSD集成至集成方法中，提出随机森林-AMSD（RF-AMSD）框架。在人口普查收入、心脏病、乳腺癌与森林覆盖类型数据集上的实证评估表明，AMSD相较标准MSD分割准确率提升2-4%，同时保持与O(N)时间复杂度相比O(N log N)穷举搜索近似相同的加速效果。我们的随机森林扩展在显著降低计算成本的同时达到当前最优准确率，证实了自适应统计分箱在大规模集成学习架构中的可行性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of handling skewed continuous attributes in decision tree induction, this work introduces Adaptive MSD-Splitting (AMSD), which dynamically adjusts binning thresholds based on feature skewness to avoid information loss in non-symmetric distributions. Building on the MSD-Splitting technique that uses mean and standard deviation for binning, AMSD modifies the standard deviation multiplier to narrow bins in dense regions, preserving discriminative power. When integrated into Random Forests as RF-AMSD, the method achieves 2-4% higher accuracy than standard MSD-Splitting across multiple real-world datasets while maintaining the same O(N) time complexity advantage over O(N log N) exhaustive search methods.
为了解决连续属性在决策树构建中因数据偏斜导致的信息损失和计算瓶颈，本文提出自适应MSD分割（AMSD），该方法根据特征偏斜动态调整分箱阈值，避免固定标准差截断造成的精度下降。AMSD将标准差乘数自适应调整，保留密集区域的判别能力。将其集成到随机森林中形成RF-AMSD框架，在多个基准数据集上比标准MSD分割提升2–4%准确率，同时保持与穷举搜索相当的计算效率优势。
ReImagine: Rethinking Controllable High-Quality Human Video Generation via Image-First Synthesis
Authors: Zhengwentai Sun, Keru Zheng, Chenghong Li, Hongjie Liao, Xihe Yang, Heyuan Li, Yihao Zhi, Shuliang Ning, Shuguang Cui, Xiaoguang Han
First: 2026-04-21T17:47:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:47:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Human video generation remains challenging due to the difficulty of jointly modeling human appearance, motion, and camera viewpoint under limited multi-view data. Existing methods often address these factors separately, resulting in limited controllability or reduced visual quality. We revisit this problem from an image-first perspective, where high-quality human appearance is learned via image generation and used as a prior for video synthesis, decoupling appearance modeling from temporal consistency. We propose a pose- and viewpoint-controllable pipeline that combines a pretrained image backbone with SMPL-X-based motion guidance, together with a training-free temporal refinement stage based on a pretrained video diffusion model. Our method produces high-quality, temporally consistent videos under diverse poses and viewpoints. We also release a canonical human dataset and an auxiliary model for compositional human image synthesis. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/Taited/ReImagine.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReImagine：通过图像优先合成重新思考可控高质量人体视频生成
由于在有限的多视角数据下难以同时建模人体外观、运动和摄像机视角，人体视频生成仍具挑战性。现有方法常将这些因素分别处理，导致可控性有限或视觉质量下降。我们从图像优先视角重新审视该问题，通过图像生成学习高质量人体外观，并将其作为视频合成的先验，从而将外观建模与时间一致性解耦。我们提出一种姿态和视角可控的流程，结合预训练图像骨干网络与基于SMPL-X的运动引导，以及基于预训练视频扩散模型的无训练时间精炼阶段。该方法可在多样姿态和视角下生成高质量、时间一致的视频。我们同时发布了一个标准人体数据集和用于组合式人体图像合成的辅助模型。代码和数据可在https://github.com/Taited/ReImagine获取。

Motivated by the challenge of jointly modeling human appearance, motion, and viewpoint in video generation under limited data, this work proposes an image-first approach that decouples appearance modeling from temporal consistency. It leverages a pretrained image backbone to generate high-quality human images, which serve as priors for video synthesis, and combines SMPL-X motion guidance with a training-free temporal refinement stage based on a pretrained video diffusion model. Experimental results demonstrate that the method produces temporally consistent, high-fidelity videos across diverse poses and viewpoints, outperforming prior methods in both controllability and visual quality.

Discovering a Shared Logical Subspace: Steering LLM Logical Reasoning via Alignment of Natural-Language and Symbolic Views
Authors: Feihao Fang, My T. Thai, Yuanyuan Lei
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-21T17:42:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:42:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) still struggle with multi-step logical reasoning. Existing approaches either purely refine the reasoning chain in natural language form or attach a symbolic solver as an external module. In this work, we instead ask whether LLMs contain a shared internal logical subspace that simultaneously aligns natural-language and symbolic-language views of the reasoning process. Our hypothesis is that this logical subspace captures logical reasoning capabilities in LLMs that are shared across views while remaining independent of surface forms. To verify this, we employ Canonical Correlation Analysis on the paired residual activations from natural-language and symbolic-language reasoning chains, learning a low-dimensional subspace with maximum cross-view correlation. Furthermore, we design a training-free approach that steers LLMs reasoning chain along this logical subspace, thereby leveraging the complementary reasoning signals from both views. Experiments on four logical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, improving accuracy by up to 11 percentage points and generalizing well on out-of-domain problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：发现共享的逻辑子空间：通过自然语言与符号语言视角的对齐引导大语言模型逻辑推理
大语言模型（LLMs）在多步逻辑推理方面仍存在困难。现有方法要么仅在自然语言形式中优化推理链，要么将符号求解器作为外部模块附加。本文提出，LLMs是否包含一个同时对齐自然语言与符号语言推理视角的共享内部逻辑子空间。我们的假设是，该逻辑子空间捕捉了LLMs中跨视角共享的逻辑推理能力，且独立于表面形式。为验证该假设，我们对自然语言与符号语言推理链的配对残差激活进行典型相关分析，学习一个具有最大跨视角相关性的低维子空间。此外，我们设计了一种无需训练的方法，引导LLMs推理链沿该逻辑子空间进行，从而利用两种视角的互补推理信号。在四个逻辑推理基准上的实验表明，该方法有效，准确率提升最高达11个百分点，并在域外问题上具有良好泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of LLMs in multi-step logical reasoning, this work investigates whether LLMs possess a shared internal logical subspace that aligns natural-language and symbolic reasoning views. The method employs Canonical Correlation Analysis on paired residual activations from both reasoning forms to learn a low-dimensional subspace maximizing cross-view correlation. A training-free steering mechanism then guides LLMs to reason within this subspace, leveraging complementary signals from both views. Experiments across four benchmarks show up to 11 percentage point accuracy gains and strong generalization to out-of-domain problems.
为解决大语言模型在多步逻辑推理中的不足，该研究探索是否存在一个同时对自然语言与符号语言推理视图均对齐的共享内部逻辑子空间。通过在自然语言与符号语言推理链的配对残差激活上应用典型相关分析，学习出最大化跨视图相关性的低维子空间，并设计无训练方法引导模型在该子空间内推理。在四个基准测试中，该方法提升准确率最高达11个百分点，并在跨域问题上表现良好。
A Network-Aware Evaluation of Distributed Energy Resource Control in Smart Distribution Systems
Authors: Houchao Gan
First: 2026-04-21T17:40:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:40:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distribution networks with high penetration of Distributed Energy Resources (DERs) increasingly rely on communication networks to coordinate grid-interactive control. While many distributed control schemes have been proposed, they are often evaluated under idealized communication assumptions, making it difficult to assess their performance under realistic network conditions. This work presents an implementation-driven evaluation of a representative virtual power plant (VPP) dispatch algorithm using a co-simulation framework that couples a linearized distribution-system model with packet-level downlink emulation in ns-3. The study considers a modified IEEE~37-node feeder with high photovoltaic penetration and a primal--dual VPP dispatch that simultaneously targets feeder-head active power tracking and voltage regulation. Communication effects are introduced only on the downlink path carrying dual-variable updates, where per-DER packet delays and a hold-last-value strategy are modeled. Results show that, under ideal communication, the dispatch achieves close tracking of the feeder-head power reference while maintaining voltages within the prescribed limits at selected buses. When realistic downlink delay is introduced, the same controller exhibits large oscillations in feeder-head power and more frequent voltage limit violations. These findings highlight that distributed DER control performance can be strongly influenced by communication behavior and motivate evaluation frameworks that explicitly incorporate network dynamics into the assessment of grid-interactive control schemes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向智能配电网的分布式能源资源控制的网络感知评估
高渗透率分布式能源资源（DERs）的配电网日益依赖通信网络协调电网互动控制。尽管已提出多种分布式控制方案，但它们常在理想通信假设下评估，难以在真实网络条件下评估其性能。本文采用联合仿真框架，将线性化配电网模型与ns-3中的包级下行链路仿真耦合，对代表性虚拟电厂（VPP）调度算法进行实施驱动评估。研究考虑高光伏渗透率的修改IEEE 37节点馈线，采用同时兼顾馈线首端有功功率跟踪与电压调节的原始-对偶VPP调度算法。通信影响仅在传输对偶变量更新的下行链路中引入，建模各DER的包延迟及“保持最后值”策略。结果表明，在理想通信条件下，调度可实现馈线首端功率参考值的紧密跟踪，同时在选定节点维持电压在规定限值内；当引入真实下行链路延迟时，相同控制器出现馈线首端功率大幅振荡及电压限值更频繁越限。这些发现表明分布式DER控制性能受通信行为显著影响，呼吁建立明确纳入网络动态的评估框架，以评估电网互动控制方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate distributed energy resource control under realistic communication conditions, this study employs a co-simulation framework coupling a linearized distribution system model with packet-level downlink emulation in ns-3 to assess a primal-dual virtual power plant dispatch algorithm on a modified IEEE 37-node feeder with high photovoltaic penetration. The controller targets feeder-head power tracking and voltage regulation, with communication effects modeled only on the downlink path carrying dual-variable updates, including per-DER packet delays and a hold-last-value strategy. Results indicate that under ideal communication, the controller achieves accurate power tracking and voltage regulation; however, introducing realistic downlink delays causes significant power oscillations and increased voltage violations, demonstrating that communication dynamics critically impact distributed control performance.
为评估分布式能源资源控制在真实通信环境下的表现，本研究在ns-3网络仿真器与线性化配电系统模型耦合的共仿真框架中，实现并测试了一种基于原-对偶的虚拟电厂调度算法。实验基于高光伏渗透率的修改IEEE 37节点馈线，关注馈线首端功率跟踪与电压调节。结果表明，在理想通信条件下，控制器可精确跟踪功率目标并维持电压限值；但引入现实下行链路延迟后，系统出现显著功率振荡和电压越限频发，说明通信行为对分布式控制性能具有决定性影响。
SpecAgent: A Speculative Retrieval and Forecasting Agent for Code Completion
Authors: George Ma, Anurag Koul, Qi Chen, Yawen Wu, Sachit Kuhar, Yu Yu, Aritra Sengupta, Varun Kumar, Murali Krishna Ramanathan
First: 2025-10-20T08:04:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:40:07+00:00
Comments: In Proceedings of the Sixty-Fourth Annual Meeting of the Association for Computational Linguistics (2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at code-related tasks but often struggle in realistic software repositories, where project-specific APIs and cross-file dependencies are crucial. Retrieval-augmented methods mitigate this by injecting repository context at inference time. The low inference-time latency budget affects either retrieval quality or the added latency adversely impacts user experience. We address this limitation with SpecAgent, an agent that improves both latency and code-generation quality by proactively exploring repository files during indexing and constructing speculative context that anticipates future edits in each file. This indexing-time asynchrony allows thorough context computation, masking latency, and the speculative nature of the context improves code-generation quality. Additionally, we identify the problem of future context leakage in existing benchmarks, which can inflate reported performance. To address this, we construct a synthetic, leakage-free benchmark that enables a more realistic evaluation of our agent against baselines. Experiments show that SpecAgent consistently achieves absolute gains of 9-11% (48-58% relative) compared to the best-performing baselines, while significantly reducing inference latency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpecAgent：一种用于代码补全的推测性检索与预测代理
大型语言模型（LLMs）擅长代码相关任务，但在现实软件仓库中常因项目特定API和跨文件依赖而表现不佳。检索增强方法通过在推理时注入仓库上下文缓解此问题，但推理时的低延迟预算会损害检索质量或增加延迟，影响用户体验。我们提出SpecAgent，该代理在索引阶段主动探索仓库文件，构建可预测未来编辑的推测性上下文，从而同时提升延迟和代码生成质量。索引阶段的异步性允许充分计算上下文并隐藏延迟，推测性上下文则提升代码生成质量。此外，我们发现现有基准测试中存在未来上下文泄露问题，可能夸大性能表现。为此，我们构建了一个无泄露的合成基准，使评估更贴近现实。实验表明，SpecAgent相比最佳基线模型持续获得绝对9-11%（相对48-58%）的性能提升，同时显著降低推理延迟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that large language models struggle with real-world code completion due to project-specific APIs and cross-file dependencies, SpecAgent introduces a speculative retrieval and forecasting mechanism that proactively explores repository files during indexing to construct anticipatory context. This approach decouples context computation from inference time, reducing latency while improving code-generation quality through speculative context. Experiments demonstrate that SpecAgent achieves absolute performance gains of 9-11% (48-58% relative) over top baselines while significantly lowering inference latency, and a newly constructed leakage-free benchmark confirms its robustness against inflated performance metrics.
为了解决大语言模型在真实软件仓库中因项目特定API和跨文件依赖而表现不佳的问题，SpecAgent在索引阶段异步预处理仓库文件，构建可预测未来编辑的推测性上下文，从而同时提升生成质量和推理延迟。该方法通过索引时的异步计算隐藏延迟，并构建更丰富的推测上下文；同时，为消除现有基准中的未来上下文泄露问题，构建了无泄露的合成评估集。实验表明，SpecAgent相较最优基线模型绝对提升9-11%（相对提升48-58%），并显著降低推理延迟。
Ultrametric OGP - parametric RDT \emph{symmetric} binary perceptron connection
Authors: Mihailo Stojnic
First: 2026-04-21T17:36:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:36:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In [97,99,100], an fl-RDT framework is introduced to characterize \emph{statistical computational gaps} (SCGs). Studying \emph{symmetric binary perceptrons} (SBPs), [100] obtained an \emph{algorithmic} threshold estimate $α_a\approx α_c^{(7)}\approx 1.6093$ at the 7th lifting level (for $κ=1$ margin), closely approaching $1.58$ local entropy (LE) prediction [18]. In this paper, we further connect parametric RDT to overlap gap properties (OGPs), another key geometric feature of the solution space. Specifically, for any positive integer $s$, we consider $s$-level ultrametric OGPs ($ult_s$-OGPs) and rigorously upper-bound the associated constraint densities $α_{ult_s}$. To achieve this, we develop an analytical union-bounding program consisting of combinatorial and probabilistic components. By casting the combinatorial part as a convex problem and the probabilistic part as a nested integration, we conduct numerical evaluations and obtain that the tightest bounds at the first two levels, $\barα_{ult_1} \approx 1.6578$ and $\barα_{ult_2} \approx 1.6219$, closely approach the 3rd and 4th lifting level parametric RDT estimates, $α_c^{(3)} \approx 1.6576$ and $α_c^{(4)} \approx 1.6218$. We also observe excellent agreement across other key parameters, including overlap values and the relative sizes of ultrametric clusters. Based on these observations, we propose several conjectures linking $ult$-OGP and parametric RDT. Specifically, we conjecture that algorithmic threshold $α_a=\lim_{s\rightarrow\infty} α_{ult_s} = \lim_{s\rightarrow\infty} \barα{ult_s} = \lim_{r\rightarrow\infty} α_{c}^{(r)}$, and $α_{ult_s} \leq α_{c}^{(s+2)}$ (with possible equality for some (maybe even all) $s$). Finally, we discuss the potential existence of a full isomorphism connecting all key parameters of $ult$-OGP and parametric RDT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超度量OGP - 参数化RDT的对称二元感知器连接
在[97,99,100]中，引入了fl-RDT框架以刻画统计计算间隙（SCGs）。研究对称二元感知器（SBPs）时，[100]在第7级提升（κ=1边距）下获得了算法阈值估计α_a≈α_c^(7)≈1.6093，接近[18]中1.58的局部熵（LE）预测。 本文进一步将参数化RDT与重叠间隙属性（OGPs）——解空间的另一关键几何特征——相连接。具体而言，对任意正整数s，我们考虑s级超度量OGP（ult_s-OGP），并严格上界其相关约束密度α_ult_s。为此，我们开发了一个包含组合与概率成分的分析性联合上界程序。通过将组合部分建模为凸问题，概率部分建模为嵌套积分，我们进行数值评估，得到前两级最紧上界：¯α_ult_1≈1.6578和¯α_ult_2≈1.6219，分别接近第3和第4级提升的参数化RDT估计值α_c^(3)≈1.6576和α_c^(4)≈1.6218。我们还观察到其他关键参数（包括重叠值和超度量簇的相对大小）之间高度一致。 基于这些观察，我们提出若干将ult-OGP与参数化RDT关联的猜想。具体而言，我们猜想算法阈值α_a=lim_{s→∞}α_ult_s=lim_{s→∞}¯α_ult_s=lim_{r→∞}α_c^(r)，且α_ult_s≤α_c^(s+2)（某些（甚至全部）s可能取等）。最后，我们讨论了可能存在一个完全同构，将ult-OGP与参数化RDT的所有关键参数相连接。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better characterize statistical computational gaps in symmetric binary perceptrons, this paper connects parametric RDT with ultrametric overlap gap properties (OGPs) to derive tighter bounds on constraint densities. It introduces s-level ultrametric OGPs and develops an analytical union-bounding program combining combinatorial convex optimization and probabilistic nested integrals. Numerical evaluations show that bounds at the first two levels, α_ult₁ ≈ 1.6578 and α_ult₂ ≈ 1.6219, closely match parametric RDT estimates at higher lifting levels (α_c^(3) ≈ 1.6576 and α_c^(4) ≈ 1.6218), with strong agreement on other solution-space parameters. The authors conjecture that the algorithmic threshold α_a equals the limit of ultrametric OGP bounds as s→∞ and that α_ult_s ≤ α_c^(s+2), suggesting a deep structural correspondence between the two frameworks.
为更精确刻画对称二元感知器中的统计计算间隙，本文将参数化RDT与超度量重叠间隙性质（OGP）相联系，以推导约束密度的更紧上界。文章引入s阶超度量OGP，并构建结合组合凸优化与概率嵌套积分的分析性联合上界程序。数值计算显示，前两阶超度量OGP上界α_ult₁≈1.6578和α_ult₂≈1.6219分别与更高阶参数化RDT估计值α_c^(3)≈1.6576和α_c^(4)≈1.6218高度吻合，且在重叠值与簇大小等关键参数上亦表现一致。文章进一步提出猜想：算法阈值α_a等于超度量OGP上界在s→∞时的极限，且α_ult_s ≤ α_c^(s+2)，暗示二者在结构上存在深层对应关系。
"We are currently clean on OPSEC": Why JD Can't Encrypt
Authors: Maurice Chiodo, Toni Erskine, Dennis Müller, James G. Wright
First: 2026-04-21T17:34:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:34:53+00:00
Comments: 31 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We analyse the 2025 Signalgate leak of sensitive US military information by the Trump administration, addressing why confidentiality was violated (messages leaked to the press) in spite of encryption (Signal), to deepen the socio-technical considerations when designing and deploying encryption. First, we use applied pi-calculus to formally model the boutique secure facility setup requested by the US Defence Secretary, to prove that a leak would not be prevented. We then examine how using a secure channel might still not give overall information security, as, in this case, power imbalances between personnel and officials led to the application of cryptography that compromised their operational security. We look at how cryptographic tools may have instilled a false sense of security, and led officials to "overshare". We then apply this analysis to the Trump administration's general desire to burn through political, legal, and now technical process, and demonstrate geopolitical harms that may arise from such ineffective use of cryptography in a brief use case. We conclude that, even with advancements in usability of cryptographic tools, genuine message security is still out of reach of the "average user".
中文标题/摘要
标题：“我们目前在OPSEC上是干净的”：为何JD无法加密
我们分析了2025年特朗普政府因Signalgate事件泄露敏感美军信息的案例，探讨了尽管使用了加密（Signal），为何保密性仍被破坏（消息泄露给媒体），以深化设计和部署加密时的社会技术考量。首先，我们使用应用π演算正式建模美国防部长要求的精品安全设施设置，证明该设置无法防止信息泄露。接着，我们探讨即使使用安全通道，仍可能无法保障整体信息安全，因为在此案例中，人员与官员之间的权力失衡导致应用的密码学措施损害了其操作安全。我们分析了密码学工具可能造成虚假安全感，促使官员“过度分享”。随后，我们将此分析应用于特朗普政府普遍希望快速消耗政治、法律，如今技术流程的倾向，并展示此类无效密码学使用在简短案例中可能引发的地缘政治危害。我们得出结论：即使密码学工具的可用性不断进步，普通用户仍无法实现真正的消息安全。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why encrypted communications via Signal failed to prevent the 2025 Signalgate leak of sensitive U.S. military information, despite technical encryption, by analyzing the socio-technical context. Using applied pi-calculus, the authors model the secure facility setup requested by the U.S. Defense Secretary and formally prove that it could not prevent leaks. They argue that even secure channels can fail when power imbalances lead users to misapply cryptography, creating a false sense of security that encourages oversharing. The study links this to broader political and legal failures in the Trump administration, showing how cryptographic tools, when misused, can cause geopolitical harm. The conclusion is that, regardless of usability improvements, true message security remains inaccessible to average users.
本文分析了2025年Signalgate事件中，尽管使用了Signal加密通信，敏感的美国军方信息仍被泄露的原因，揭示了技术加密与实际安全之间的脱节。作者运用应用pi演算对美国防长要求的保密设施进行形式化建模，证明其设计无法阻止信息泄露。研究指出，即使使用安全通道，权力失衡也可能导致用户误用加密工具，产生虚假安全感并引发过度分享。文章进一步将此现象与特朗普政府急于绕过政治、法律和技术流程的倾向联系起来，说明不当使用加密可能带来地缘政治危害。最终结论是，即便加密工具的易用性不断提升，普通用户仍难以实现真正的消息安全。
SpanVLA: Efficient Action Bridging and Learning from Negative-Recovery Samples for Vision-Language-Action Model
Authors: Zewei Zhou, Ruining Yang, Xuewei, Qi, Yiluan Guo, Sherry X. Chen, Tao Feng, Kateryna Pistunova, Yishan Shen, Lili Su, Jiaqi Ma
First: 2026-04-21T17:34:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:34:19+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://spanvla.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising autonomous driving paradigm for leveraging world knowledge and reasoning capabilities, especially in long-tail scenarios. However, existing VLA models often struggle with the high latency in action generation using an autoregressive generation framework and exhibit limited robustness. In this paper, we propose SpanVLA, a novel end-to-end autonomous driving framework, integrating an autoregressive reasoning and a flow-matching action expert. First, SpanVLA introduces an efficient bridge to leverage the vision and reasoning guidance of VLM to efficiently plan future trajectories using a flow-matching policy conditioned on historical trajectory initialization, which significantly reduces inference time. Second, to further improve the performance and robustness of the SpanVLA model, we propose a GRPO-based post-training method to enable the VLA model not only to learn from positive driving samples but also to learn how to avoid the typical negative behaviors and learn recovery behaviors. We further introduce mReasoning, a new real-world driving reasoning dataset, focusing on complex, reasoning-demanding scenarios and negative-recovery samples. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM (v1 and v2) demonstrate the competitive performance of the SpanVLA model. Additionally, the qualitative results across diverse scenarios highlight the planning performance and robustness of our model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpanVLA：面向视觉-语言-动作模型的高效动作桥接与负样本恢复学习
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型为利用世界知识和推理能力实现自动驾驶提供了有前景的范式，尤其适用于长尾场景。然而，现有VLA模型在使用自回归生成框架进行动作生成时往往面临高延迟问题，且鲁棒性有限。本文提出SpanVLA，一种新颖的端到端自动驾驶框架，融合自回归推理与流匹配动作专家。首先，SpanVLA引入高效桥接机制，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉与推理指导，基于历史轨迹初始化条件地规划未来轨迹，显著降低推理时间。其次，为提升SpanVLA模型的性能与鲁棒性，我们提出基于GRPO的后训练方法，使VLA模型不仅能从正向驾驶样本中学习，还能学习规避典型负向行为并掌握恢复行为。我们进一步引入mReasoning，一个聚焦复杂推理场景与负样本恢复的全新真实世界驾驶推理数据集。在NAVSim（v1和v2）上的大量实验验证了SpanVLA的竞争力。此外，跨多样场景的定性结果突显了模型的规划能力与鲁棒性。

The method uses a vision-language model to guide trajectory planning via flow-matching, reducing inference latency, and employs a GRPO-based post-training strategy to enable learning from both positive driving examples and negative-recovery scenarios. Experimental results on NAVSIM datasets show SpanVLA achieves competitive performance and demonstrates superior robustness and planning capability across diverse, complex driving situations.
