Quantifying Data Similarity Using Cross Learning
Authors: Shudong Sun, Hao Helen Zhang, Joseph C Watkins
First: 2025-10-13T00:18:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:33:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Measuring dataset similarity is fundamental in machine learning, particularly for transfer learning and domain adaptation. In the context of supervised learning, most existing approaches quantify similarity of two data sets based on their input feature distributions, neglecting label information and feature-response alignment. To address this, we propose the Cross-Learning Score (CLS), which measures dataset similarity through bidirectional generalization performance of decision rules. We establish its theoretical foundation by linking CLS to cosine similarity between decision boundaries under canonical linear models, providing a geometric interpretation. A robust ensemble-based estimator is developed that is easy to implement and bypasses high-dimensional density estimation entirely. For transfer learning applications, we introduce a "transferable zones" framework that categorizes source datasets into positive, ambiguous, and negative transfer regions. To accommodate deep learning, we extend CLS to encoder-head architectures, aligning with modern representation-based pipelines. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of CLS for similarity measurement and transfer assessment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用交叉学习量化数据相似性
测量数据集相似性是机器学习的基础，尤其在迁移学习和领域自适应中至关重要。在监督学习背景下，现有方法大多基于输入特征分布量化两个数据集的相似性，忽略了标签信息和特征-响应对齐。为此，我们提出交叉学习得分（CLS），通过决策规则的双向泛化性能衡量数据集相似性。我们通过将CLS与经典线性模型下决策边界的余弦相似性关联，建立了其理论基础，提供了几何解释。我们开发了一种鲁棒的基于集成的估计器，易于实现且完全避免高维密度估计。针对迁移学习应用，我们引入“可迁移区域”框架，将源数据集划分为正迁移、模糊和负迁移区域。为适应深度学习，我们将CLS扩展至编码器-头架构，与现代基于表示的流水线保持一致。在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验验证了CLS在相似性度量和迁移评估中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately measure dataset similarity for transfer learning and domain adaptation, this work introduces the Cross-Learning Score (CLS), which evaluates similarity by assessing bidirectional generalization performance of decision rules across datasets, thereby incorporating label information and feature-response alignment. Theoretically grounded in cosine similarity between decision boundaries under linear models, CLS is estimated via a robust ensemble method that avoids high-dimensional density estimation. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate CLS’s effectiveness in quantifying similarity and identifying transferable zones, with strong performance in guiding transfer learning success.
为提升迁移学习与领域自适应中数据集相似度的测量精度，本文提出交叉学习得分（CLS），通过双向决策规则泛化性能评估相似性，兼顾特征与标签对齐。理论层面，CLS 与线性模型下决策边界的余弦相似度相关联，且提出易实现的集成估计器，无需高维密度估计。在合成与真实数据集上的实验表明，CLS 在相似度度量与迁移能力评估中表现优异，优于现有方法，并能有效划分源域的正迁移、模糊与负迁移区域。
OmniGen2: Towards Instruction-Aligned Multimodal Generation
Authors: Chenyuan Wu, Pengfei Zheng, Ruiran Yan, Shitao Xiao, Xin Luo, Yueze Wang, Wanli Li, Xiyan Jiang, Yexin Liu, Junjie Zhou, Ze Liu, Ziyi Xia, Chaofan Li, Haoge Deng, Jiahao Wang, Kun Luo, Bo Zhang, Defu Lian, Xinlong Wang, Zhongyuan Wang, Tiejun Huang, Zheng Liu
First: 2025-06-23T17:38:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:32:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
In this work, we introduce OmniGen2, a versatile and open-source generative model designed to provide a unified solution for diverse generation tasks, including text-to-image, image editing, and in-context generation. Unlike OmniGen v1, OmniGen2 features two distinct decoding pathways for text and image modalities, utilizing unshared parameters and a decoupled image tokenizer. This design enables OmniGen2 to build upon existing multimodal understanding models without the need to re-adapt VAE inputs, thereby preserving the original text generation capabilities. To facilitate the training of OmniGen2, we developed comprehensive data construction pipelines, encompassing image editing and in-context generation data. Additionally, we introduce a reflection mechanism tailored for image generation tasks and curate a dedicated reflection dataset based on OmniGen2. Despite its relatively modest parameter size, OmniGen2 achieves competitive results on multiple task benchmarks, including text-to-image and image editing. To further evaluate in-context generation, also referred to as subject-driven tasks, we introduce a new benchmark named OmniContext. OmniGen2 achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source models in terms of consistency. We will release our models, training code, datasets, and data construction pipeline to support future research in this field. Project Page: https://vectorspacelab.github.io/OmniGen2; GitHub Link: https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/OmniGen2

Regression with Large Language Models for Materials and Molecular Property Prediction
Authors: Ryan Jacobs, Maciej P. Polak, Lane E. Schultz, Hamed Mahdavi, Vasant Honavar, Dane Morgan
First: 2024-09-09T21:26:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:27:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We demonstrate the ability of large language models (LLMs) to perform material and molecular property regression tasks, a significant deviation from the conventional LLM use case. We benchmark the Large Language Model Meta AI (LLaMA) 3 on several molecular properties in the QM9 dataset and 28 materials properties. Only composition-based input strings are used as the model input and we fine tune on only the generative loss. We broadly find that LLaMA 3, when fine-tuned using the SMILES representation of molecules, provides useful regression results which can rival standard materials property prediction models like random forest or fully connected neural networks on the QM9 dataset. Not surprisingly, LLaMA 3 errors are 5-10x higher than those of the state-of-the-art models that were trained using far more granular representation of molecules (e.g., atom types and their coordinates) for the same task. Similarly, LLaMA 3 provides comparable, although slightly worse, accuracy relative to random forest and elemental descriptors when using just compound chemical description on our set of 28 materials properties. Interestingly, LLaMA 3 provides improved predictions compared to GPT-3.5 and GPT-4o. This work highlights the versatility of LLMs, suggesting that LLM-like generative models can potentially transcend their traditional applications to tackle complex physical phenomena, thus paving the way for future research and applications in chemistry, materials science and other scientific domains.

Cross-Family Speculative Decoding for Polish Language Models on Apple~Silicon: An Empirical Evaluation of Bielik~11B with UAG-Extended MLX-LM
Authors: Krzysztof Fonal
First: 2026-03-22T19:07:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:25:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by using a small draft model to propose k candidate tokens for a target model to verify. While effective for same-tokenizer pairs on high-bandwidth GPUs, its applicability to cross-family pairs with mismatched tokenizers and consumer-grade unified memory remains underexplored. We extend the MLX-LM framework with Universal Assisted Generation (UAG) to enable cross-tokenizer speculative decoding on Apple Silicon. We evaluate Bielik 11B-Instruct (Mistral-based) as the target model, paired with three draft models: Bielik 1.5B (Qwen-based with custom tokenizer), Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Llama 3.2-1B. Experiments on three Polish-language datasets (Wikipedia, pl_alpaca, synthetic) use draft lengths k in {2, 4, 6} to compare naive and context-aware token translation. Results show: (1) context-aware translation consistently improves acceptance rates across all configurations; (2) the Polish-specialized Bielik 1.5B achieves lower acceptance than general-purpose Qwen2.5 and Llama 3.2 drafters; (3) throughput on Apple Silicon is content-dependent, reaching 1.7x speedup for structured text but failing for varied instructions; and (4) verification cost on unified memory does not amortize as theory predicts because both models are memory-bandwidth bound, making sequential drafting expensive relative to batched verification. We propose a hardware-aware speedup formula and characterize conditions for cross-family speculative decoding on Apple Silicon. This is the first systematic evaluation of cross-family speculative decoding for Polish LLMs and the first empirical study of UAG-based decoding on unified memory architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向苹果硅芯片的波兰语模型跨家族推测解码实证评估：Bielik-11B与扩展UAG-MLX-LM的比较
推测解码通过使用小型草稿模型为目标模型生成k个候选令牌以供验证，从而加速大语言模型推理。尽管在高带宽GPU上对同tokenizer对有效，其在tokenizer不匹配且采用消费级统一内存的跨家族对中的适用性仍待探索。我们通过扩展MLX-LM框架引入通用辅助生成（UAG），以支持苹果硅芯片上的跨tokenizer推测解码。我们以Bielik 11B-Instruct（基于Mistral）为目标模型，搭配三个草稿模型：Bielik 1.5B（基于Qwen并自定义tokenizer）、Qwen2.5-1.5B和Llama 3.2-1B。在三个波兰语数据集（Wikipedia、pl_alpaca、合成数据）上的实验中，采用草稿长度k∈{2,4,6}，比较朴素与上下文感知的令牌翻译。结果表明：（1）上下文感知翻译在所有配置中均持续提升接受率；（2）专为波兰语设计的Bielik 1.5B草稿模型接受率低于通用型Qwen2.5和Llama 3.2；（3）苹果硅芯片上的吞吐量依赖内容，结构化文本可实现1.7倍加速，但对多样化指令则失败；（4）统一内存上的验证成本未如理论预测般摊销，因两模型均受内存带宽限制，使顺序草稿生成相对于批量验证更昂贵。我们提出硬件感知加速公式，并刻画苹果硅芯片上跨家族推测解码的适用条件。这是首个针对波兰语LLM的跨家族推测解码系统性评估，也是首个基于UAG解码在统一内存架构上的实证研究。

Face Anything: 4D Face Reconstruction from Any Image Sequence
Authors: Umut Kocasari, Simon Giebenhain, Richard Shaw, Matthias Nießner
Venue: www
First: 2026-04-21T17:22:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:22:39+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://kocasariumut.github.io/FaceAnything/ , Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wSGHpAscp0Y
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Accurate reconstruction and tracking of dynamic human faces from image sequences is challenging because non-rigid deformations, expression changes, and viewpoint variations occur simultaneously, creating significant ambiguity in geometry and correspondence estimation. We present a unified method for high-fidelity 4D facial reconstruction based on canonical facial point prediction, a representation that assigns each pixel a normalized facial coordinate in a shared canonical space. This formulation transforms dense tracking and dynamic reconstruction into a canonical reconstruction problem, enabling temporally consistent geometry and reliable correspondences within a single feed-forward model. By jointly predicting depth and canonical coordinates, our method enables accurate depth estimation, temporally stable reconstruction, dense 3D geometry, and robust facial point tracking within a single architecture. We implement this formulation using a transformer-based model that jointly predicts depth and canonical facial coordinates, trained using multi-view geometry data that non-rigidly warps into the canonical space. Extensive experiments on image and video benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across reconstruction and tracking tasks, achieving approximately 3$\times$ lower correspondence error and faster inference than prior dynamic reconstruction methods, while improving depth accuracy by 16%. These results highlight canonical facial point prediction as an effective foundation for unified feed-forward 4D facial reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Face Anything：从任意图像序列中进行4D人脸重建
从图像序列中准确重建和跟踪动态人脸具有挑战性，因为非刚性形变、表情变化和视角变化同时发生，导致几何结构和对应关系估计存在显著歧义。我们提出了一种统一的高保真4D人脸重建方法，基于规范人脸点预测表示，该表示为每个像素分配共享规范空间中的归一化人脸坐标。该方法将密集跟踪和动态重建转化为规范空间中的重建问题，使单次前馈模型能够实现时间一致的几何结构和可靠的对应关系。通过联合预测深度和规范坐标，我们的方法在一个架构内实现了精确的深度估计、时间稳定的重建、密集3D几何结构和鲁棒的人脸点跟踪。我们使用基于Transformer的模型联合预测深度和规范人脸坐标，训练数据为多视角几何数据，经非刚性变换映射至规范空间。在图像和视频基准测试中，我们的方法在重建和跟踪任务上均达到当前最优性能，对应误差降低约3倍，推理速度更快，深度精度提升16%。这些结果表明规范人脸点预测是统一前馈4D人脸重建的有效基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately reconstructing dynamic human faces from image sequences due to simultaneous non-rigid deformations, expression changes, and viewpoint variations, the authors propose a unified 4D facial reconstruction method based on canonical facial point prediction, which assigns each pixel a normalized coordinate in a shared canonical space. 为解决图像序列中因非刚性形变、表情变化和视角变化同时存在而导致的动态人脸重建难题，本文提出一种基于规范面部点预测的统一4D人脸重建方法。该方法将每个像素映射到共享规范空间中的标准化面部坐标，将密集跟踪与动态重建转化为规范空间内的重建问题，由单个前馈模型解决。模型采用Transformer架构联合预测深度与规范坐标，训练数据通过非刚性变换映射至规范空间。实验表明，该方法在重建与跟踪任务上达到当前最优水平，对应误差降低约3倍，深度精度提升16%，推理速度更快。
QSLM: A Performance- and Memory-aware Quantization Framework with Tiered Search Strategy for Spike-driven Language Models
Authors: Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra, Pasindu Wickramasinghe, Muhammad Shafique
First: 2026-01-02T13:05:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:21:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Design, Automation and Test in Europe Conference (DATE) 2025 on April 20th-22nd, 2026 in Verona, Italy
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have been emerging as prominent AI models for solving many natural language tasks due to their high performance (e.g., accuracy) and capabilities in generating high-quality responses to the given inputs. However, their large computational cost, huge memory footprints, and high processing power/energy make it challenging for their embedded deployments. Amid several tinyLLMs, recent works have proposed spike-driven language models (SLMs) for significantly reducing the processing power/energy of LLMs. However, their memory footprints still remain too large for low-cost and resource-constrained embedded devices. Manual quantization approach may effectively compress SLM memory footprints, but it requires a huge design time and compute power to find the quantization setting for each network, hence making this approach not-scalable for handling different networks, performance requirements, and memory budgets. To bridge this gap, we propose QSLM, a novel framework that performs automated quantization for compressing pre-trained SLMs, while meeting the performance and memory constraints. To achieve this, QSLM first identifies the hierarchy of the given network architecture and the sensitivity of network layers under quantization, then employs a tiered quantization strategy (e.g., global-, block-, and module-level quantization) while leveraging a multi-objective performance-and-memory trade-off function to select the final quantization setting. Experimental results indicate that our QSLM reduces memory footprint by up to 86.5%, reduces power consumption by up to 20%, maintains high performance across different tasks (i.e., by up to 84.4% accuracy of sentiment classification on the SST-2 dataset and perplexity score of 23.2 for text generation on the WikiText-2 dataset) close to the original non-quantized model while meeting the performance and memory constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QSLM：一种面向脉冲驱动语言模型的性能与内存感知量化框架，采用分层搜索策略
大型语言模型（LLMs）因其高性能（如准确性）和生成高质量响应的能力，已成为解决多种自然语言任务的主流AI模型。然而，其高昂的计算成本、巨大的内存占用和高功耗使其难以在嵌入式设备上部署。尽管近期提出的脉冲驱动语言模型（SLMs）显著降低了LLMs的功耗，但其内存占用仍过大，难以满足低成本、资源受限的嵌入式设备需求。手动量化虽可有效压缩SLM内存，但需大量设计时间和计算资源以确定每种网络的量化设置，因此难以扩展以应对不同网络、性能要求和内存预算。为弥合此差距，我们提出QSLM框架，通过自动化量化压缩预训练SLM，同时满足性能与内存约束。QSLM首先识别给定网络架构的层次结构及各层在量化下的敏感性，然后采用分层量化策略（如全局、块级、模块级量化），并结合多目标性能-内存权衡函数选择最终量化设置。实验表明，QSLM可将内存占用降低高达86.5%，功耗降低高达20%，在不同任务中保持高精度（如在SST-2数据集上情感分类准确率达84.4%，在WikiText-2数据集上文本生成困惑度为23.2），接近原始非量化模型，同时满足性能与内存约束。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to deploy large language models on resource-constrained devices without sacrificing performance, QSLM introduces an automated quantization framework that intelligently balances memory reduction and computational efficiency. It first analyzes the network hierarchy and layer sensitivity under quantization, then applies a tiered quantization strategy—global, block, and module-level—guided by a multi-objective function optimizing for both performance and memory. Experiments show QSLM achieves up to 86.5% memory compression and 20% power reduction while preserving high accuracy (84.4% on SST-2 sentiment classification) and low perplexity (23.2 on WikiText-2), demonstrating its scalability and effectiveness across diverse models and constraints.
为在资源受限设备上部署大语言模型，QSLM提出一种自动化量化框架，兼顾内存压缩与计算效率。该框架首先分析网络结构层次与各层量化敏感度，再采用分层量化策略（全局、块级、模块级），结合多目标函数优化性能与内存的权衡。实验表明，QSLM可实现最高86.5%的内存压缩和20%的功耗降低，同时保持高精度（SST-2情感分类达84.4%）和低困惑度（WikiText-2为23.2），在满足性能与内存约束下接近原始模型表现。
3D Foundation Model for Generalizable Disease Detection in Head Computed Tomography
Authors: Weicheng Zhu, Haoxu Huang, Huanze Tang, Rushabh Musthyala, Boyang Yu, Long Chen, Emilio Vega, Thomas O'Donnell, Seena Dehkharghani, Jennifer A. Frontera, Arjun V. Masurkar, Kara Melmed, Narges Razavian
First: 2025-02-04T23:42:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:21:08+00:00
Comments: Nature Biomedical Engineering (2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Head computed tomography (CT) imaging is a widely-used imaging modality with multitudes of medical indications, particularly in assessing pathology of the brain, skull, and cerebrovascular system. It is commonly the first-line imaging in neurologic emergencies given its rapidity of image acquisition, safety, cost, and ubiquity. Deep learning models may facilitate detection of a wide range of diseases. However, the scarcity of high-quality labels and annotations, particularly among less common conditions, significantly hinders the development of powerful models. To address this challenge, we introduce FM-CT: a Foundation Model for Head CT for generalizable disease detection, trained using self-supervised learning. Our approach pre-trains a deep learning model on a large, diverse dataset of 361,663 non-contrast 3D head CT scans without the need for manual annotations, enabling the model to learn robust, generalizable features. To investigate the potential of self-supervised learning in head CT, we employed both discrimination with self-distillation and masked image modeling, and we construct our model in 3D rather than at the slice level (2D) to exploit the structure of head CT scans more comprehensively and efficiently. The model's downstream classification performance is evaluated using internal and three external datasets, encompassing both in-distribution (ID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) data. Our results demonstrate that the self-supervised foundation model significantly improves performance on downstream diagnostic tasks compared to models trained from scratch and previous 3D CT foundation models on scarce annotated datasets. This work highlights the effectiveness of self-supervised learning in medical imaging and sets a new benchmark for head CT image analysis in 3D, enabling broader use of artificial intelligence for head CT-based diagnosis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于头部CT通用疾病检测的3D基础模型
头部计算机断层扫描（CT）成像是广泛使用的影像学方法，具有多种医学用途，尤其在评估脑部、颅骨和脑血管系统病理方面。由于其成像速度快、安全、成本低且普及率高，常作为神经急症的首选影像检查手段。深度学习模型可促进多种疾病检测，但高质量标注数据稀缺，特别是罕见疾病标注不足，严重制约了强大模型的开发。为解决此问题，我们提出FM-CT：一种基于自监督学习训练的头部CT通用疾病检测基础模型。该模型在361,663例无对比剂的3D头部CT扫描数据集上进行预训练，无需人工标注，从而学习到稳健且具泛化能力的特征。为探索自监督学习在头部CT中的潜力，我们结合自蒸馏判别与掩码图像建模方法，并采用3D而非2D切片级建模，更全面高效地利用头部CT结构。模型在内部及三个外部数据集上的下游分类性能评估涵盖分布内（ID）与分布外（OOD）数据。结果表明，该自监督基础模型在标注数据稀缺情况下，显著优于从零训练的模型及先前3D CT基础模型。本研究凸显了自监督学习在医学影像中的有效性，并为3D头部CT图像分析设立了新基准，推动人工智能在头部CT诊断中的广泛应用。

The model leverages self-distillation and masked image modeling to learn robust features without manual annotations, and operates in 3D to capture spatial structure more effectively than 2D slice-based methods. Evaluated across internal and external datasets including in-distribution and out-of-distribution cases, FM-CT outperforms models trained from scratch and prior 3D CT foundation models, demonstrating strong generalization and setting a new benchmark for AI-driven head CT analysis.
为应对头颅CT扫描中罕见疾病标注数据稀缺的问题，本研究提出FM-CT，一种基于自监督学习的3D基础模型，利用361,663例无标注非增强头颅CT数据进行预训练，结合自蒸馏与掩码图像建模技术，无需人工标注即可学习通用特征，并在3D空间建模以更充分捕捉解剖结构。在包含分布内与分布外数据的内部及三个外部数据集上评估，FM-CT显著优于从零训练及先前3D CT基础模型，展现出优异泛化能力，为AI辅助头颅CT诊断树立新基准。
MAGICIAN: Efficient Long-Term Planning with Imagined Gaussians for Active Mapping
Authors: Shiyao Li, Antoine Guédon, Shizhe Chen, Vincent Lepetit
Venue: CVPR 2026 Oral
First: 2026-03-23T23:53:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:20:27+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CVPR 2026 (Oral). Project webpage: https://shiyao-li.github.io/magician/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Active mapping aims to determine how an agent should move to efficiently reconstruct unknown environments. Most existing approaches rely on greedy next-best-view prediction, resulting in inefficient exploration and incomplete reconstruction. To address this, we introduce MAGICIAN, a novel long-term planning framework that maximizes accumulated surface coverage gain through Imagined Gaussians, a scene representation based on 3D Gaussian Splatting, derived from a pre-trained occupancy network with strong structural priors. This representation enables efficient coverage gain computation for any novel viewpoint via fast volumetric rendering, allowing its integration into a tree-search algorithm for long-horizon planning. We update Imagined Gaussians and refine the trajectory in a closed loop. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across indoor and outdoor benchmarks with varying action spaces, highlighting the advantage of long-term planning in active mapping.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAGICIAN：基于想象高斯的高效主动建图长期规划
主动建图旨在确定智能体应如何移动以高效重建未知环境。现有方法大多依赖贪心的下一最佳视角预测，导致探索效率低下且重建不完整。为解决此问题，我们提出MAGICIAN，一种新颖的长期规划框架，通过基于3D高斯点绘的场景表示——想象高斯（由具有强结构先验的预训练占据网络推导而来）——最大化累积表面覆盖增益。该表示支持通过快速体渲染高效计算任意新视角的覆盖增益，从而可集成至树搜索算法中实现长时规划。我们通过闭环更新想象高斯并优化轨迹。该方法在不同动作空间的室内与室外基准测试中均达到最先进性能，凸显了主动建图中长期规划的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of greedy next-best-view strategies in active mapping, MAGICIAN introduces a long-term planning framework that leverages Imagined Gaussians—a 3D scene representation derived from a pre-trained occupancy network—to compute surface coverage gains for any viewpoint via fast volumetric rendering. This enables integration into a tree-search algorithm for horizon-aware trajectory optimization, with continuous refinement in a closed loop. Experiments across indoor and outdoor benchmarks with diverse action spaces demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, confirming that long-term planning significantly improves exploration efficiency and reconstruction completeness compared to greedy baselines.
为解决现有主动建图方法中贪心策略导致的探索效率低和重建不完整问题，MAGICIAN提出一种基于想象高斯（Imagined Gaussians）的长期规划框架。该框架利用从预训练占据网络中获得的3D高斯表示，通过快速体渲染计算任意新视角的表面覆盖增益，并将其集成到树搜索算法中实现长时规划，再通过闭环更新高斯表示优化轨迹。在多种室内与室外基准测试中，MAGICIAN在不同动作空间下均取得最先进性能，验证了长期规划在主动建图中的优势。
Epistemic orientation in parliamentary discourse is associated with deliberative democracy
Authors: Segun Aroyehun, Stephan Lewandowsky, David Garcia
First: 2026-04-21T17:19:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:19:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The pursuit of truth is central to democratic deliberation and governance, yet political discourse reflects varying epistemic orientations, ranging from evidence-based reasoning grounded in verifiable information to intuition-based reasoning rooted in beliefs and subjective interpretation. We introduce a scalable approach to measure epistemic orientation using the Evidence--Minus--Intuition (EMI) score, derived from large language model (LLM) ratings and embedding-based semantic similarity. Applying this approach to 15 million parliamentary speech segments spanning 1946 to 2025 across seven countries, we examine temporal patterns in discourse and its association with deliberative democracy and governance. We find that EMI is positively associated with deliberative democracy within countries over time, with consistent relationships in both contemporaneous and lagged analyses. EMI is also positively associated with the transparency and predictable implementation of laws as a dimension of governance. These findings suggest that the epistemic nature of political discourse is crucial for both the quality of democracy and governance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：议会话语中的认识论取向与审议式民主相关
追求真理是民主审议与治理的核心，但政治话语反映不同的认识论取向，从基于可验证信息的证据推理，到基于信念和主观解读的直觉推理。我们引入一种可扩展的方法，通过大型语言模型（LLM）评分和基于嵌入的语义相似度计算“证据减直觉”（EMI）得分，以衡量认识论取向。将该方法应用于1946至2025年间七国共1500万段议会发言，我们考察了话语的时间模式及其与审议式民主和治理的关系。研究发现，EMI得分随时间在各国内部与审议式民主呈正相关，且在同期和滞后分析中关系一致。EMI得分也与治理维度中的法律透明度和可预测实施呈正相关。这些发现表明，政治话语的认识论性质对民主与治理质量至关重要。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how epistemic orientation—whether political discourse prioritizes evidence-based reasoning or intuition-based interpretation—affects democratic deliberation and governance. Using a novel scalable method called the Evidence--Minus--Intuition (EMI) score, derived from large language model ratings and semantic similarity, the researchers analyzed 15 million parliamentary speech segments from 1946 to 2025 across seven countries. Their findings reveal that higher EMI scores correlate positively with the development of deliberative democracy over time, both in contemporaneous and lagged analyses, and also with more transparent and predictable law implementation, suggesting that the epistemic quality of political discourse is a key driver of democratic and governance outcomes.
本研究探讨政治话语的求真取向（基于证据或直觉）如何影响民主质量和治理效能。研究者构建了基于大语言模型评分与语义相似度的“证据减直觉”（EMI）得分，分析了1946至2025年间七国共1500万条议会发言片段。结果表明，EMI得分越高，民主协商程度和治理透明性、可预测性越强，且这种关联在同期与滞后分析中均保持稳定，说明政治话语中基于证据的推理对民主质量具有促进作用。
On two ways to use determinantal point processes for Monte Carlo integration
Authors: Guillaume Gautier, Rémi Bardenet, Michal Valko
Venue: NeurIPS 2019
First: 2026-04-21T17:17:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:17:43+00:00
Comments: NeurIPS 2019
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The standard Monte Carlo estimator $\widehat{I}_N^{\mathrm{MC}}$ of $\int fdω$ relies on independent samples from $ω$ and has variance of order $1/N$. Replacing the samples with a determinantal point process (DPP), a repulsive distribution, makes the estimator consistent, with variance rates that depend on how the DPP is adapted to $f$ and $ω$. We examine two existing DPP-based estimators: one by Bardenet & Hardy (2020) with a rate of $\mathcal{O}(N^{-(1+1/d)})$ for smooth $f$, but relying on a fixed DPP. The other, by Ermakov & Zolotukhin (1960), is unbiased with rate of order $1/N$, like Monte Carlo, but its DPP is tailored to $f$. We revisit these estimators, generalize them to continuous settings, and provide sampling algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于使用行列式点过程进行蒙特卡洛积分的两种方法
标准蒙特卡洛估计量 $\widehat{I}_N^{\mathrm{MC}}$ 用于估计 $\int fdω$，依赖于从 $ω$ 中抽取的独立样本，其方差为 $1/N$ 量级。若用行列式点过程（DPP，一种排斥分布）替代样本，可使估计量保持一致，其方差率取决于 DPP 如何适应 $f$ 和 $ω$。我们考察两种现有的基于 DPP 的估计量：Bardenet & Hardy (2020) 提出的方法，对光滑函数 $f$ 具有 $\mathcal{O}(N^{-(1+1/d)})$ 的收敛速率，但依赖固定 DPP；另一种由 Ermakov & Zolotukhin (1960) 提出，无偏且收敛速率与蒙特卡洛方法相同（$1/N$），但其 DPP 针对 $f$ 进行定制。我们重新审视这两种估计量，将其推广至连续情形，并提供采样算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to reduce variance in Monte Carlo integration, this paper investigates two determinantal point process (DPP)-based estimators that leverage repulsive sampling to improve convergence. The first, from Bardenet & Hardy (2020), uses a fixed DPP and achieves a variance rate of O(N^(-(1+1/d))) for smooth functions, while the second, from Ermakov & Zolotukhin (1960), is unbiased with a 1/N rate but requires the DPP to be specifically constructed for the integrand. The authors generalize both methods to continuous settings, provide sampling algorithms, and demonstrate that the tailored DPP estimator can match Monte Carlo performance while the fixed DPP estimator offers faster convergence in high dimensions for smooth functions.
为降低蒙特卡洛积分的方差，本文研究了两种基于行列式点过程（DPP）的估计器，利用排斥性采样提升收敛性。第一种方法（Bardenet & Hardy, 2020）使用固定DPP，对光滑被积函数可达到O(N^(-(1+1/d)))的方差速率；第二种方法（Ermakov & Zolotukhin, 1960）虽无偏且方差速率与蒙特卡洛一致（1/N），但需针对被积函数f定制DPP。作者将两种方法推广至连续域，提供采样算法，并表明定制DPP可匹配蒙特卡洛性能，而固定DPP在高维光滑函数下表现更优。
Unveiling Fine-Grained Visual Traces: Evaluating Multimodal Interleaved Reasoning Chains in Multimodal STEM Tasks
Authors: Jing Jin, Hao Liu, Yan Bai, Yihang Lou, Zhenke Wang, Tianrun Yuan, Juntong Chen, Yongkang Zhu, Fanhu Zeng, Xuanyu Zhu, Yige Xu
First: 2026-04-21T17:17:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:17:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown promising reasoning abilities, yet evaluating their performance in specialized domains remains challenging. STEM reasoning is a particularly valuable testbed because it provides highly verifiable feedback, but existing benchmarks often permit unimodal shortcuts due to modality redundancy and focus mainly on final-answer accuracy, overlooking the reasoning process itself. To address this challenge, we introduce StepSTEM: a graduate-level benchmark of 283 problems across mathematics, physics, chemistry, biology, and engineering for fine-grained evaluation of cross-modal reasoning in MLLMs. StepSTEM is constructed through a rigorous curation pipeline that enforces strict complementarity between textual and visual inputs. We further propose a general step-level evaluation framework for both text-only chain-of-thought and interleaved image-text reasoning, using dynamic programming to align predicted reasoning steps with multiple reference solutions. Experiments across a wide range of models show that current MLLMs still rely heavily on textual reasoning, with even Gemini 3.1 Pro and Claude Opus 4.6 achieving only 38.29% accuracy. These results highlight substantial headroom for genuine cross-modal STEM reasoning and position StepSTEM as a benchmark for fine-grained evaluation of multimodal reasoning. Source code is available at https://github.com/lll-hhh/STEPSTEM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：揭示细粒度视觉痕迹：评估多模态STEM任务中交错式多模态推理链
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）展现了有前景的推理能力，但在专业领域的性能评估仍具挑战性。STEM推理是极具价值的测试平台，因其反馈高度可验证，但现有基准常因模态冗余允许单模态捷径，且主要关注最终答案准确率，忽视推理过程本身。为应对这一挑战，我们提出StepSTEM：一个涵盖数学、物理、化学、生物和工程共283道题的研究生级基准，用于细粒度评估MLLMs的跨模态推理能力。StepSTEM通过严谨的筛选流程构建，强制文本与视觉输入间保持严格互补性。我们进一步提出通用的步骤级评估框架，支持纯文本链式思维与交错图文推理，利用动态规划对预测推理步骤与多个参考解进行对齐。在广泛模型上的实验表明，当前MLLMs仍高度依赖文本推理，即使Gemini 3.1 Pro与Claude Opus 4.6也仅达到38.29%准确率。这些结果凸显真实跨模态STEM推理的巨大提升空间，并确立StepSTEM为多模态推理细粒度评估基准。源代码见https://github.com/lll-hhh/STEPSTEM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in STEM domains where reasoning processes matter as much as final answers, the authors introduce StepSTEM—a graduate-level benchmark of 283 problems across five STEM fields designed to assess fine-grained cross-modal reasoning. The dataset enforces strict complementarity between textual and visual inputs to prevent unimodal shortcuts. They propose a step-level evaluation framework using dynamic programming to align predicted reasoning chains with multiple reference solutions, supporting both text-only and interleaved image-text reasoning. Experiments reveal that even top models like Gemini 3.1 Pro and Claude Opus 4.6 achieve only 38.29% accuracy, indicating heavy reliance on textual reasoning and substantial room for improvement in genuine multimodal STEM reasoning.
为评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在STEM领域中跨模态推理能力，作者构建了StepSTEM基准，包含数学、物理、化学、生物和工程共283道研究生级题目，强制文本与视觉输入互补以避免单模态捷径。他们提出基于动态规划的步骤级评估框架，支持纯文本和图文交错推理，并对预测推理步骤与多参考答案进行对齐。实验表明，即使如Gemini 3.1 Pro和Claude Opus 4.6等顶尖模型准确率也仅38.29%，说明当前模型仍严重依赖文本推理，凸显跨模态STEM推理能力仍有巨大提升空间。
Planning in entropy-regularized Markov decision processes and games
Authors: Jean-Bastien Grill, Omar Darwiche Domingues, Pierre Ménard, Rémi Munos, Michal Valko
Venue: NeurIPS 2019
First: 2026-04-21T17:17:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:17:33+00:00
Comments: NeurIPS 2019
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose SmoothCruiser, a new planning algorithm for estimating the value function in entropy-regularized Markov decision processes and two-player games, given a generative model of the environment. SmoothCruiser makes use of the smoothness of the Bellman operator promoted by the regularization to achieve problem-independent sample complexity of order O~(1/epsilon^4) for a desired accuracy epsilon, whereas for non-regularized settings there are no known algorithms with guaranteed polynomial sample complexity in the worst case.
中文标题/摘要
标题：熵正则化马尔可夫决策过程与博弈中的规划
我们提出了SmoothCruiser，一种用于在给定环境生成模型的情况下估计熵正则化马尔可夫决策过程和两人博弈的价值函数的新规划算法。SmoothCruiser利用正则化带来的贝尔曼算子的平滑性，实现了与问题无关的样本复杂度阶为O~(1/ε⁴)，以达到期望精度ε；而在非正则化设置中，目前尚无已知算法能在最坏情况下保证多项式样本复杂度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient planning in entropy-regularized MDPs and games, the authors introduce SmoothCruiser, an algorithm that leverages the smoothness induced by entropy regularization to estimate value functions. Given a generative model, SmoothCruiser achieves a problem-independent sample complexity of order O~(1/epsilon^4) for accuracy epsilon, outperforming known non-regularized methods which lack polynomial sample complexity guarantees in the worst case.
为提升熵正则化MDP和博弈中的规划效率，作者提出SmoothCruiser算法，利用熵正则化带来的算子平滑性估计价值函数。该算法基于环境生成模型，实现O~(1/epsilon^4)的样本复杂度（epsilon为精度），优于缺乏多项式样本复杂度保证的非正则化方法。
Co-Constructing Alignment: A Participatory Approach to Situate AI Values
Authors: Anne Arzberger, Enrico Liscio, Maria Luce Lupetti, Inigo Martinez de Rituerto de Troya, Jie Yang
First: 2026-01-22T12:20:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:15:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI systems become embedded in everyday practice, value misalignment has emerged as a pressing concern. Yet, dominant alignment approaches remain model centric, treating users as passive recipients of prespecified values rather than as epistemic agents who encounter and respond to misalignment during interactions. Drawing on situated perspectives, we frame alignment as an interactional practice co-constructed during human AI interaction. We investigate how users understand and wish to contribute to this process through a participatory workshop that combines misalignment diaries with generative design activities. We surface how misalignments materialise in practice and how users envision acting on them, grounded in the context of researchers using Large Language Models as research assistants. Our findings show that misalignments are experienced less as abstract ethical violations than as unexpected responses, and task or social breakdowns. Participants articulated roles ranging from adjusting and interpreting model behaviour to deliberate non-engagement as an alignment strategy. We conclude with implications for designing systems that support alignment as an ongoing, situated, and shared practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：共构对齐：一种参与式的人机交互价值定位方法
随着AI系统融入日常实践，价值错位已成为紧迫问题。然而主流对齐方法仍以模型为中心，将用户视为被动接受预设价值的接收者，而非在互动中遭遇并回应错位的主体。基于情境化视角，我们将对齐视为人类与AI互动中共同构建的实践过程。通过结合错位日记与生成式设计活动的参与式工作坊，我们探究用户如何理解并希望参与这一过程。研究聚焦于研究人员使用大语言模型作为研究助手的情境，揭示错位如何在实践中显现，以及用户如何设想应对。研究发现，错位更多被体验为意外反应或任务/社会性中断，而非抽象伦理违规；参与者提出从调整与解释模型行为到主动不参与等多元角色，作为对齐策略。研究最后提出设计支持对齐作为持续、情境化、共享实践的系统意义。
Summary / 总结
As AI systems integrate into daily life, value misalignment poses a critical challenge, yet current alignment methods treat users as passive recipients rather than active participants. Findings reveal that misalignments are experienced as practical disruptions—unexpected responses or task/social breakdowns—rather than abstract ethical failures. Bayesian Event-Based Model for Disease Subtype and Stage Inference
Authors: Hongtao Hao, Joseph L. Austerweil
Venue: Proceedings of Machine Learning Research (PMLR), vol. 297: Machine Learning for Health (ML4H), 2025
First: 2025-12-03T05:45:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:13:47+00:00
Comments: 32 pages; machine learning for health symposium (2025); Proceedings of the 5th Machine Learning for Health Symposium in PMLR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chronic diseases often progress differently across patients. Rather than randomly varying, there are typically a small number of subtypes for how a disease progresses across patients. To capture this structured heterogeneity, the Subtype and Stage Inference Event-Based Model (SuStaIn) estimates the number of subtypes, the order of disease progression for each subtype, and assigns each patient to a subtype from primarily cross-sectional data. It has been widely applied to uncover the subtypes of many diseases and inform our understanding of them. But how robust is its performance? In this paper, we develop a principled Bayesian subtype variant of the event-based model (BEBMS) and compare its performance to SuStaIn in a variety of synthetic data experiments with varied levels of model misspecification. BEBMS substantially outperforms SuStaIn across ordering, staging, and subtype assignment tasks. Further, we apply BEBMS and SuStaIn to a real-world Alzheimer's data set. We find BEBMS has results that are more consistent with the scientific consensus of Alzheimer's disease progression than SuStaIn.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于贝叶斯的事件驱动疾病亚型与分期推断模型
慢性疾病在不同患者中的进展模式通常存在结构化异质性，而非随机变化。为捕捉这种异质性，亚型与分期推断事件驱动模型（SuStaIn）从主要的横断面数据中估计疾病亚型数量、每种亚型的疾病进展顺序，并将每位患者分配至相应亚型。该模型已被广泛应用于揭示多种疾病的亚型并增进对其理解。但其性能究竟有多稳健？本文开发了事件驱动模型的贝叶斯亚型变体（BEBMS），并在多种合成数据实验中（含不同程度模型误设）与SuStaIn进行比较。BEBMS在排序、分期和亚型分配任务中显著优于SuStaIn。此外，我们还将BEBMS与SuStaIn应用于真实阿尔茨海默病数据集，发现BEBMS的结果更符合阿尔茨海默病进展的科学共识。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better capture structured disease progression patterns across patients, this paper introduces a Bayesian event-based model (BEBMS) to improve upon the widely used SuStaIn method. BEBMS incorporates Bayesian principles to estimate disease subtypes, progression order, and patient assignments from cross-sectional data, while being more robust to model misspecification. Experimental results on synthetic datasets show BEBMS consistently outperforms SuStaIn in subtype identification, staging accuracy, and ordering tasks. Applied to real Alzheimer’s data, BEBMS yields results more aligned with established scientific understanding of disease progression than SuStaIn.
为更准确捕捉慢性病在患者间存在的结构化异质性，本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯的事件模型（BEBMS），以改进广泛使用的SuStaIn方法。BEBMS通过贝叶斯框架从横断面数据中估计疾病亚型、进展顺序及患者分配，对模型误设更具鲁棒性。在合成数据实验中，BEBMS在亚型识别、分期准确性和顺序推断任务上均显著优于SuStaIn。在真实阿尔茨海默病数据上的应用显示，BEBMS的结果更符合当前科学界对疾病进展的共识，优于SuStaIn。
A-MAR: Agent-based Multimodal Art Retrieval for Fine-Grained Artwork Understanding
Authors: Shuai Wang, Hongyi Zhu, Jia-Hong Huang, Yixian Shen, Chengxi Zeng, Stevan Rudinac, Monika Kackovic, Nachoem Wijnberg, Marcel Worring
Venue: ICMR 2026, ACM International Conference on Multimedia Retrieval
First: 2026-04-21T17:11:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:11:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Understanding artworks requires multi-step reasoning over visual content and cultural, historical, and stylistic context. While recent multimodal large language models show promise in artwork explanation, they rely on implicit reasoning and internalized knowl- edge, limiting interpretability and explicit evidence grounding. We propose A-MAR, an Agent-based Multimodal Art Retrieval framework that explicitly conditions retrieval on structured reasoning plans. Given an artwork and a user query, A-MAR first decomposes the task into a structured reasoning plan that specifies the goals and evidence requirements for each step. Retrieval is then conditionedon this plan, enabling targeted evidence selection and supporting step-wise, grounded explanations. To evaluate agent-based multi- modal reasoning within the art domain, we introduce ArtCoT-QA. This diagnostic benchmark features multi-step reasoning chains for diverse art-related queries, enabling a granular analysis that extends beyond simple final answer accuracy. Experiments on SemArt and Artpedia show that A-MAR consistently outperforms static, non planned retrieval and strong MLLM baselines in final explanation quality, while evaluations on ArtCoT-QA further demonstrate its advantages in evidence grounding and multi-step reasoning ability. These results highlight the importance of reasoning-conditioned retrieval for knowledge-intensive multimodal understanding and position A-MAR as a step toward interpretable, goal-driven AI systems, with particular relevance to cultural industries. The code and data are available at: https://github.com/ShuaiWang97/A-MAR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：A-MAR：基于代理的多模态艺术检索，用于细粒度艺术品理解
理解艺术品需要对视觉内容及文化、历史和风格背景进行多步推理。尽管近期多模态大语言模型在艺术品解释方面展现出潜力，但它们依赖隐式推理和内化知识，限制了可解释性和明确证据锚定。我们提出A-MAR，一种基于代理的多模态艺术检索框架，其明确将检索条件化于结构化推理计划。给定一件艺术品和用户查询，A-MAR首先将任务分解为结构化推理计划，明确每一步的目标和证据需求。随后，检索基于该计划进行，实现针对性证据选择并支持逐步、锚定的解释。为评估艺术领域中的基于代理的多模态推理，我们引入ArtCoT-QA诊断基准，该基准包含多样艺术相关查询的多步推理链，支持超越简单最终答案准确性的细粒度分析。在SemArt和Artpedia上的实验表明，A-MAR在最终解释质量上持续优于静态、无计划检索及强MLLM基线；在ArtCoT-QA上的评估进一步证明其在证据锚定和多步推理能力上的优势。这些结果突显了推理条件化检索在知识密集型多模态理解中的重要性，并将A-MAR定位为迈向可解释、目标驱动AI系统的一步，尤其适用于文化产业。代码和数据可在：https://github.com/ShuaiWang97/A-MAR 获取。

Motivated by the need for interpretable and evidence-grounded artwork understanding, A-MAR introduces an agent-based framework that explicitly conditions multimodal retrieval on structured reasoning plans. Given an artwork and query, it first decomposes the task into step-by-step reasoning goals with specified evidence requirements, then retrieves relevant evidence conditioned on each step. Evaluated on SemArt and Artpedia, A-MAR outperforms static retrieval and strong MLLM baselines in explanation quality, and on the new ArtCoT-QA benchmark, it demonstrates superior evidence grounding and multi-step reasoning capability, underscoring the value of reasoning-guided retrieval for complex cultural content analysis.
为实现可解释且有证据支撑的艺术品理解，A-MAR提出一种基于代理的多模态检索框架，其核心是将检索过程显式地与结构化推理计划绑定。面对艺术品和用户查询，系统首先生成包含目标与证据需求的分步推理计划，再据此条件化地检索相关证据以支持逐步、有依据的解释。在SemArt和Artpedia数据集上，A-MAR在解释质量上持续优于静态检索和主流多模态大模型基线；在新提出的ArtCoT-QA基准测试中，其在证据定位与多步推理能力上表现更优，凸显了推理引导检索在知识密集型多模态任务中的重要性。
Quantum Non-Linear Bandit Optimization
Authors: Zakaria Shams Siam, Chaowen Guan, Chong Liu
First: 2025-03-04T21:53:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:09:23+00:00
Comments: Camera-ready version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study non-linear bandit optimization where the learner maximizes a black-box function with zeroth order function oracle, which has been successfully applied in many critical applications such as drug discovery and materials design. Existing works have showed that with the aid of quantum computing, it is possible to break the classical $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret lower bound and achieve the new $O(\mathrm{poly}\log T)$ upper bound. However, they usually assume that the objective function sits within the reproducing kernel Hilbert space and their algorithms suffer from the curse of dimensionality. In this paper, we propose the new Q-NLB-UCB algorithm which enjoys an \emph{input dimension-free} $O(\mathrm{poly}\log T)$ upper bound, making it applicable for high-dimensional tasks. At the heart of our algorithm design are quantum Monte Carlo mean estimator, parametric function approximation technique, and a new quantum non-linear regression oracle, which can be of independent interests in more quantum machine learning problems. Our algorithm is also validated for its efficiency compared with other quantum algorithms on both high-dimensional synthetic and real-world tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量子非线性多臂赌博机优化
我们研究非线性多臂赌博机优化问题，其中学习者需通过零阶函数查询器最大化一个黑盒函数，该方法已在药物发现和材料设计等关键应用中取得成功。现有研究显示，借助量子计算，可突破经典Ω(√T)的遗憾下界，实现新的O(poly log T)上界。然而，这些方法通常假设目标函数位于再生核希尔伯特空间内，且算法受维度诅咒影响。本文提出新的Q-NLB-UCB算法，其具有输入维度无关的O(poly log T)上界，适用于高维任务。算法核心包括量子蒙特卡洛均值估计器、参数化函数逼近技术及一种新的量子非线性回归查询器，这些技术对其他量子机器学习问题亦具独立价值。算法在高维合成与真实任务中，经验证其效率优于其他量子算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient non-linear bandit optimization in high-dimensional settings like drug discovery, this paper introduces Q-NLB-UCB, a quantum algorithm that achieves an input dimension-free regret bound of O(poly log T), surpassing the classical Ω(√T) lower bound. The method combines quantum Monte Carlo mean estimation, parametric function approximation, and a novel quantum non-linear regression oracle to avoid the curse of dimensionality. Experimental results confirm its superior efficiency over existing quantum algorithms on both synthetic and real-world high-dimensional tasks.
为应对药物发现等高维非线性带优化任务的需求，本文提出Q-NLB-UCB量子算法，实现无需输入维度的O(poly log T)后悔上界，突破经典Ω(√T)下界。该方法结合量子蒙特卡洛均值估计、参数化函数逼近与新型量子非线性回归算子，有效避免维度诅咒。实验表明其在合成与真实高维任务上显著优于现有量子算法。
An Answer is just the Start: Related Insight Generation for Open-Ended Document-Grounded QA
Authors: Saransh Sharma, Pritika Ramu, Aparna Garimella, Koyel Mukherjee
Venue: ACL
First: 2026-04-21T17:07:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:07:53+00:00
Comments: Paper accepted at ACL Findings 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Answering open-ended questions remains challenging for AI systems because it requires synthesis, judgment, and exploration beyond factual retrieval, and users often refine answers through multiple iterations rather than accepting a single response. Existing QA benchmarks do not explicitly support this refinement process. To address this gap, we introduce a new task, document-grounded related insight generation, where the goal is to generate additional insights from a document collection that help improve, extend, or rethink an initial answer to an open-ended question, ultimately supporting richer user interaction and a better overall question answering experience. We curate and release SCOpE-QA (Scientific Collections for Open-Ended QA), a dataset of 3,000 open-ended questions across 20 research collections. We present InsightGen, a two-stage approach that first constructs a thematic representation of the document collection using clustering, and then selects related context based on neighborhood selection from the thematic graph to generate diverse and relevant insights using LLMs. Extensive evaluation on 3,000 questions using two generation models and two evaluation settings shows that InsightGen consistently produces useful, relevant, and actionable insights, establishing a strong baseline for this new task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：答案只是开始：面向开放文档接地问答的相关洞察生成
回答开放性问题对AI系统仍具挑战，因其需超越事实检索，进行综合、判断与探索，且用户常通过多次迭代优化答案而非接受单一回复。现有问答基准未明确支持此优化过程。为此，我们提出新任务——文档接地相关洞察生成，旨在从文档集合中生成辅助改进、扩展或重构初始答案的额外洞察，从而支持更丰富的用户交互与更优的问答体验。我们构建并发布SCOpE-QA（开放问答科学文献集合），包含20个研究集合中的3000个开放性问题。我们提出InsightGen，一种两阶段方法：首先通过聚类构建文档集合主题表示，再基于主题图邻域选择相关上下文，利用大语言模型生成多样且相关的洞察。在3000个问题上使用两种生成模型与两种评估设置的广泛评估表明，InsightGen持续生成有用、相关且可操作的洞察，为该新任务奠定坚实基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for AI systems to support iterative user refinement in open-ended document-grounded QA, this work introduces a new task: generating related insights to improve or rethink initial answers. The method, InsightGen, uses clustering to build a thematic representation of document collections, then selects context via neighborhood-based graph traversal to guide LLMs in producing diverse, relevant insights. Experiments on 3,000 questions across 20 scientific collections show that InsightGen consistently generates useful, actionable, and contextually relevant insights, establishing a strong baseline for this emerging task.
为支持用户在开放性文档问答中对答案进行迭代优化，本文提出新任务：基于文档生成相关见解以改进或重新思考初始答案。方法 InsightGen 首先通过聚类构建文档集合的主题表示，再基于主题图的邻域选择机制筛选上下文，引导大语言模型生成多样且相关的见解。在20个科学文献集合的3000个问题上实验表明，InsightGen 能稳定生成有用、可操作且语境相关的见解，为该新任务建立了坚实基线。
PREF-XAI: Preference-Based Personalized Rule Explanations of Black-Box Machine Learning Models
Authors: Salvatore Greco, Jacek Karolczak, Roman Słowiński, Jerzy Stefanowski
First: 2026-04-21T17:07:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:07:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) has predominantly focused on generating model-centric explanations that approximate the behavior of black-box models. However, such explanations often overlook a fundamental aspect of interpretability: different users require different explanations depending on their goals, preferences, and cognitive constraints. Although recent work has explored user-centric and personalized explanations, most existing approaches rely on heuristic adaptations or implicit user modeling, lacking a principled framework for representing and learning individual preferences. In this paper, we consider Preference-Based Explainable Artificial Intelligence (PREF-XAI), a novel perspective that reframes explanation as a preference-driven decision problem. Within PREF-XAI, explanations are not treated as fixed outputs, but as alternatives to be evaluated and selected according to user-specific criteria. In the PREF-XAI perspective, here we propose a methodology that combines rule-based explanations with formal preference learning. User preferences are elicited through a ranking of a small set of candidate explanations and modeled via an additive utility function inferred using robust ordinal regression. Experimental results on real-world datasets show that PREF-XAI can accurately reconstruct user preferences from limited feedback, identify highly relevant explanations, and discover novel explanatory rules not initially considered by the user. Beyond the proposed methodology, this work establishes a connection between XAI and preference learning, opening new directions for interactive and adaptive explanation systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PREF-XAI：基于偏好的黑箱机器学习模型个性化规则解释
可解释人工智能（XAI）主要侧重于生成以模型为中心的解释，以近似黑箱模型的行为。然而，此类解释往往忽视可解释性的一个基本方面：不同用户根据其目标、偏好和认知限制需要不同的解释。尽管近期研究已探索用户中心化和个性化解释，但大多数现有方法依赖启发式调整或隐式用户建模，缺乏一个用于表示和学习个体偏好的原则性框架。本文提出偏好驱动的可解释人工智能（PREF-XAI），将解释重新定义为偏好驱动的决策问题。在PREF-XAI框架下，解释不被视为固定输出，而是根据用户特定标准进行评估和选择的备选方案。本文提出一种结合基于规则的解释与形式化偏好学习的方法，通过用户对少量候选解释的排序来获取偏好，并利用鲁棒序回归推断加性效用函数。在真实数据集上的实验表明，PREF-XAI能从有限反馈中准确重建用户偏好，识别高度相关解释，并发现用户未考虑的新型解释规则。此外，本工作建立了XAI与偏好学习之间的联系，为交互式和自适应解释系统开辟了新方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for personalized explanations that align with individual user preferences rather than generic model approximations, this paper introduces PREF-XAI, a framework that treats explanation selection as a preference-driven decision problem. It combines rule-based explanations with formal preference learning, eliciting user preferences through ranking a small set of candidate explanations and modeling them via an additive utility function learned via robust ordinal regression. Experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that PREF-XAI can accurately infer user preferences from minimal feedback, identify highly relevant explanations, and even discover previously unconsidered rules that better satisfy user criteria, thereby advancing the integration of XAI with preference learning.
为解决现有可解释AI方法忽视用户个性化需求的问题，本文提出PREF-XAI框架，将解释选择视为偏好驱动的决策问题。该方法结合基于规则的解释与偏好学习，通过用户对少量候选解释的排序来获取偏好，并利用鲁棒序回归推断加性效用函数。实验表明，PREF-XAI能从有限反馈中准确重建用户偏好，识别高度相关解释，并发现用户未考虑的新规则，推动了交互式与自适应解释系统的发展。
Mask World Model: Predicting What Matters for Robust Robot Policy Learning
Authors: Yunfan Lou, Xiaowei Chi, Xiaojie Zhang, Zezhong Qian, Chengxuan Li, Rongyu Zhang, Yaoxu Lyu, Guoyu Song, Chuyao Fu, Haoxuan Xu, Pengwei Wang, Shanghang Zhang
First: 2026-04-21T17:05:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:05:37+00:00
Comments: 16 pages,5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World models derived from large-scale video generative pre-training have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist robot policy learning. However, standard approaches often focus on high-fidelity RGB video prediction, this can result in overfitting to irrelevant factors, such as dynamic backgrounds and illumination changes. These distractions reduce the model's ability to generalize, ultimately leading to unreliable and fragile control policies. To address this, we introduce the Mask World Model (MWM), which leverages video diffusion architectures to predict the evolution of semantic masks instead of pixels. This shift imposes a geometric information bottleneck, forcing the model to capture essential physical dynamics and contact relations while filtering out visual noise. We seamlessly integrate this mask dynamics backbone with a diffusion-based policy head to enable robust end-to-end control. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the superiority of MWM on the LIBERO and RLBench simulation benchmarks, significantly outperforming the state-of-the-art RGB-based world models. Furthermore, real-world experiments and robustness evaluation (via random token pruning) reveal that MWM exhibits superior generalization capabilities and robust resilience to texture information loss.

标题：掩码世界模型：预测对稳健机器人策略学习至关重要的因素
基于大规模视频生成预训练的世界模型已成为通用机器人策略学习的有前景范式。然而，标准方法通常专注于高保真RGB视频预测，这可能导致模型过度拟合无关因素，如动态背景和光照变化。这些干扰降低了模型的泛化能力，最终导致控制策略不可靠且脆弱。为解决此问题，我们提出了掩码世界模型（MWM），该模型利用视频扩散架构预测语义掩码的演化，而非像素值。这一转变施加了几何信息瓶颈，迫使模型捕捉关键物理动态和接触关系，同时过滤视觉噪声。我们无缝地将此掩码动态主干与基于扩散的策略头集成，以实现稳健的端到端控制。大量评估表明，MWM在LIBERO和RLBench仿真基准上显著优于现有基于RGB的世界模型。此外，真实世界实验和鲁棒性评估（通过随机令牌剪枝）表明，MWM展现出更优的泛化能力和对纹理信息丢失的更强鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the overfitting issues of standard RGB-based world models in robot policy learning, this work introduces the Mask World Model (MWM), which predicts semantic masks instead of raw pixels using video diffusion architectures. This design enforces a geometric information bottleneck, compelling the model to focus on essential physical dynamics and contact relations while ignoring distracting visual noise. The MWM integrates this mask dynamics backbone with a diffusion-based policy head for end-to-end control. Experimental results show MWM outperforms state-of-the-art RGB models on LIBERO and RLBench benchmarks and demonstrates superior generalization and robustness in real-world tests, even under texture loss induced by random token pruning.
为解决传统RGB世界模型易受无关视觉噪声干扰、导致泛化能力差的问题，本文提出掩码世界模型（MWM），通过视频扩散架构预测语义掩码而非像素，强制模型聚焦物理动态与接触关系，过滤视觉干扰。MWM将掩码动态主干与扩散策略头结合，实现端到端控制。在LIBERO与RLBench仿真基准上，MWM显著优于现有RGB世界模型，且在真实世界实验及随机令牌剪枝鲁棒性测试中展现出更强的泛化能力和抗纹理丢失能力。
IYKYK (But AI Doesn't): Automated Content Moderation Does Not Capture Communities' Heterogeneous Attitudes Towards Reclaimed Language
Authors: Christina Chance, Rebecca Pattichis, Arjun Subramonian, James He, Shruti Narayanan, Saadia Gabriel, Kai-Wei Chang
First: 2026-04-17T19:19:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T17:02:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reclaimed slur usage is a common and meaningful practice online for many marginalized communities. It serves as a source of solidarity, identity, and shared experience. However, contemporary automated and AI-based moderation tools for online content largely fail to distinguish between reclaimed and hateful uses of slurs, resulting in the suppression of marginalized voices. In this work, we use quantitative and qualitative methods to examine the attitudes of social media users in LGBTQIA+, Black, and women communities around reclaimed slurs targeting our focus groups including the f-word, n-word, and b-word. With social media users from these communities, we collect and analyze an annotated online slur usage corpus. The corpus includes annotators' perceptions of whether an online text containing a slur should be flagged as hate speech, as well as contextual features of the slur usage. Across all communities and annotation questions, we observe low inter-annotator agreement, indicating substantial disagreement among in-group annotators. This is compounded by the fact that, absent clear contextual signals of identity and intent, even in-group members may disagree on how to interpret reclaimed slur usage online. Semi-structured interviews with annotators suggest that differences in lived experience and personal history contribute to this variation as well. We find poor alignment between annotator judgments and automated hate speech assessments produced by Perspective API. We further observe that certain features of a text such as whether the slur usage was derogatory and if the slur was targeted at oneself are more associated with whether annotators report the text as hate speech. Together, these findings highlight the inherent subjectivity and contextual nature of how marginalized communities interpret slurs online.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IYKYK（但AI做不到）：自动化内容审核无法捕捉边缘群体对重新接纳语言态度的异质性
重新接纳侮辱性词汇是许多边缘化群体在线上常见的、有意义的实践，它作为团结、身份认同和共同经验的来源。然而，当前基于自动化和AI的内容审核工具大多无法区分重新接纳与仇恨性使用侮辱词，导致边缘化声音被压制。本研究采用定量与定性方法，考察LGBTQIA+、黑人及女性群体对针对我们研究对象（如f词、n词、b词）的重新接纳侮辱词的态度。我们收集并分析了来自这些社群的社交媒体用户标注的侮辱词使用语料库，包含标注者对含侮辱词文本是否应被标记为仇恨言论的判断，以及该词使用的语境特征。在所有社群和标注问题中，我们观察到标注者间一致性较低，表明群体内部存在显著分歧。这进一步被加剧，因为缺乏明确的身份和意图语境信号时，即使群体成员也可能对线上重新接纳侮辱词的解读产生分歧。对标注者的半结构化访谈表明，生活经历和个人历史的差异也导致了这种分歧。我们发现，标注者判断与Perspective API生成的自动化仇恨言论评估结果之间缺乏一致性。此外，我们观察到文本某些特征（如侮辱词是否具有贬义、是否针对自身）更与标注者是否报告该文本为仇恨言论相关。这些发现共同强调了边缘化群体在线上解读侮辱词时固有的主观性和语境依赖性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why automated content moderation systems fail to accurately classify reclaimed slurs used by marginalized communities, such as LGBTQIA+, Black, and women groups, as non-hateful. Using a mixed-methods approach, researchers collected and analyzed a corpus of annotated online slur usage—including the f-word, n-word, and b-word—where annotators judged whether each instance should be flagged as hate speech. Results show low inter-annotator agreement across communities, indicating significant disagreement even among in-group members about the appropriateness of reclaimed slurs, often due to differing lived experiences and contextual interpretations. The study further finds that automated tools like Perspective API poorly align with human judgments, and that specific text features—such as whether the slur was derogatory or self-directed—are more predictive of annotator decisions than algorithmic assessments.
