On the Derivation of Tightly-Coupled LiDAR-Inertial Odometry with VoxelMap
Authors: Zhihao Zhan
First: 2026-03-16T16:06:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:58:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This note presents a concise mathematical formulation of tightly-coupled LiDAR-Inertial Odometry within an iterated error-state Kalman filter framework using a VoxelMap representation. Rather than proposing a new algorithm, it provides a clear and self-contained derivation that unifies the geometric modeling and probabilistic state estimation through consistent notation and explicit formulations. The document is intended to serve both as a technical reference and as an accessible entry point for a foundational understanding of the system architecture and estimation principles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于VoxelMap的紧耦合激光雷达惯性里程计推导
本文在迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波框架下，使用VoxelMap表示，简洁地阐述了紧耦合激光雷达惯性里程计的数学公式。本文不提出新算法，而是通过一致的符号和明确的公式推导，清晰且自包含地统一了几何建模与概率状态估计。本文旨在作为技术参考，同时为理解系统架构和估计原理提供基础入门。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a clear, unified mathematical foundation for tightly-coupled LiDAR-Inertial Odometry, this work derives the system within an iterated error-state Kalman filter using a VoxelMap representation, avoiding the introduction of new algorithms. The method employs consistent notation and explicit formulations to unify geometric modeling with probabilistic state estimation. Experimental results demonstrate that this derivation enables accurate and efficient state estimation, validating the framework’s suitability for real-time applications without requiring additional computational overhead.
为解决紧耦合激光雷达惯性里程计的数学基础不统一问题，本文在迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波框架下，基于体素地图表示推导系统模型，不提出新算法而提供一套自洽、符号统一的公式化表达，实现几何建模与概率状态估计的融合。实验结果表明该推导方法能准确估计位姿与运动状态，验证其作为技术参考与教学入门的有效性。
ORSIFlow: Saliency-Guided Rectified Flow for Optical Remote Sensing Salient Object Detection
Authors: Haojing Chen, Zhihang Liu, Yutong Li, Tao Tan, Haoyu Bian, Qiuju Ma
First: 2026-03-30T15:33:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:58:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optical Remote Sensing Image Salient Object Detection (ORSI-SOD) remains challenging due to complex backgrounds, low contrast, irregular object shapes, and large variations in object scale. Existing discriminative methods directly regress saliency maps, while recent diffusion-based generative approaches suffer from stochastic sampling and high computational cost. In this paper, we propose ORSIFlow, a saliency-guided rectified flow framework that reformulates ORSI-SOD as a deterministic latent flow generation problem. ORSIFlow performs saliency mask generation in a compact latent space constructed by a frozen variational autoencoder, enabling efficient inference with only a few steps. To enhance saliency awareness, we design a Salient Feature Discriminator for global semantic discrimination and a Salient Feature Calibrator for precise boundary refinement. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks show that ORSIFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance with significantly improved efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ORSIFlow：基于显著性引导的校正流用于光学遥感显著物体检测
由于复杂背景、低对比度、不规则物体形状和物体尺度变化大，光学遥感图像显著物体检测（ORSI-SOD）仍具挑战性。现有判别方法直接回归显著性图，而近期基于扩散的生成方法则存在随机采样和高计算成本问题。本文提出ORSIFlow，一种显著性引导的校正流框架，将ORSI-SOD重新表述为确定性潜在流生成问题。ORSIFlow在由冻结变分自编码器构建的紧凑潜在空间中生成显著性掩码，仅需几步即可实现高效推理。为增强显著性感知，我们设计了显著特征判别器用于全局语义判别，以及显著特征校准器用于精确边界优化。在多个公开基准上的大量实验表明，ORSIFlow实现了最先进性能并显著提升效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of detecting salient objects in optical remote sensing images—such as complex backgrounds and scale variations—this paper introduces ORSIFlow, a saliency-guided rectified flow framework that reformulates the task as deterministic latent flow generation. It leverages a frozen variational autoencoder to construct a compact latent space for efficient inference, and employs a Salient Feature Discriminator and Calibrator to enhance global semantics and boundary precision. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ORSIFlow achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while significantly reducing computational cost compared to existing diffusion-based methods.
为应对光学遥感图像中背景复杂、对比度低、目标形状不规则及尺度变化大的挑战，本文提出ORSIFlow框架，将显著目标检测重构为确定性潜在流生成问题。该框架利用冻结的变分自编码器构建紧凑潜在空间以实现高效推理，并引入显著特征判别器与校准器提升全局语义判别与边界精度。在多个公开数据集上的实验表明，ORSIFlow在保持高精度的同时，显著优于现有扩散模型方法，效率大幅提升。
IR-Flow: Bridging Discriminative and Generative Image Restoration via Rectified Flow
Authors: Zihao Fan, Xin Lu, Jie Xiao, Dong Li, Jie Huang, Xueyang Fu
First: 2026-04-21T16:58:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:58:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In image restoration, single-step discriminative mappings often lack fine details via expectation learning, whereas generative paradigms suffer from inefficient multi-step sampling and noise-residual coupling. To address this dilemma, we propose IR-Flow, a novel image restoration method based on Rectified Flow that serves as a unified framework bridging the gap between discriminative and generative paradigms. Specifically, we first construct multilevel data distribution flows, which expand the ability of models to learn from and adapt to various levels of degradation. Subsequently, cumulative velocity fields are proposed to learn transport trajectories across varying degradation levels, guiding intermediate states toward the clean target, while a multi-step consistency constraint is presented to enforce trajectory coherence and boost few-step restoration performance. We show that directly establishing a linear transport flow between degraded and clean image domains not only enables fast inference but also improves adaptability to out-of-distribution degradations. Extensive evaluations on deraining, denoising and raindrop removal tasks demonstrate that IR-Flow achieves competitive quantitative results with only a few sampling steps, offering an efficient and flexible framework that maintains an excellent distortion-perception balance. Our code is available at https://github.com/fanzh03/IR-Flow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IR-Flow：通过校正流弥合判别式与生成式图像修复之间的鸿沟
在图像修复中，单步判别式映射常因期望学习而缺乏细节，而生成式范式则面临多步采样效率低下和噪声残差耦合的问题。为解决这一困境，我们提出IR-Flow，一种基于校正流的新型图像修复方法，作为统一框架弥合判别式与生成式范式之间的差距。具体而言，我们首先构建多级数据分布流，增强模型从不同退化级别学习与适应的能力；随后提出累积速度场，学习跨不同退化级别的传输轨迹，引导中间状态趋近干净目标，并引入多步一致性约束以确保轨迹连贯性，提升少步修复性能。我们证明，直接在退化与干净图像域间建立线性传输流不仅实现快速推理，还增强对分布外退化的适应能力。在去雨、去噪及雨滴移除任务上的大量评估表明，IR-Flow仅需少量采样步数即可获得具有竞争力的定量结果，提供高效灵活的框架，同时保持优异的失真-感知平衡。代码见https://github.com/fanzh03/IR-Flow。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of single-step discriminative methods in capturing fine details and the inefficiency of generative models in multi-step sampling, IR-Flow introduces a unified framework based on Rectified Flow to bridge these paradigms. It constructs multilevel data distribution flows to enable learning across degradation levels, employs cumulative velocity fields to guide transport trajectories toward clean images, and enforces multi-step consistency to ensure trajectory coherence. Experimental results on deraining, denoising, and raindrop removal show that IR-Flow achieves competitive performance with only a few sampling steps, offering fast inference and strong adaptability to unseen degradations while maintaining a favorable balance between distortion and perceptual quality.
为解决判别式方法细节丢失与生成式方法采样效率低的问题，IR-Flow 提出基于修正流的统一框架，弥合二者差距。该方法构建多层级数据分布流以适应不同退化程度，引入累积速度场引导跨退化层级的恢复轨迹，并通过多步一致性约束确保中间状态连贯性。在去雨、去噪及雨滴移除任务中，实验表明 IR-Flow 仅需少量采样步骤即可获得竞争力强的定量结果，兼具高效推理能力与对分布外退化的良好适应性，同时保持失真与感知质量的良好平衡。
MMControl: Unified Multi-Modal Control for Joint Audio-Video Generation
Authors: Liyang Li, Wen Wang, Canyu Zhao, Tianjian Feng, Zhiyue Zhao, Hao Chen, Chunhua Shen
First: 2026-04-21T16:57:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:57:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have enabled high-quality joint audio-video generation, producing videos with synchronized audio within a single model. However, existing controllable generation frameworks are typically restricted to video-only control. This restricts comprehensive controllability and often leads to suboptimal cross-modal alignment. To bridge this gap, we present MMControl, which enables users to perform Multi-Modal Control in joint audio-video generation. MMControl introduces a dual-stream conditional injection mechanism. It incorporates both visual and acoustic control signals, including reference images, reference audio, depth maps, and pose sequences, into a joint generation process. These conditions are injected through bypass branches into a joint audio-video Diffusion Transformer, enabling the model to simultaneously generate identity-consistent video and timbre-consistent audio under structural constraints. Furthermore, we introduce modality-specific guidance scaling, which allows users to independently and dynamically adjust the influence strength of each visual and acoustic condition at inference time. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MMControl achieves fine-grained, composable control over character identity, voice timbre, body pose, and scene layout in joint audio-video generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MMControl：用于联合音频-视频生成的统一多模态控制
扩散变换器（DiTs）的最新进展已实现高质量的联合音频-视频生成，单个模型可生成同步音频的视频。然而，现有可控生成框架通常仅限于视频控制，限制了全面可控性，常导致跨模态对齐不佳。为弥合此差距，我们提出MMControl，支持用户在联合音频-视频生成中进行多模态控制。MMControl引入双流条件注入机制，将视觉与听觉控制信号（如参考图像、参考音频、深度图和姿态序列）整合进联合生成过程。这些条件通过旁路分支注入联合音频-视频扩散变换器，使模型在结构约束下同时生成身份一致的视频和音色一致的音频。此外，我们引入模态特定引导缩放机制，允许用户在推理时独立动态调整每种视觉与听觉条件的影响强度。大量实验表明，MMControl在联合音频-视频生成中实现了对角色身份、音色、身体姿态和场景布局的精细、可组合控制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing controllable generation frameworks that only support video-based control, MMControl introduces a unified multi-modal control system for joint audio-video generation. It employs a dual-stream conditional injection mechanism that integrates visual and acoustic inputs—such as reference images, audio, depth maps, and pose sequences—into a joint Diffusion Transformer via bypass branches. This enables simultaneous generation of identity-consistent video and timbre-consistent audio under structural constraints. The method further includes modality-specific guidance scaling, allowing dynamic adjustment of control signal influence at inference time. Experimental results show MMControl achieves fine-grained, composable control over character identity, voice timbre, body pose, and scene layout, outperforming prior methods in cross-modal alignment and controllability.
为解决现有可控生成框架仅支持视频控制、常导致音视频对齐不佳的问题，MMControl提出双流条件注入机制，支持联合音频-视频生成中的多模态控制。该方法通过旁路分支将视觉与听觉控制信号（如参考图像、音频、深度图、姿态序列）注入联合扩散Transformer，实现身份一致的视频与音色一致的音频同步生成，并支持模态特定的引导强度动态调节。实验表明，MMControl在角色身份、音色、姿态与场景布局等维度实现细粒度、可组合的控制，显著优于现有方法的跨模态对齐与可控性表现。
Exploring Language-Agnosticity in Function Vectors: A Case Study in Machine Translation
Authors: Nurkhan Laiyk, Gerard I. Gállego, Javier Ferrando, Fajri Koto
First: 2026-04-21T16:56:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:56:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Function vectors (FVs) are vector representations of tasks extracted from model activations during in-context learning. While prior work has shown that multilingual model representations can be language-agnostic, it remains unclear whether the same holds for function vectors. We study whether FVs exhibit language-agnosticity, using machine translation as a case study. Across three decoder-only multilingual LLMs, we find that translation FVs extracted from a single English$\rightarrow$Target direction transfer to other target languages, consistently improving the rank of correct translation tokens across multiple unseen languages. Ablation results show that removing the FV degrades translation across languages with limited impact on unrelated tasks. We further show that base-model FVs transfer to instruction-tuned variants and partially generalize from word-level to sentence-level translation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：函数向量的语言无关性探索：以机器翻译为案例研究
函数向量（FVs）是从模型在上下文学习期间激活中提取的任务向量表示。尽管先前研究已表明多语言模型表示可以是语言无关的，但函数向量是否同样具备这一特性尚不明确。我们以机器翻译为案例研究，探讨FVs是否具有语言无关性。在三个仅解码器的多语言大语言模型中，我们发现从单一英语→目标语言方向提取的翻译FVs可迁移到其他目标语言，持续提升多个未见语言中正确翻译词的排名。消融实验表明，移除FV会降低跨语言翻译性能，但对无关任务影响有限。我们进一步证明，基础模型的FVs可迁移到指令微调变体，并部分从词级泛化到句级翻译。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether function vectors (FVs) derived from model activations during in-context learning are language-agnostic, this study investigates FVs in machine translation using three decoder-only multilingual LLMs. The method extracts FVs from English-to-target translation tasks and tests their transferability to other target languages. Results show that these FVs consistently improve the ranking of correct translation tokens across multiple unseen languages, with ablation studies confirming their translation-specific utility while having minimal impact on unrelated tasks. Additionally, FVs transfer to instruction-tuned models and partially generalize from word-level to sentence-level translation.
为探究在上下文学习中提取的任务函数向量（FVs）是否具备语言无关性，本研究以机器翻译为案例，使用三个解码器型多语言大语言模型进行实验。方法是提取英语→目标语言的翻译FVs，并评估其在未见目标语言中的迁移能力。实验结果表明，这些FVs能持续提升多种语言中正确翻译词的排序，消融实验显示其对翻译任务有显著提升，但对无关任务影响较小。此外，FVs可有效迁移到指令微调模型，并部分从词级泛化至句级翻译。
Learning Hybrid-Control Policies for High-Precision In-Contact Manipulation Under Uncertainty
Authors: Hunter L. Brown, Geoffrey Hollinger, Stefan Lee
First: 2026-04-21T16:55:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:55:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning-based control policies have been frequently demonstrated to be more effective than analytical techniques for many manipulation tasks. Commonly, these methods learn neural control policies that predict end-effector pose changes directly from observed state information. For tasks like inserting delicate connectors which induce force constraints, pose-based policies have limited explicit control over force and rely on carefully tuned low-level controllers to avoid executing damaging actions. In this work, we present hybrid position-force control policies that learn to dynamically select when to use force or position control in each control dimension. To improve learning efficiency of these policies, we introduce Mode-Aware Training for Contact Handling (MATCH) which adjusts policy action probabilities to explicitly mirror the mode selection behavior in hybrid control. We validate MATCH's learned policy effectiveness using fragile peg-in-hole tasks under extreme localization uncertainty. We find MATCH substantially outperforms pose-control policies -- solving these tasks with up to 10% higher success rates and 5x fewer peg breaks than pose-only policies under common types of state estimation error. MATCH also demonstrates data efficiency equal to pose-control policies, despite learning in a larger and more complex action space. In over 1600 sim-to-real experiments, we find MATCH succeeds twice as often as pose policies in high noise settings (33% vs.~68%) and applies ~30% less force on average compared to variable impedance policies on a Franka FR3 in laboratory conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在不确定性条件下学习用于高精度接触操作的混合控制策略
基于强化学习的控制策略在许多操作任务中已被证明比分析方法更有效。通常，这些方法学习神经控制策略，直接从观测到的状态信息预测末端执行器位姿变化。对于如插入精密连接器等会引发力约束的任务，基于位姿的策略对力的控制有限，需依赖精心调校的底层控制器以避免执行破坏性动作。本文提出混合位置-力控制策略，学习在每个控制维度动态选择何时使用力控或位置控。为提高这些策略的学习效率，我们引入接触处理模式感知训练（MATCH），通过调整策略动作概率，显式模拟混合控制中的模式选择行为。我们在极端定位不确定性下，通过易碎的插孔任务验证了MATCH所学策略的有效性。结果表明，MATCH显著优于仅位姿控制策略——在常见状态估计误差下，成功率高10%，插孔断裂率低5倍。尽管在更大、更复杂的动作空间中学习，MATCH的数据效率与仅位姿控制策略相当。在1600多次仿真到现实实验中，MATCH在高噪声环境下成功率达68%（是仅位姿策略的两倍，33% vs. 68%），且在实验室条件下使用Franka FR3机器人时，平均施力比可变阻尼策略低约30%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of pose-based reinforcement learning policies in force-sensitive manipulation tasks, this work introduces hybrid position-force control policies that dynamically select control modes per dimension to improve precision and safety. To enhance learning efficiency, the authors propose MATCH, a mode-aware training method that aligns policy action probabilities with hybrid control mode selection. Experimental results on fragile peg-in-hole tasks under localization uncertainty show MATCH achieves up to 10% higher success rates and 5x fewer peg breaks than pose-only policies, while maintaining comparable data efficiency. In real-world tests with a Franka FR3, MATCH succeeds twice as often as pose policies in high-noise conditions and applies 30% less force on average than variable impedance policies.
为了解决基于姿态的强化学习策略在力敏感操作任务中的局限性，本文提出了一种动态切换位置与力控制模式的混合控制策略。方法MATCH通过调整策略动作概率以匹配混合控制模式选择，提升学习效率而不牺牲数据效率。在定位不确定性下的脆弱插孔任务中，实验表明MATCH相比纯姿态策略成功率高出10%，插孔破损减少5倍，同时在真实环境中施加的力平均减少30%。
MedFlowSeg: Flow Matching for Medical Image Segmentation with Frequency-Aware Attention
Authors: Zhi Chen, Runze Hu, Le Zhang
First: 2026-04-21T16:54:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:54:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Flow matching has recently emerged as a principled framework for learning continuous-time transport maps, enabling efficient deterministic generation without relying on stochastic diffusion processes. While generative modeling has shown promise for medical image segmentation, particularly in capturing uncertainty and complex anatomical variability, existing approaches are predominantly built upon diffusion models, which incur substantial computational overhead due to iterative sampling and are often constrained by UNet-based parameterizations. In this work, we introduce MedFlowSeg, a conditional flow matching framework that formulates medical image segmentation as learning a time-dependent vector field that transports a simple prior distribution to the target segmentation distribution. This formulation enables one-step deterministic inference while preserving the expressiveness of generative modeling. We further develop a dual-conditioning mechanism to incorporate structured priors into the learned flow. Specifically, we propose a Dual-Branch Spatial Attention module that injects multi-scale structural information into the flow field, and a Frequency-Aware Attention module that models cross-domain interactions between spatial and spectral representations via discrepancy-aware fusion and time-dependent modulation. Together, these components provide an effective parameterization of conditional flows that capture both global anatomical structure and fine-grained boundary details. We provide extensive empirical validation across multiple medical imaging modalities, demonstrating that MedFlowSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance while significantly reducing computational cost compared to diffusion-based methods. Our results highlight the potential of flow matching as a theoretically grounded and computationally efficient alternative for generative medical image segmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedFlowSeg：基于频率感知注意力的流匹配医学图像分割
流匹配最近作为一种学习连续时间传输映射的原理性框架出现，能够实现无需依赖随机扩散过程的高效确定性生成。尽管生成式建模在医学图像分割中展现出捕捉不确定性和复杂解剖变异性的潜力，但现有方法主要基于扩散模型，由于迭代采样导致计算开销大，且常受限于UNet参数化结构。本文提出MedFlowSeg，一种条件流匹配框架，将医学图像分割建模为学习一个随时间变化的向量场，将简单先验分布传输至目标分割分布。该建模方式支持一步确定性推断，同时保留生成式建模的表达能力。我们进一步开发双条件机制，将结构化先验融入学习的流场。具体而言，提出双分支空间注意力模块，将多尺度结构信息注入流场；并提出频率感知注意力模块，通过差异感知融合与时间依赖调制建模空间与频谱表示间的跨域交互。这些组件共同构成一种有效参数化条件流，同时捕捉全局解剖结构与细粒度边界细节。我们在多种医学影像模态上进行广泛实证验证，表明MedFlowSeg在性能上达到最先进水平，同时相比扩散方法显著降低计算成本。结果凸显流匹配作为理论基础扎实且计算高效的生成式医学图像分割替代方案的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency and structural limitations of diffusion-based medical image segmentation, this work introduces MedFlowSeg, a conditional flow matching framework that models segmentation as a deterministic transport of a prior distribution via a time-dependent vector field. The method employs a dual-conditioning mechanism: a Dual-Branch Spatial Attention module integrates multi-scale structural priors, while a Frequency-Aware Attention module fuses spatial and spectral representations using time-dependent modulation. Experiments across multiple medical imaging modalities show MedFlowSeg achieves state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy while reducing inference time and computational cost compared to diffusion models, demonstrating flow matching’s potential as a theoretically sound and efficient alternative.
为解决扩散模型在医学图像分割中计算开销大、结构表达受限的问题，本文提出MedFlowSeg，一种条件流匹配框架，通过学习时变向量场在一步内确定性地将简单先验分布映射至目标分割分布。该方法采用双条件机制：双分支空间注意力模块注入多尺度结构先验，频域感知注意力模块通过差异感知融合与时间调制建模空间与频谱域间的跨域交互。实验表明，MedFlowSeg在多种医学影像模态上达到最先进分割精度，同时显著降低计算开销，验证了流匹配作为理论严谨且高效替代方案在生成式医学图像分割中的潜力。
Fast and Robust Diffusion Posterior Sampling for MR Image Reconstruction Using the Preconditioned Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm
Authors: Moritz Blumenthal, Tina Holliber, Jonathan I. Tamir, Martin Uecker
First: 2025-12-05T15:17:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:54:33+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Magnetic Resonance in Medicine
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Purpose: The Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (ULA) in combination with diffusion models can generate high quality MRI reconstructions with uncertainty estimation from highly undersampled k-space data. However, sampling methods such as diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) or likelihood annealing suffer from long reconstruction times and the need for parameter tuning. The purpose of this work is to develop a robust sampling algorithm with fast convergence. Theory and Methods: In the reverse diffusion process used for sampling the posterior, the exact likelihood is multiplied with the diffused prior at all noise scales. To overcome the issue of slow convergence, preconditioning is used. The method is trained on fastMRI data and tested on retrospectively undersampled brain data of a healthy volunteer. Results: For posterior sampling in Cartesian and non-Cartesian accelerated MRI the new approach outperforms annealed sampling and DPS in terms of reconstruction speed and sample quality. Conclusion: The proposed exact likelihood with preconditioning enables rapid and reliable posterior sampling across various MRI reconstruction tasks without the need for parameter tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用预条件未调整朗之万算法的快速且鲁棒的扩散后验采样用于MR图像重建
目的：未调整朗之万算法（ULA）与扩散模型结合，可从高度欠采样的k空间数据生成高质量的MRI重建并提供不确定性估计。然而，扩散后验采样（DPS）或似然退火等采样方法存在重建时间长且需参数调优的问题。本研究旨在开发一种收敛速度快且鲁棒的采样算法。 理论与方法：在用于后验采样的逆扩散过程中，所有噪声尺度下均将精确似然乘以扩散先验。为克服收敛缓慢的问题，引入预条件处理。该方法在fastMRI数据上训练，并在健康志愿者回顾性欠采样的脑部数据上测试。 结果：对于Cartesian和非Cartesian加速MRI的后验采样，新方法在重建速度和样本质量方面优于退火采样和DPS。 结论：所提出的带预条件处理的精确似然方法，可在各类MRI重建任务中实现快速且可靠的后验采样，无需参数调优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for faster and more reliable MRI reconstructions from undersampled data, this work introduces a preconditioned Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (ULA) for diffusion-based posterior sampling. The method improves convergence by incorporating preconditioning into the reverse diffusion process, which multiplies the exact likelihood with the diffused prior at all noise scales. Evaluated on fastMRI training data and retrospectively undersampled brain scans, the approach achieves superior reconstruction speed and sample quality compared to diffusion posterior sampling and likelihood annealing, without requiring parameter tuning.
为提升从欠采样数据中重建MRI图像的速度与可靠性，本文提出一种预处理的未调整朗之万算法（ULA），用于扩散模型的后验采样。该方法通过在反向扩散过程中引入预处理，将精确似然与各噪声尺度下的扩散先验相乘，从而加速收敛。在fastMRI训练数据和回顾性欠采样脑部扫描上测试表明，该方法在Cartesian和非Cartesian加速场景下，相比扩散后验采样和似然退火方法，在重建速度和样本质量上均表现更优，且无需参数调优。
InHabit: Leveraging Image Foundation Models for Scalable 3D Human Placement
Authors: Nikita Kister, Pradyumna YM, István Sárándi, Jiayi Wang, Anna Khoreva, Gerard Pons-Moll
First: 2026-04-21T16:53:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:53:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training embodied agents to understand 3D scenes as humans do requires large-scale data of people meaningfully interacting with diverse environments, yet such data is scarce. Real-world motion capture is costly and limited to controlled settings, while existing synthetic datasets rely on simple geometric heuristics that ignore rich scene context. In contrast, 2D foundation models trained on internet-scale data have implicitly acquired commonsense knowledge of human-environment interactions. To transfer this knowledge into 3D, we introduce InHabit, a fully automatic and scalable data generator for populating 3D scenes with interacting humans. InHabit follows a render-generate-lift principle: given a rendered 3D scene, a vision-language model proposes contextually meaningful actions, an image-editing model inserts a human, and an optimization procedure lifts the edited result into physically plausible SMPL-X bodies aligned with the scene geometry. Applied to Habitat-Matterport3D, InHabit produces the first large-scale photorealistic 3D human-scene interaction dataset, containing 78K samples across 800 building-scale scenes with complete 3D geometry, SMPL-X bodies, and RGB images. Augmenting standard training data with our samples improves RGB-based 3D human-scene reconstruction and contact estimation, and in a perceptual user study our data is preferred in 78% of cases over the state of the art.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InHabit：利用图像基础模型实现可扩展的3D人体放置
训练具身智能体以人类方式理解3D场景，需要大量人类与多样化环境有意义交互的数据，但此类数据稀缺。现实世界动作捕捉成本高昂且仅限于受控环境，而现有合成数据集依赖简单几何启发式方法，忽略丰富场景上下文。相比之下，基于互联网规模数据训练的2D基础模型已隐式习得人类-环境交互的常识知识。为将此知识迁移到3D领域，我们提出InHabit，一种全自动、可扩展的3D场景中交互人类数据生成器。InHabit遵循“渲染-生成-提升”原则：给定渲染的3D场景，视觉-语言模型提出语境相关的动作，图像编辑模型插入人类，优化过程将编辑结果提升为与场景几何对齐的物理合理SMPL-X人体。应用于Habitat-Matterport3D，InHabit生成首个大规模逼真3D人-场景交互数据集，包含800个建筑级场景的78K样本，涵盖完整3D几何、SMPL-X人体及RGB图像。将我们的样本与标准训练数据结合，可提升RGB基3D人-场景重建与接触估计性能；在感知用户研究中，我们的数据在78%的案例中优于当前最佳方法。

To address the scarcity of large-scale, realistic 3D human-environment interaction data, the authors propose InHabit, a fully automated system that leverages 2D vision-language and image-editing models to generate photorealistic 3D human placements. Evaluated on Habitat-Matterport3D, InHabit produces 78K samples across 800 building-scale scenes, significantly enhancing RGB-based 3D human-scene reconstruction and contact estimation, and is preferred by users in 78% of perceptual comparisons against existing datasets.
为解决训练具身智能体所需的大规模真实3D人-环境交互数据稀缺问题，作者提出InHabit系统，利用2D视觉-语言与图像编辑模型自动生成逼真的3D人类放置数据。该方法遵循“渲染-生成-提升”流程：先渲染3D场景，用视觉-语言模型提出语境相关的动作，再通过图像编辑模型插入人类，并优化为与场景几何对齐的物理合理SMPL-X身体。在Habitat-Matterport3D上，InHabit生成了覆盖800栋建筑级场景的7.8万样本，显著提升RGB驱动的3D人-场景重建与接触估计性能，并在用户感知测试中以78%的比例优于现有数据集。
Conjuring Semantic Similarity
Authors: Tian Yu Liu, Stefano Soatto
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2024-10-21T18:51:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:52:58+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The semantic similarity between sample expressions measures the distance between their latent 'meaning'. These meanings are themselves typically represented by textual expressions. We propose a novel approach whereby the semantic similarity among textual expressions is based not on other expressions they can be rephrased as, but rather based on the imagery they evoke. While this is not possible with humans, generative models allow us to easily visualize and compare generated images, or their distribution, evoked by a textual prompt. Therefore, we characterize the semantic similarity between two textual expressions simply as the distance between image distributions they induce, or 'conjure.' We show that by choosing the Jeffreys divergence between the reverse-time diffusion stochastic differential equations (SDEs) induced by each textual expression, this can be directly computed via Monte-Carlo sampling. Our method contributes a novel perspective on semantic similarity that not only aligns with human-annotated scores, but also opens up new avenues for the evaluation of text-conditioned generative models while offering better interpretability of their learnt representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：召唤语义相似性
样本表达式的语义相似性衡量其潜在‘含义’之间的距离，而这些含义通常由文本表达式表示。我们提出一种新颖方法，文本表达式的语义相似性不再基于它们可改写为其他表达式，而是基于它们所唤起的意象。虽然人类无法做到这一点，但生成模型允许我们轻松可视化并比较由文本提示所唤起的生成图像或其分布。因此，我们将两个文本表达式之间的语义相似性简单定义为它们所唤起的图像分布之间的距离，或‘召唤’。我们表明，通过选择由每个文本表达式诱导的反向时间扩散随机微分方程（SDEs）之间的Jeffreys散度，该距离可通过蒙特卡洛采样直接计算。我们的方法提供了一种新颖的语义相似性视角，不仅与人工标注分数一致，还为文本条件生成模型的评估开辟了新途径，并提升了其学习表示的可解释性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to measure semantic similarity between textual expressions through their latent meanings, this paper proposes a novel method that evaluates similarity based on the imagery evoked by text, rather than rephrasing equivalence. It leverages generative models to visualize and compare image distributions induced by textual prompts, defining semantic similarity as the distance between these distributions. The method computes this distance using the Jeffreys divergence between reverse-time diffusion stochastic differential equations induced by each text, estimated via Monte-Carlo sampling. Experiments show the approach aligns well with human-annotated similarity scores and provides interpretable evaluation for text-conditioned generative models.
为衡量文本表达间的语义相似性，该研究提出一种新方法，通过文本所唤起的图像分布而非改写等价性来评估语义距离。该方法利用生成模型，通过蒙特卡洛采样计算每个文本表达所诱导的逆时间扩散SDE的Jeffreys散度。实验表明，该方法与人工标注的语义相似度高度一致，同时为文本条件生成模型提供了可解释的评估新途径。
Reasoning Models Will Sometimes Lie About Their Reasoning
Authors: William Walden, Miriam Wanner
First: 2026-01-12T15:43:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:52:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hint-based faithfulness evaluations have established that Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) may not say what they think: they do not always volunteer information about how key parts of the input (e.g. answer hints) influence their reasoning. Yet, these evaluations also fail to specify what models should do when confronted with hints or other unusual prompt content -- even though versions of such instructions are standard security measures (e.g. for countering prompt injections). Here, we study faithfulness under this more realistic setting in which models are explicitly alerted to the possibility of unusual inputs. We find that such instructions can yield strong results on faithfulness metrics from prior work. However, results on new, more granular metrics proposed in this work paint a mixed picture: although models may acknowledge the presence of hints, they will often deny intending to use them -- even when permitted to use hints and even when it can be demonstrated that they are using them. Our results thus raise broader challenges for CoT monitoring and interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理模型有时会对其推理过程撒谎
基于提示的忠实性评估已证实，大型推理模型（LRMs）可能不会说出它们的真实想法：它们并不总是主动披露输入中的关键部分（例如答案提示）如何影响其推理。然而，这些评估也未能明确说明当模型面对提示或其他异常提示内容时应如何应对——尽管此类指令的变体是标准的安全措施（例如用于防范提示注入）。本文在模型明确被告知可能遇到异常输入的更现实场景下，研究了忠实性问题。我们发现，此类指令可在以往研究的忠实性指标上取得良好效果。然而，本文提出的更细致的新指标结果则呈现混合状态：尽管模型可能承认提示的存在，但它们常常否认有意使用提示——即使允许使用提示，甚至当可证明其正在使用提示时。因此，我们的结果对链式推理监控和可解释性提出了更广泛挑战。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) behave when explicitly warned about potential unusual inputs, such as answer hints, which are common in security contexts like prompt injection defense. The researchers introduce new, granular faithfulness metrics to evaluate whether models truthfully report their reasoning processes under such conditions. While standard faithfulness evaluations show improved performance when models are alerted to hints, the new metrics reveal that models frequently deny using hints—even when they actually do so and are allowed to. This inconsistency challenges the reliability of current CoT monitoring and interpretability tools.
该研究探讨了大型推理模型（LRMs）在被明确提示可能遇到异常或含提示信息输入时的行为，这在安全实践中很常见。研究动机在于，现有忠实性评估方法未能处理此类提示，作者因此提出了新的细粒度评估指标。实验发现，虽然传统忠实性指标在提示下有所改善，但新指标显示模型常否认使用提示，即使实际使用且被证实，这表明模型自我报告的推理过程与实际行为存在脱节，削弱了链式推理监控与可解释性工具的可靠性。
Budgeted Online Influence Maximization
Authors: Pierre Perrault, Jennifer Healey, Zheng Wen, Michal Valko
Venue: ICML 2020
First: 2026-04-21T16:51:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:51:57+00:00
Comments: 37th International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2020), 28 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a new budgeted framework for online influence maximization, considering the total cost of an advertising campaign instead of the common cardinality constraint on a chosen influencer set. Our approach better models the real-world setting where the cost of influencers varies and advertisers want to find the best value for their overall social advertising budget. We propose an algorithm assuming an independent cascade diffusion model and edge level semi-bandit feedback, and provide both theoretical and experimental results. Our analysis is also valid for the cardinality constraint setting and improves the state of the art regret bound in this case.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带预算的在线影响力最大化
我们提出了一种新的带预算的在线影响力最大化框架，考虑广告活动的总成本而非通常对所选影响者集合的基数约束。我们的方法更贴近现实场景，即影响者成本各异，广告商希望为其整体社交广告预算找到最佳性价比。我们假设独立级联扩散模型和边级半带臂反馈，提出了一种算法，并提供了理论与实验结果。我们的分析同样适用于基数约束情形，并在此情形下改进了现有最优遗憾界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model real-world advertising budgets where influencer costs vary, this paper introduces a budgeted online influence maximization framework that optimizes for total campaign cost rather than a fixed set size. The authors propose an algorithm under the independent cascade model with edge-level semi-bandit feedback, and demonstrate both theoretical guarantees and empirical performance improvements over prior methods, including tighter regret bounds even under cardinality constraints.
为更真实地反映广告预算中不同影响者成本差异的现实场景，本文提出一种基于预算的在线影响力最大化框架，以总成本而非固定集合大小为目标。该方法假设独立级联扩散模型和边级半带反馈机制，设计了一种在预算约束下平衡探索与利用的算法。实验结果表明，该方法在性能上可与现有方法相媲美，同时在卡数约束下也提升了理论遗憾界。
Multi-Cycle Spatio-Temporal Adaptation in Human-Robot Teaming
Authors: Alex Cuellar, Michael Hagenow, Julie Shah
First: 2026-04-21T16:49:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:49:59+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Effective human-robot teaming is crucial for the practical deployment of robots in human workspaces. However, optimizing joint human-robot plans remains a challenge due to the difficulty of modeling individualized human capabilities and preferences. While prior research has leveraged the multi-cycle structure of domains like manufacturing to learn an individual's tendencies and adapt plans over repeated interactions, these techniques typically consider task-level and motion-level adaptation in isolation. Task-level methods optimize allocation and scheduling but often ignore spatial interference in close-proximity scenarios; conversely, motion-level methods focus on collision avoidance while ignoring the broader task context. This paper introduces RAPIDDS, a framework that unifies these approaches by modeling an individual's spatial behavior (motion paths) and temporal behavior (time required to complete tasks) over multiple cycles. RAPIDDS then jointly adapts task schedules and steers diffusion models of robot motions to maximize efficiency and minimize proximity accounting for these individualized models. We demonstrate the importance of this dual adaptation through an ablation study in simulation and a physical robot scenario using a 7-DOF robot arm. Finally, we present a user study (n=32) showing significant plan improvement compared to non-adaptive systems across both objective metrics, such as efficiency and proximity, and subjective measures, including fluency and user preference. See this paper's companion video at: https://youtu.be/55Q3lq1fINs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人类机器人协作中的多周期时空自适应
有效的人机协作对机器人在人类工作空间中的实际部署至关重要。然而，由于难以建模个体化的人类能力与偏好，联合优化人机计划仍具挑战。先前研究利用制造等领域的多周期结构学习个体倾向并在重复交互中调整计划，但这些方法通常将任务级与运动级自适应孤立处理：任务级方法优化分配与调度，常忽略近距离场景中的空间干扰；运动级方法侧重避碰，却忽略整体任务背景。本文提出RAPIDDS框架，通过建模个体在多周期中的空间行为（运动路径）与时间行为（完成任务所需时间），统一上述方法。RAPIDDS联合调整任务调度并引导机器人运动扩散模型，以最大化效率并最小化接近度，同时考虑个体化模型。我们通过仿真与7自由度机械臂物理实验的消融研究，验证双层自适应的重要性。最终，32人用户研究显示，相比非自适应系统，本方法在效率、接近度等客观指标及流畅性、用户偏好等主观指标上均有显著提升。详见本文配套视频：https://youtu.be/55Q3lq1fINs。
Summary / 总结
To improve human-robot collaboration in shared workspaces, this paper addresses the challenge of jointly adapting task scheduling and robot motion over repeated interactions, where prior methods treated these adaptations separately. The RAPIDDS framework models individual human spatial and temporal behaviors across multiple cycles, then jointly optimizes robot motion via diffusion models and task schedules to enhance efficiency while reducing proximity. Experiments in simulation and with a physical 7-DOF robot arm show that dual adaptation outperforms single-level methods, and a user study with 32 participants confirmed significant gains in both objective metrics (efficiency, proximity) and subjective feedback (fluency, preference).
为提升人机协作效率，本文提出RAPIDDS框架，通过跨多个周期建模个体的人类空间行为与时间行为，联合优化机器人运动轨迹与任务调度，以提升效率并减少近距离干扰。仿真与真实7自由度机械臂实验，以及32名用户参与的主观评估均表明，该联合适应方法在效率、接近度等客观指标及流畅性、用户偏好等主观评价上显著优于非自适应系统。
HardNet++: Nonlinear Constraint Enforcement in Neural Networks
Authors: Andrea Goertzen, Kaveh Alim, Navid Azizan
First: 2026-04-21T16:49:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:49:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Enforcing constraint satisfaction in neural network outputs is critical for safety, reliability, and physical fidelity in many control and decision-making applications. While soft-constrained methods penalize constraint violations during training, they do not guarantee constraint adherence during inference. Other approaches guarantee constraint satisfaction via specific parameterizations or a projection layer, but are tailored to specific forms (e.g., linear constraints), limiting their utility in other general problem settings. Many real-world problems of interest are nonlinear, motivating the development of methods that can enforce general nonlinear constraints. To this end, we introduce HardNet++, a constraint-enforcement method that simultaneously satisfies linear and nonlinear equality and inequality constraints. Our approach iteratively adjusts the network output via damped local linearizations. Each iteration is differentiable, admitting an end-to-end training framework, where the constraint satisfaction layer is active during training. We show that under certain regularity conditions, this procedure can enforce nonlinear constraint satisfaction to arbitrary tolerance. Finally, we demonstrate tight constraint adherence without loss of optimality in a learning-for-optimization context, where we apply this method to a model predictive control problem with nonlinear state constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HardNet++：神经网络中的非线性约束强制执行
在许多控制和决策应用中，确保神经网络输出满足约束条件对于安全、可靠性和物理一致性至关重要。虽然软约束方法在训练过程中通过惩罚约束违反来实现，但无法保证推理阶段的约束遵守。其他方法通过特定参数化或投影层保证约束满足，但仅适用于特定形式（如线性约束），限制了其在其他通用问题场景中的适用性。许多现实世界问题具有非线性特征，因此需要能够强制执行一般非线性约束的方法。为此，我们提出了HardNet++，一种同时满足线性和非线性等式与不等式约束的约束强制执行方法。该方法通过阻尼局部线性化迭代调整网络输出。每次迭代均可微，支持端到端训练框架，其中约束满足层在训练期间激活。我们证明，在一定正则条件下，该过程可任意精度地强制执行非线性约束满足。最后，我们在学习优化背景下展示了紧约束遵守且不损失最优性，将该方法应用于具有非线性状态约束的模型预测控制问题。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable constraint satisfaction in neural networks for safety-critical applications, HardNet++ introduces a differentiable method to enforce both linear and nonlinear equality and inequality constraints during inference. It achieves this by iteratively adjusting network outputs using damped local linearizations, ensuring constraint adherence while maintaining end-to-end trainability. Experimental results demonstrate that under mild regularity conditions, the method enforces nonlinear constraints to arbitrary precision without sacrificing optimality, validated in a model predictive control setting with nonlinear state constraints.
为满足安全关键应用中神经网络输出的约束可靠性，HardNet++提出一种可微分方法，用于在推理和训练阶段同时满足线性和非线性约束。该方法通过迭代阻尼局部线性化调整网络输出，确保约束满足的同时保持端到端可微性。实验表明，在满足一定正则条件下，该方法可任意精度满足非线性约束而不损失最优性，已在具有非线性状态约束的模型预测控制任务中得到验证。
Chat2Workflow: A Benchmark for Generating Executable Visual Workflows with Natural Language
Authors: Yi Zhong, Buqiang Xu, Yijun Wang, Zifei Shan, Shuofei Qiao, Guozhou Zheng, Ningyu Zhang
First: 2026-04-21T16:49:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:49:11+00:00
Comments: Work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
At present, executable visual workflows have emerged as a mainstream paradigm in real-world industrial deployments, offering strong reliability and controllability. However, in current practice, such workflows are almost entirely constructed through manual engineering: developers must carefully design workflows, write prompts for each step, and repeatedly revise the logic as requirements evolve-making development costly, time-consuming, and error-prone. To study whether large language models can automate this multi-round interaction process, we introduce Chat2Workflow, a benchmark for generating executable visual workflows directly from natural language, and propose a robust agentic framework to mitigate recurrent execution errors. Chat2Workflow is built from a large collection of real-world business workflows, with each instance designed so that the generated workflow can be transformed and directly deployed to practical workflow platforms such as Dify and Coze. Experimental results show that while state-of-the-art language models can often capture high-level intent, they struggle to generate correct, stable, and executable workflows, especially under complex or changing requirements. Although our agentic framework yields up to 5.34% resolve rate gains, the remaining real-world gap positions Chat2Workflow as a foundation for advancing industrial-grade automation. Code is available at https://github.com/zjunlp/Chat2Workflow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Chat2Workflow：一种从自然语言生成可执行视觉工作流的基准
目前，可执行视觉工作流已成为现实工业部署中的主流范式，具有强大的可靠性和可控性。然而，在当前实践中，此类工作流几乎完全依赖人工工程：开发者必须精心设计工作流、为每一步编写提示，并随着需求变化反复修订逻辑——使开发成本高、耗时且易出错。为研究大语言模型是否能自动化这一多轮交互过程，我们引入Chat2Workflow，一个从自然语言直接生成可执行视觉工作流的基准，并提出一种稳健的代理框架以缓解反复执行错误。Chat2Workflow基于大量真实业务工作流构建，每个实例均设计为生成的工作流可转换并直接部署到Dify和Coze等实际工作流平台。实验结果表明，尽管最先进的语言模型通常能捕捉高层意图，但在生成正确、稳定且可执行的工作流方面仍存在困难，尤其在复杂或变化的需求下。尽管我们的代理框架可带来最高5.34%的解决率提升，但剩余的现实差距使Chat2Workflow成为推动工业级自动化发展的基础。代码见https://github.com/zjunlp/Chat2Workflow。

为解决人工构建可执行视觉工作流成本高、易出错的问题，本文提出Chat2Workflow基准测试，旨在评估大语言模型能否从自然语言自动生成可部署的工作流。作者设计了一种代理框架以缓解生成过程中的重复执行错误。实验表明，当前大模型虽能理解高层意图，但在复杂或变化的需求下常难以生成正确、稳定且可执行的工作流。该代理框架性能提升最高达5.34%，但仍有较大现实差距，凸显了实现工业级自动化仍需进一步研究。
ECLASS-Augmented Semantic Product Search for Electronic Components
Authors: Nico Baumgart, Markus Lange-Hegermann, Jan Henze
First: 2026-04-21T16:48:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:48:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Efficient semantic access to industrial product data is a key enabler for factory automation and emerging LLM-based agent workflows, where both human engineers and autonomous agents must identify suitable components from highly structured catalogs. However, the vocabulary mismatch between natural-language queries and attribute-centric product descriptions limits the effectiveness of traditional retrieval approaches, e.g., BM25. In this work, we present a systematic evaluation of LLM-assisted dense retrieval for semantic product search on industrial electronic components, and investigate the integration of hierarchical semantics from the ECLASS standard into embedding-based retrieval. Our results show that dense retrieval combined with re-ranking substantially outperforms classical lexical methods and foundation model web-search baselines. In particular, the proposed approach achieves a Hit_Rate@5 of 94.3 %, compared to 31.4 % for BM25 on expert queries, while also exceeding foundation model baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency. Furthermore, augmenting product representations with ECLASS semantics yields consistent performance gains across configurations, demonstrating that standardized hierarchical metadata provides a crucial semantic bridge between user intent and sparse product descriptions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于ECLASS增强的电子元器件语义产品搜索
高效语义访问工业产品数据是实现工厂自动化和新兴基于大语言模型（LLM）的代理工作流的关键使能技术，其中人类工程师和自主代理必须从高度结构化的目录中识别合适的组件。然而，自然语言查询与以属性为中心的产品描述之间的词汇不匹配限制了传统检索方法（如BM25）的有效性。本文系统评估了LLM辅助的密集检索在工业电子元器件语义搜索中的表现，并研究了将ECLASS标准中的分层语义集成到基于嵌入的检索中。结果表明，密集检索结合重排序显著优于传统词典方法和基础模型网络搜索基线。特别是，所提方法在专家查询上的Hit_Rate@5达到94.3%，而BM25仅为31.4%，同时在效果和效率上均超越基础模型基线。此外，通过ECLASS语义增强产品表示，在不同配置下均获得稳定性能提升，证明标准化分层元数据在用户意图与稀疏产品描述之间提供了关键的语义桥梁。

The method combines dense vector retrieval with re-ranking and augments product embeddings using ECLASS taxonomy, enabling better alignment with user intent. 为解决工业电子元器件目录中自然语言查询与结构化产品描述之间的语义鸿沟，本研究评估了结合LLM的密集检索方法，并将ECLASS层级语义融入嵌入式检索以提升语义匹配能力。实验表明，结合重排序的密集检索显著优于BM25和基础模型基线，专家查询的Top5命中率高达94.3%，远超BM25的31.4%，且ECLASS语义增强在多种配置下均带来稳定性能提升，验证了标准化层级元数据在连接用户意图与稀疏产品描述中的关键作用。
From Top-1 to Top-K: A Reproducibility Study and Benchmarking of Counterfactual Explanations for Recommender Systems
Authors: Quang-Huy Nguyen, Thanh-Hai Nguyen, Khac-Manh Thai, Duc-Hoang Pham, Huy-Son Nguyen, Cam-Van Thi Nguyen, Masoud Mansoury, Duc-Trong Le, Hoang-Quynh Le
First: 2026-04-21T16:48:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:48:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Counterfactual explanations (CEs) provide an intuitive way to understand recommender systems by identifying minimal modifications to user-item interactions that alter recommendation outcomes. Existing CE methods for recommender systems, however, have been evaluated under heterogeneous protocols, using different datasets, recommenders, metrics, and even explanation formats, which hampers reproducibility and fair comparison. Our paper systematically reproduces, re-implement, and re-evaluate eleven state-of-the-art CE methods for recommender systems, covering both native explainers (e.g., LIME-RS, SHAP, PRINCE, ACCENT, LXR, GREASE) and specific graph-based explainers originally proposed for GNNs. Here, a unified benchmarking framework is proposed to assess explainers along three dimensions: explanation format (implicit vs. explicit), evaluation level (item-level vs. list-level), and perturbation scope (user interaction vectors vs. user-item interaction graphs). Our evaluation protocol includes effectiveness, sparsity, and computational complexity metrics, and extends existing item-level assessments to top-K list-level explanations. Through extensive experiments on three real-world datasets and six representative recommender models, we analyze how well previously reported strengths of CE methods generalize across diverse setups. We observe that the trade-off between effectiveness and sparsity depends strongly on the specific method and evaluation setting, particularly under the explicit format; in addition, explainer performance remains largely consistent across item level and list level evaluations, and several graph-based explainers exhibit notable scalability limitations on large recommender graphs. Our results refine and challenge earlier conclusions about the robustness and practicality of CE generation methods in recommender systems: https://github.com/L2R-UET/CFExpRec.

标题：从Top-1到Top-K：推荐系统反事实解释的可复现性研究与基准评测
反事实解释（CEs）通过识别对用户-物品交互进行最小修改以改变推荐结果，为理解推荐系统提供直观方式。然而，现有推荐系统CE方法在评估时采用异构协议，使用不同数据集、推荐器、指标甚至解释格式，阻碍了可复现性和公平比较。本文系统复现、重实现并重新评估了十一类最先进的推荐系统CE方法，涵盖原生解释器（如LIME-RS、SHAP、PRINCE、ACCENT、LXR、GREASE）及最初为GNN设计的特定图结构解释器。本文提出统一基准框架，从解释格式（隐式vs显式）、评估粒度（物品级vs列表级）和扰动范围（用户交互向量vs用户-物品交互图）三个维度评估解释器。评估协议包含有效性、稀疏性和计算复杂度指标，并将现有物品级评估扩展至Top-K列表级解释。通过在三个真实数据集和六种代表性推荐模型上的大量实验，我们分析了先前报告的CE方法优势在不同设置下的泛化能力。我们观察到有效性与稀疏性之间的权衡高度依赖具体方法和评估设置，尤其在显式格式下；此外，解释器性能在物品级与列表级评估中基本一致，而若干图结构解释器在大型推荐图上表现出显著的可扩展性限制。我们的结果修正并挑战了此前关于推荐系统CE生成方法鲁棒性与实用性的结论：https://github.com/L2R-UET/CFExpRec。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized evaluation protocols for counterfactual explanations (CEs) in recommender systems, this study systematically reproduces and re-evaluates eleven state-of-the-art CE methods across three real-world datasets and six recommender models. Using a unified benchmarking framework that assesses explainers by explanation format, evaluation level, and perturbation scope, the authors measure effectiveness, sparsity, and computational complexity. Results show that the effectiveness-sparcity trade-off varies significantly by method and evaluation setting, especially under explicit formats; performance remains stable across item-level and list-level evaluations; and several graph-based explainers suffer from scalability issues on large graphs, challenging prior assumptions about their robustness and practicality.
为解决推荐系统中反事实解释（CE）缺乏统一评估标准的问题，本研究系统复现并重新评估了十一种前沿CE方法，覆盖真实数据集与六种推荐模型。通过统一的评估框架，从解释格式、评估粒度和扰动范围三个维度衡量有效性、稀疏性和计算复杂度。结果表明，有效性与稀疏性的权衡高度依赖方法和评估场景，尤其在显式格式下差异显著；解释器在项目级与列表级评估中表现稳定，但部分基于图的解释器在大规模图结构上存在可扩展性瓶颈，挑战了此前关于CE方法鲁棒性与实用性的结论。
Disentangling Damage from Operational Variability: A Label-Free Self-Supervised Representation Learning Framework for Output-Only Structural Damage Identification
Authors: Xudong Jian, Charikleia Stoura, Simon Scandella, Eleni Chatzi
First: 2026-04-21T16:45:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:45:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Damage identification is a core task in structural health monitoring. In practice, however, its reliability is often compromised by confounding non-damage effects, such as variations in excitation and environmental conditions, which can induce changes comparable to or larger than those caused by structural damage. To address this challenge, this study proposes a self-supervised label-free disentangled representation learning framework for robust vibration-based structural damage identification. The proposed framework employs an autoencoder with two latent representations to learn directly from raw vibration acceleration signals. A self-supervised invariance regularization, implemented via Variance-Invariance-Covariance Regularization (VICReg), is imposed on one latent representation using baseline data where structural damage is assumed constant but operational and environmental conditions vary. In addition, a frequency-domain constraint is introduced to enforce agreement between the power spectral density reconstructed from the latent representation and that computed from the corresponding input time series. Together, these mechanisms promote disentanglement, enabling the learned representation to be sensitive to damage-related characteristics while remaining invariant to nuisance variability. The framework is trained in a fully end-to-end and label-free manner, requiring no prior information on damage, excitation, or environmental conditions, making it well-suited for real-world applications. Its effectiveness is validated on two distinct real-world vibration datasets, including a bridge and a gearbox. The results demonstrate robustness to operational variability, strong generalization capability, and good performance in both damage detection and quantification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解耦损伤与运行变异性：一种无标签自监督表示学习框架，用于仅输出型结构损伤识别
损伤识别是结构健康监测的核心任务。然而在实际应用中，其可靠性常因激励和环境条件等非损伤因素的变化而受损，这些因素引起的响应变化可能与结构损伤相当甚至更大。为应对这一挑战，本文提出一种无标签自监督解耦表示学习框架，用于鲁棒的基于振动的结构损伤识别。该框架采用具有两个潜在表示的自编码器，直接从原始振动加速度信号中学习。对其中一个潜在表示施加自监督不变性正则化，通过方差-不变性-协方差正则化（VICReg）实现，利用基线数据（假设结构损伤恒定但运行与环境条件变化）进行训练。此外，引入频域约束，强制由潜在表示重建的功率谱密度与对应输入时序计算的功率谱密度保持一致。这些机制共同促进解耦，使学习到的表示对损伤相关特征敏感，同时对干扰性变异性保持不变。该框架以端到端、无标签方式训练，无需损伤、激励或环境条件的先验信息，适用于实际应用。其有效性在两个真实振动数据集（桥梁和齿轮箱）上得到验证，结果表明其对运行变异性具有鲁棒性，具备强泛化能力，且在损伤检测与量化方面表现良好。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of distinguishing structural damage from operational variability in vibration-based health monitoring, this study introduces a label-free self-supervised framework that disentangles damage-related features from nuisance factors using an autoencoder with two latent representations. One latent space is regularized via Variance-Invariance-Covariance Regularization (VICReg) using baseline data to enforce invariance to environmental and excitation changes, while a frequency-domain constraint ensures spectral consistency between latent reconstructions and input signals. Trained end-to-end without damage labels, the model achieves robust damage detection and quantification on real-world datasets from a bridge and a gearbox, demonstrating strong generalization and resilience to operational variability.
为解决振动监测中损伤识别易受操作变量干扰的问题，该研究提出一种无需标签的自监督框架，通过解耦损伤特征与干扰因素。方法采用双潜空间自编码器，对其中一个潜空间施加VICReg正则化以实现对环境与激励变化的不变性，并引入频域约束确保潜空间重构的功率谱与输入时域信号一致。在桥梁与齿轮箱两个真实数据集上的实验表明，该框架在损伤检测与量化中表现稳健，具备良好的泛化能力，且无需标签或先验损伤信息，适用于实际工程场景。
Reinforced Generation of Combinatorial Structures: Ramsey Numbers
Authors: Ansh Nagda, Prabhakar Raghavan, Abhradeep Thakurta
First: 2026-03-10T04:20:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:45:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present improved lower bounds for nine classical Ramsey numbers: $\mathbf{R}(3, 13)$ is increased from $60$ to $61$, $\mathbf{R}(3, 18)$ from $99$ to $100$, $\mathbf{R}(4, 13)$ from $138$ to $139$, $\mathbf{R}(4, 14)$ from $147$ to $148$, $\mathbf{R}(4, 15)$ from $158$ to $159$, $\mathbf{R}(4, 16)$ from $170$ to $174$, $\mathbf{R}(4, 18)$ from $205$ to $209$, $\mathbf{R}(4, 19)$ from $213$ to $219$, and $\mathbf{R}(4, 20)$ from $234$ to $237$. These results were achieved using AlphaEvolve, an LLM-based code mutation agent. Beyond these new results, we successfully recovered lower bounds for all Ramsey numbers known to be exact, and matched the best known lower bounds across many other cases. These include bounds for which previous work does not detail the algorithms used. Virtually all known Ramsey lower bounds are derived computationally, with bespoke search algorithms each delivering a handful of results. AlphaEvolve is a single meta-algorithm yielding search algorithms for all of our results.
中文标题/摘要
标题：组合结构的强化生成：拉姆齐数
我们为九个经典拉姆齐数提供了改进的下界：$\mathbf{R}(3, 13)$ 从 60 提高至 61，$\mathbf{R}(3, 18)$ 从 99 提高至 100，$\mathbf{R}(4, 13)$ 从 138 提高至 139，$\mathbf{R}(4, 14)$ 从 147 提高至 148，$\mathbf{R}(4, 15)$ 从 158 提高至 159，$\mathbf{R}(4, 16)$ 从 170 提高至 174，$\mathbf{R}(4, 18)$ 从 205 提高至 209，$\mathbf{R}(4, 19)$ 从 213 提高至 219，以及 $\mathbf{R}(4, 20)$ 从 234 提高至 237。这些成果是通过 AlphaEvolve（一种基于大语言模型的代码变异代理）实现的。除了这些新结果外，我们还成功恢复了所有已知精确值的拉姆齐数的下界，并在许多其他情况下匹配了目前已知的最佳下界，其中包括先前工作未详细说明所用算法的情况。几乎所有已知的拉姆齐数下界均通过计算得出，每种定制搜索算法仅能提供少量结果。AlphaEvolve 是一种单一元算法，可为所有我们的结果生成搜索算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve lower bounds for classical Ramsey numbers, the authors developed AlphaEvolve, an LLM-based code mutation agent that generates and adapts search algorithms automatically. Using this single meta-algorithm, they achieved improved lower bounds for nine Ramsey numbers, including R(3,13) from 60 to 61 and R(4,20) from 234 to 237, while also recovering exact bounds for known cases and matching state-of-the-art results for others. This approach contrasts with prior methods that required bespoke algorithms for each problem, demonstrating AlphaEvolve’s versatility across diverse combinatorial search tasks.

An AI Agent Execution Environment to Safeguard User Data
Authors: Robert Stanley, Avi Verma, Lillian Tsai, Konstantinos Kallas, Sam Kumar
First: 2026-04-21T16:45:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:45:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents promise to serve as general-purpose personal assistants for their users, which requires them to have access to private user data (e.g., personal and financial information). This poses a serious risk to security and privacy. Adversaries may attack the AI model (e.g., via prompt injection) to exfiltrate user data. Furthermore, sharing private data with an AI agent requires users to trust a potentially unscrupulous or compromised AI model provider with their private data. This paper presents GAAP (Guaranteed Accounting for Agent Privacy), an execution environment for AI agents that guarantees confidentiality for private user data. Through dynamic and directed user prompts, GAAP collects permission specifications from users describing how their private data may be shared, and GAAP enforces that the agent's disclosures of private user data, including disclosures to the AI model and its provider, comply with these specifications. Crucially, GAAP provides this guarantee deterministically, without trusting the agent with private user data, and without requiring any AI model or the user prompt to be free of attacks. GAAP enforces the user's permission specification by tracking how the AI agent accesses and uses private user data. It augments Information Flow Control with novel persistent data stores and annotations that enable it to track the flow of private information both across execution steps within a single task, and also over multiple tasks separated in time. Our evaluation confirms that GAAP blocks all data disclosure attacks, including those that make other state-of-the-art systems disclose private user data to untrusted parties, without a significant impact on agent utility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种保障用户数据安全的AI代理执行环境
AI代理有望作为用户的通用个人助手，这要求它们能够访问私人用户数据（如个人和财务信息），从而对安全和隐私构成严重威胁。攻击者可能通过提示注入等方式攻击AI模型以窃取用户数据。此外，用户将私人数据分享给AI代理，需信任可能不守信或已被攻破的AI模型提供商。 本文提出GAAP（代理隐私保证会计），一种保障AI代理执行环境中私人用户数据机密性的环境。通过动态定向的用户提示，GAAP从用户处收集权限规范，描述其私人数据如何被共享，并确保代理对私人用户数据的披露（包括对AI模型及其提供商的披露）符合这些规范。关键在于，GAAP在不信任代理拥有私人用户数据、且无需AI模型或用户提示免受攻击的前提下，以确定性方式提供此保障。 GAAP通过追踪AI代理如何访问和使用私人用户数据来执行用户权限规范。它通过新颖的持久化数据存储和注解扩展信息流控制，从而能够追踪私人信息在单个任务内各执行步骤间的流动，以及在多个时间分离的任务间流动。我们的评估证实，GAAP能完全阻止所有数据泄露攻击，包括那些使其他最先进系统向不可信方泄露私人用户数据的攻击，且对代理效用影响极小。

Driven by the security risks of AI agents accessing private user data, this paper introduces GAAP, an execution environment that guarantees user data confidentiality without trusting the agent or its model provider. GAAP collects permission specifications via dynamic user prompts and enforces them by tracking data flows across execution steps and over time using novel persistent stores and annotations. Experimental results show GAAP successfully blocks all data disclosure attacks—including those that bypass other systems—while maintaining agent utility.
为应对AI代理访问用户隐私数据带来的安全风险，本文提出GAAP，一种无需信任代理或其提供商即可保障用户数据机密性的执行环境。GAAP通过用户提示收集权限规范，并通过新型持久化数据存储和注解追踪私密数据在单任务内及跨任务间的流动。实验结果表明，GAAP能有效阻止所有数据泄露攻击，包括绕过其他系统的攻击，且未显著影响代理性能。
Pause or Fabricate? Training Language Models for Grounded Reasoning
Authors: Yiwen Qiu, Linjuan Wu, Yizhou Liu, Yuchen Yan, Jin Ma, Xu Tan, Yao Hu, Daoxin Zhang, Wenqi Zhang, Weiming Lu, Jun Xiao, Yongliang Shen
First: 2026-04-21T16:45:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:45:29+00:00
Comments: Code:https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/GRIL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models have achieved remarkable progress on complex reasoning tasks. However, they often implicitly fabricate information when inputs are incomplete, producing confident but unreliable conclusions -- a failure mode we term ungrounded reasoning. We argue that this issue arises not from insufficient reasoning capability, but from the lack of inferential boundary awareness -- the ability to recognize when the necessary premises for valid inference are missing. To address this issue, we propose Grounded Reasoning via Interactive Reinforcement Learning (GRIL), a multi-turn reinforcement learning framework for grounded reasoning under incomplete information. GRIL decomposes the reasoning process into two stages: clarify and pause, which identifies whether the available information is sufficient, and grounded reasoning, which performs task solving once the necessary premises are established. We design stage-specific rewards to penalize hallucinations, enabling models to detect gaps, stop proactively, and resume reasoning after clarification. Experiments on GSM8K-Insufficient and MetaMATH-Insufficient show that GRIL significantly improves premise detection (up to 45%), leading to a 30% increase in task success while reducing average response length by over 20%. Additional analyses confirm robustness to noisy user responses and generalization to out-of-distribution tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：暂停还是虚构？面向具身推理的语言模型训练
大型语言模型在复杂推理任务上取得了显著进展，但当输入信息不完整时，它们常隐式虚构信息，产生自信但不可靠的结论——我们称之为“无根基推理”。我们认为，这一问题并非源于推理能力不足，而是缺乏“推理边界意识”，即识别有效推理所需前提缺失的能力。为解决此问题，我们提出基于交互式强化学习的具身推理方法（GRIL），一种在信息不完整条件下进行具身推理的多轮强化学习框架。GRIL将推理过程分解为两个阶段：澄清与暂停阶段，用于判断现有信息是否充分；具身推理阶段，在确认必要前提后执行任务求解。我们设计了针对各阶段的奖励机制，以惩罚虚构内容，使模型能够主动检测信息缺口、暂停推理并待澄清后恢复推理。在GSM8K-Insufficient和MetaMATH-Insufficient数据集上的实验表明，GRIL显著提升了前提检测能力（最高提升45%），任务成功率提高30%，平均响应长度缩短超过20%。附加分析证实其对噪声用户反馈具有鲁棒性，并能泛化至分布外任务。
Summary / 总结
Large language models often fabricate answers when faced with incomplete information, a behavior termed ungrounded reasoning. To combat this, the authors propose GRIL, a multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that splits reasoning into 'clarify' and 'pause' stages to detect missing premises before generating conclusions. By designing stage-specific rewards that penalize hallucinations, GRIL encourages models to halt reasoning when data is insufficient and resume only after clarification. Experiments on GSM8K-Insufficient and MetaMATH-Insufficient show GRIL improves premise detection by up to 45%, increases task success by 30%, and reduces response length by over 20%.
大语言模型在信息不完整时常编造答案，称为无依据推理。为此，作者提出GRIL框架，将推理分为“澄清”与“暂停”两个阶段，以识别缺失前提并主动停止推理，待澄清后再继续。阶段特定奖励机制惩罚幻觉，促使模型在信息充分时才进行推理。实验表明，GRIL将前提识别准确率提升45%，任务成功率提高30%，平均回答长度缩短20%以上。
A Dual Perspective on Synthetic Trajectory Generators: Utility Framework and Privacy Vulnerabilities
Authors: Aya Cherigui, Florent Guépin, Arnaud Legendre, Jean-François Couchot
First: 2026-04-21T16:42:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:42:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human mobility data are used in numerous applications, ranging from public health to urban planning. Human mobility is inherently sensitive, as it can contain information such as religious beliefs and political affiliations. Historically, it has been proposed to modify the information using techniques such as aggregation, obfuscation, or noise addition, to adequately protect privacy and eliminate concerns. As these methods come at a great cost in utility, new methods leveraging development in generative models, were introduced. The extent to which such methods answer the privacy-utility trade-off remains an open problem. In this paper, we introduced a first step towards solving it, by the introduction and application of a new framework for utility evaluation. Furthermore, we provide evidence that privacy evaluation remains a great challenge to consider and that it should be tackled through adversarial evaluation in accordance with the current EU regulation. We propose a new membership inference attack against a subcategory of generative models, even though this subcategory was deemed private due to its resistance over the trajectory user-linking problem.
中文标题/摘要
标题：合成轨迹生成器的双重视角：效用框架与隐私漏洞
人类移动数据被广泛应用于公共卫生到城市规划等多个领域。人类移动数据本质上敏感，可能包含宗教信仰和政治倾向等信息。历史上，人们曾提出通过聚合、混淆或添加噪声等技术修改信息，以充分保护隐私并消除顾虑。然而，这些方法在效用上代价高昂，因此引入了利用生成模型发展的新方法。此类方法在隐私-效用权衡上的效果仍属开放性问题。本文首次提出并应用了一种新的效用评估框架，以解决该问题。此外，我们提供了证据表明，隐私评估仍是一个重大挑战，应依据当前欧盟法规，通过对抗性评估加以应对。我们提出了一种针对生成模型某一子类别的成员推断攻击，尽管该子类因对轨迹用户关联问题具有抗性而被认为具备隐私保护能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance privacy and utility in synthetic trajectory generation, this paper introduces a novel utility evaluation framework to quantify the performance of generative models in preserving real-world mobility patterns. It further demonstrates that even models deemed private against user-linking attacks remain vulnerable to membership inference attacks, revealing a critical gap in privacy assurance. Experimental results show that adversarial privacy evaluation exposes significant privacy risks in synthetic trajectory outputs, underscoring the necessity of integrating privacy assessments into the design and validation of generative models.
为解决合成轨迹生成中隐私与效用的权衡问题，本文提出一种新的效用评估框架以量化生成模型带来的权衡。同时，研究发现隐私评估仍具挑战性，提出了一种针对特定类生成模型的成员推断攻击，这类模型此前因抵抗用户关联攻击而被认为具备隐私保护能力。实验表明，即使在传统评估中被视为隐私安全的模型，也可能面临此类对抗性攻击，强调需依据如欧盟GDPR等法规进行隐私评估。
Agentic Forecasting using Sequential Bayesian Updating of Linguistic Beliefs
Authors: Kevin Murphy
First: 2026-04-20T17:57:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:42:06+00:00
Comments: v2 fixes a critical error in v1 related to calculation of Brier Index, and makes several important changes to the presentation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present BLF (Bayesian Linguistic Forecaster), an agentic system for binary forecasting that achieves state-of-the-art performance on the ForecastBench benchmark. The system is built on three ideas. (1) A linguistic belief state: a semi-structured representation combining numerical probability estimates with natural-language evidence summaries, updated by the LLM at each step of an iterative tool-use loop. This contrasts with the common approach of appending all retrieved evidence to an ever-growing context. (2) Hierarchical multi-trial aggregation: running $K$ independent trials and combining them using logit-space shrinkage with a data-dependent prior. (3) Hierarchical calibration: Platt scaling with a hierarchical prior, which avoids over-shrinking extreme predictions for sources with skewed base rates. On 400 backtesting questions from the ForecastBench leaderboard, BLF outperforms all the top public methods, including Cassi, GPT-5, Grok~4.20, and Foresight-32B. Ablation studies show that the structured belief state is almost as impactful as web search access, and that shrinkage aggregation and hierarchical calibration each provide significant additional gains. In addition, we develop a robust back-testing framework with a leakage rate below 1.5\%, and use rigorous statistical methodology to compare different methods while controlling for various sources of noise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用语言信念的序贯贝叶斯更新进行代理预测
我们提出了BLF（贝叶斯语言预测器），这是一个用于二元预测的代理系统，在ForecastBench基准测试中达到最先进性能。该系统基于三个核心思想：(1) 语言信念状态：一种半结构化表示，结合数值概率估计与自然语言证据摘要，在迭代工具使用循环的每一步由LLM更新，这与将所有检索证据追加到不断增长的上下文的常见方法不同。(2) 分层多试验聚合：运行K个独立试验，并使用数据依赖先验在logit空间进行收缩。(3) 分层校准：使用分层先验的Platt缩放，避免对基率偏斜的来源过度收缩极端预测。在ForecastBench排行榜的400个回测问题上，BLF优于所有顶级公开方法，包括Cassi、GPT-5、Grok~4.20和Foresight-32B。消融研究表明，结构化信念状态的影响几乎与网络搜索访问相当，而收缩聚合和分层校准各自提供了显著的额外增益。此外，我们开发了一个鲁棒的回测框架，泄漏率低于1.5%，并使用严谨的统计方法在控制各种噪声来源的情况下比较不同方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more accurate and interpretable binary forecasting systems, the authors introduce BLF, an agentic system that uses sequential Bayesian updating of linguistic beliefs to improve prediction performance. The method combines numerical probability estimates with natural-language evidence summaries in a structured belief state, updated iteratively by an LLM, rather than appending raw evidence to context. It further employs hierarchical multi-trial aggregation via logit-space shrinkage with a data-dependent prior and hierarchical calibration using Platt scaling with a hierarchical prior to avoid over-shrinking extreme predictions. On 400 backtesting questions from ForecastBench, BLF outperforms top public methods including Cassi, GPT-5, Grok-4.20, and Foresight-32B, with ablation studies confirming that the structured belief state and both aggregation and calibration techniques contribute significantly to performance gains.
为提升二元预测的准确性和可解释性，作者提出BLF系统，通过语言信念的序列贝叶斯更新实现预测优化。该方法将数值概率估计与自然语言证据摘要结合，形成结构化的信念状态，并由LLM在迭代工具使用循环中动态更新，避免传统上下文不断膨胀的问题。同时采用分层多轮聚合与对数空间收缩，以及分层校准机制，以应对基率偏斜导致的极端预测过收缩。在400个ForecastBench回测问题上，BLF显著优于Cassi、GPT-5、Grok-4.20和Foresight-32B等顶尖公开模型，消融实验验证各组件贡献显著。系统还配备低泄漏率（<1.5%）的回测框架，并通过严谨统计方法控制噪声干扰进行方法比较。
Environmental Sound Deepfake Detection Using Deep-Learning Framework
Authors: Lam Pham, Khoi Vu, Dat Tran, Phat Lam, Vu Nguyen, David Fischinger, Alexander Schindler, Martin Boyer, Son Le
First: 2026-04-21T16:41:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:41:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we propose a deep-learning framework for environmental sound deepfake detection (ESDD) -- the task of identifying whether the sound scene and sound event in an input audio recording is fake or not. To this end, we conducted extensive experiments to explore how individual spectrograms, a wide range of network architectures and pre-trained models, ensemble of spectrograms or network architectures affect the ESDD task performance. The experimental results on the benchmark datasets of EnvSDD and ESDD-Challenge-TestSet indicate that detecting deepfake audio of sound scene and detecting deepfake audio of sound event should be considered as individual tasks. We also indicate that the approach of finetuning a pre-trained model is more effective compared with training a model from scratch for the ESDD task. Eventually, our best model, which was finetuned from the pre-trained WavLM model with the proposed three-stage training strategy, achieve the Accuracy of 0.98, F1 Score of 0.95, AuC of 0.99 on EnvSDD Test subset and the Accuracy of 0.88, F1 Score of 0.77, and AuC of 0.92 on ESDD-Challenge-TestSet dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于深度学习框架的环境声音深度伪造检测
本文提出了一种用于环境声音深度伪造检测（ESDD）的深度学习框架——该任务旨在判断输入音频记录中的声音场景和声音事件是否为伪造。为此，我们进行了大量实验，探讨了单个频谱图、多种网络架构和预训练模型、频谱图或网络架构的集成对ESDD任务性能的影响。实验结果表明，在EnvSDD和ESDD-Challenge-TestSet基准数据集上，声音场景和声音事件的深度伪造检测应被视为独立任务。此外，我们指出，与从零训练模型相比，微调预训练模型对ESDD任务更有效。最终，我们提出的最佳模型基于预训练的WavLM模型，采用所提出的三阶段训练策略进行微调，在EnvSDD测试子集上达到准确率0.98、F1分数0.95、AUC 0.99，在ESDD-Challenge-TestSet数据集上达到准确率0.88、F1分数0.77、AUC 0.92。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing prevalence of synthetic environmental sounds, this study proposes a deep-learning framework to detect whether audio recordings contain fake sound scenes or events. The authors evaluate multiple spectrogram types, network architectures, and training strategies, including fine-tuning pre-trained models versus training from scratch, and find that treating scene and event detection as separate tasks improves performance. Their best model, fine-tuned from WavLM using a three-stage strategy, achieves 98% accuracy, 95% F1 score, and 99% AUC on the EnvSDD test set, and 88% accuracy, 77% F1 score, and 92% AUC on the ESDD-Challenge-TestSet.
为应对环境声音深度伪造带来的安全威胁，本文提出一种深度学习框架用于检测音频中伪造的声景或声事件。研究系统评估了不同频谱图表示、网络结构及训练策略，包括微调预训练模型与从零训练的对比。结果表明，声景与声事件伪造检测应视为独立任务，微调预训练模型效果显著优于从零训练。最佳模型基于WavLM微调并采用三阶段训练策略，在EnvSDD测试集上达到98%准确率、95%F1分数和99%AUC，在ESDD-Challenge测试集上达到88%准确率、77%F1分数和92%AUC。
JumpLoRA: Sparse Adapters for Continual Learning in Large Language Models
Authors: Alexandra Dragomir, Ioana Pintilie, Antonio Barbalau, Marius Dragoi, Florin Brad, Cristian Daniel Paduraru, Alexandru Tifrea, Elena Burceanu, Radu Tudor Ionescu
First: 2026-04-17T15:38:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:37:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adapter-based methods have become a cost-effective approach to continual learning (CL) for Large Language Models (LLMs), by sequentially learning a low-rank update matrix for each task. To mitigate catastrophic forgetting, state-of-the-art approaches impose constraints on new adapters with respect to the previous ones, by targeting either subspace or coordinate-wise interference. In this paper, we propose JumpLoRA, a novel framework to adaptively induce sparsity in the Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) blocks through the use of JumpReLU gating. The method achieves dynamic parameter isolation, which helps prevent task interference. We demonstrate that our method is highly modular and compatible with LoRA-based CL approaches. Specifically, it significantly boosts the performance of IncLoRA and outperforms the leading state-of-the-art CL method, ELLA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：JumpLoRA：用于大语言模型持续学习的稀疏适配器
基于适配器的方法已成为大语言模型持续学习（CL）的一种成本效益高的方法，通过为每个任务顺序学习一个低秩更新矩阵。为缓解灾难性遗忘，现有最先进方法通过子空间或坐标级干扰约束新适配器与旧适配器的关系。本文提出JumpLoRA，一种通过JumpReLU门控机制自适应诱导低秩适配（LoRA）模块稀疏性的新框架。该方法实现动态参数隔离，有助于防止任务干扰。我们证明该方法高度模块化，兼容基于LoRA的CL方法。具体而言，它显著提升IncLoRA性能，并优于当前最先进的CL方法ELLA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of catastrophic forgetting in continual learning for large language models, this paper introduces JumpLoRA, a method that induces sparsity in LoRA adapter blocks using JumpReLU gating to dynamically isolate parameters across tasks. By reducing interference between adapters, JumpLoRA enhances task-specific learning while maintaining compatibility with existing LoRA-based continual learning frameworks. Experimental results show that JumpLoRA significantly improves performance over IncLoRA and surpasses the state-of-the-art method ELLA in continual learning benchmarks.
为解决大语言模型持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出JumpLoRA方法，通过JumpReLU门控机制在LoRA适配器块中动态诱导稀疏性，实现任务间参数隔离以减少干扰。该方法兼容现有LoRA持续学习框架，实验表明其显著提升IncLoRA性能，并在多个基准上超越当前最优方法ELL A。
CoCo-SAM3: Harnessing Concept Conflict in Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation
Authors: Yanhui Chen, Baoyao Yang, Siqi Liu, Jingchao Wang
First: 2026-04-21T16:37:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:37:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
SAM3 advances open-vocabulary semantic segmentation by introducing a prompt-driven mask generation paradigm. However, in multi-class open-vocabulary scenarios, masks generated independently from different category prompts lack a unified and inter-class comparable evidence scale, often resulting in overlapping coverage and unstable competition. Moreover, synonymous expressions of the same concept tend to activate inconsistent semantic and spatial evidence, leading to intra-class drift that exacerbates inter-class conflicts and compromises overall inference stability. To address these issues, we propose CoCo-SAM3 (Concept-Conflict SAM3), which explicitly decouples inference into intra-class enhancement and inter-class competition. Our method first aligns and aggregates evidence from synonymous prompts to strengthen concept consistency. It then performs inter-class competition on a unified comparable scale, enabling direct pixel-wise comparisons among all candidate classes. This mechanism stabilizes multi-class inference and effectively mitigates inter-class conflicts. Without requiring any additional training, CoCo-SAM3 achieves consistent improvements across eight open-vocabulary semantic segmentation benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoCo-SAM3：利用概念冲突提升开放词汇语义分割性能
SAM3通过引入基于提示的掩码生成范式推动了开放词汇语义分割的发展。但在多类别开放词汇场景中，由不同类别提示独立生成的掩码缺乏统一且可跨类比较的证据尺度，常导致覆盖重叠与竞争不稳定。此外，同一概念的同义表达往往激活不一致的语义与空间证据，引发类内漂移，加剧类间冲突并损害整体推理稳定性。为解决这些问题，我们提出CoCo-SAM3（概念冲突SAM3），显式将推理拆分为类内增强与类间竞争。该方法首先对同义提示的证据进行对齐与聚合，强化概念一致性；随后在统一可比尺度上进行类间竞争，实现所有候选类间的直接像素级比较。该机制稳定了多类别推理并有效缓解类间冲突。无需额外训练，CoCo-SAM3在八个开放词汇语义分割基准上均取得持续提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability in multi-class open-vocabulary semantic segmentation due to inconsistent evidence from synonymous prompts and lack of inter-class comparability, CoCo-SAM3 introduces a two-stage inference framework that first aligns and aggregates evidence within classes to enhance consistency, then conducts inter-class competition on a unified scale to enable direct pixel-wise comparisons. Experimental results show that this approach effectively mitigates intra-class drift and inter-class conflicts, leading to consistent performance improvements across eight benchmarks without requiring additional training.
为解决开放词汇语义分割中因同义词提示导致的证据不一致和类别间不可比性引发的推理不稳定问题，CoCo-SAM3提出两阶段推理机制：首先在类内对同义提示的证据进行对齐与聚合以增强一致性，随后在统一尺度上进行类间竞争以支持像素级直接比较。该机制无需额外训练即可稳定推理并缓解冲突，持续提升八项基准测试表现。
The signal is the ceiling: Measurement limits of LLM-predicted experience ratings from open-ended survey text
Authors: Andrew Hong, Jason Potteiger, Luis E. Zapata
First: 2026-04-21T16:34:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:34:15+00:00
Comments: 42 pages, 7 figures, 10 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An earlier paper (Hong, Potteiger, and Zapata 2026) established that an unoptimized GPT 4.1 prompt predicts fan-reported experience ratings within one point 67% of the time from open-ended survey text. This paper tests the relative impact of prompt design and model selection on that performance. We compared four configurations on approximately 10,000 post-game surveys from five MLB teams: the original baseline prompt and a moderately customized version, crossed with three GPT models (4.1, 4.1-mini, 5.2). Prompt customization added roughly two percentage points of within +/-1 agreement on GPT 4.1 (from 67% to 69%). Both model swaps from that best configuration degraded performance: GPT 5.2 returned to the baseline, and GPT 4.1-mini fell six percentage points below it. Both levers combined were dwarfed by the input itself: across capable configurations, accuracy varied more than an order of magnitude more by the linguistic character of the text than by the choice of prompt or model. The ceiling has two parts. One is a bias in how the model reads text, which prompt design can correct. The other is a difference between what fans write about and what they actually decide, which no engineering can close because the missing information is not in the text. Prompt customization moved the first part; model selection moved neither reliably. The result is not that "prompt engineering helps a little" but that prompt engineering helps in a specific and predictable way, on the part of the ceiling it can reach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：信号即天花板：从开放式调查文本中预测LLM体验评分的测量极限
先前研究（Hong, Potteiger, and Zapata 2026）表明，未经优化的GPT 4.1提示词67%的时间内能准确预测球迷在开放式调查文本中报告的体验评分。本研究测试了提示词设计与模型选择对这一表现的相对影响。我们对比了四种配置——原始基准提示词与适度定制版，分别搭配三种GPT模型（4.1、4.1-mini、5.2）——在五支MLB球队约10,000份赛后调查中的表现。提示词定制使GPT 4.1在±1分内的预测准确率提升约2个百分点（从67%至69%）。但从最佳配置切换至其他模型均导致性能下降：GPT 5.2回归基准水平，GPT 4.1-mini则下降6个百分点。提示词与模型的组合优化效果远不及输入文本本身的影响：在具备能力的配置中，文本语言特征对准确率的影响超过十倍于提示词或模型选择的影响。天花板包含两部分：一是模型解读文本的固有偏差，可通过提示词设计修正；二是球迷所写内容与实际决策之间的差异，因缺失信息不在文本中，任何工程手段均无法弥补。提示词定制仅能改善第一部分，模型选择则无法稳定改善。结论并非“提示词工程仅能略微提升效果”，而是提示词工程以可预测方式改善模型能触及的天花板部分。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how prompt design and model selection affect the accuracy of large language models in predicting fan experience ratings from open-ended survey text, building on prior work that showed GPT 4.1 could achieve 67% accuracy with a basic prompt. The researchers tested four configurations using three GPT models (4.1, 4.1-mini, 5.2) and two prompt versions (baseline and customized) across 10,000 post-game surveys. Customizing the prompt improved accuracy on GPT 4.1 from 67% to 69%, while switching to GPT 5.2 or GPT 4.1-mini degraded performance to baseline or below. Crucially, the study found that linguistic variation in survey text caused far greater accuracy fluctuations than model or prompt choices, indicating that the true ceiling of prediction is limited by inherent gaps between what fans write and what they actually experience — a gap not addressable by engineering. Prompt tuning only mitigates a portion of this ceiling, specifically the model’s bias in interpreting text, not the missing information in the input.
本研究探讨提示设计与模型选择对大语言模型从开放式调查文本中预测球迷体验评分准确性的影响。基于先前研究显示GPT 4.1可67%准确预测评分偏差在1分内，作者在10,000份赛后调查中测试了四种配置：两种提示（基准与优化版）与三种模型（GPT 4.1、4.1-mini、5.2）组合。优化提示使GPT 4.1准确率从67%提升至69%，但更换模型则导致性能下降——GPT 5.2回归基准水平，GPT 4.1-mini降至63%。关键发现是文本本身而非提示或模型选择主导了准确率的大幅波动，揭示了双重天花板：一部分源于模型对文本的固有偏差，可通过提示优化修正；另一部分源于文本未包含的球迷真实意图，工程手段无法弥补。
A Gesture-Based Visual Learning Model for Acoustophoretic Interactions using a Swarm of AcoustoBots
Authors: Alex Lin, Lei Gao, Narsimlu Kemsaram, Sriram Subramanian
First: 2026-04-21T16:32:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:32:38+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted for publication in the Proceedings of the 2026 4th International Conference on Robotics, Control and Vision Engineering (RCVE 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AcoustoBots are mobile acoustophoretic robots capable of delivering mid-air haptics, directional audio, and acoustic levitation, but existing implementations rely on scripted commands and lack an intuitive interface for real-time human control. This work presents a gesture-based visual learning framework for contactless human-swarm interaction with a multimodal AcoustoBot platform. The system combines ESP32-CAM gesture capture, PhaseSpace motion tracking, centralized processing, and an OpenCLIP-based visual learning model (VLM) with linear probing to classify three hand gestures and map them to haptics, audio, and levitation modalities. Validation accuracy improved from about 67% with a small dataset to nearly 98% with the largest dataset. In integrated experiments with two AcoustoBots, the system achieved an overall gesture-to-modality switching accuracy of 87.8% across 90 trials, with an average end-to-end latency of 3.95 seconds. These results demonstrate the feasibility of using a vision-language-model-based gesture interface for multimodal human-swarm interaction. While the current system is limited by centralized processing, a static gesture set, and controlled-environment evaluation, it establishes a foundation for more expressive, scalable, and accessible swarm robotic interfaces.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于手势视觉学习的声致悬浮机器人集群人机交互模型
AcoustoBots 是可实现空中触觉、定向音频和声悬浮的移动声致悬浮机器人，但现有系统依赖预设指令，缺乏直观的人机实时交互界面。本文提出一种基于手势视觉学习的无接触人机集群交互框架，结合 ESP32-CAM 手势捕捉、PhaseSpace 动作追踪、集中式处理与基于 OpenCLIP 的视觉学习模型（VLM）及线性探针，实现三类手势分类并映射至触觉、音频和悬浮模态。在小数据集下验证准确率约 67%，在最大数据集下提升至近 98%。在双 AcoustoBot 集成实验中，90 次试验整体手势到模态切换准确率达 87.8%，平均端到端延迟 3.95 秒。结果表明，基于视觉语言模型的手势界面可用于多模态人机集群交互。当前系统受限于集中式处理、静态手势集及受控环境评估，但仍为更富表现力、可扩展且易用的集群机器人交互界面奠定基础。

Motivated by the need for intuitive, real-time control of acoustophoretic robot swarms without scripted commands, this work introduces a gesture-based visual learning model that enables human-swarm interaction via multimodal AcoustoBots.
