为实现对声致悬浮机器人集群的直观实时控制，本研究提出一种基于手势的视觉学习模型。系统结合ESP32-CAM手势捕捉、PhaseSpace动作追踪与OpenCLIP视觉语言模型（配线性探测）实现手势分类并映射至触觉、音频与悬浮输出。实验表明，分类准确率从67%提升至近98%，在双机器人集成测试中，90次试验的指令到模态切换准确率达87.8%，平均端到端延迟3.95秒，验证了视觉语言模型在多模态集群交互中的可行性。
Micro Language Models Enable Instant Responses
Authors: Wen Cheng, Tuochao Chen, Karim Helwani, Sriram Srinivasan, Luke Zettlemoyer, Shyamnath Gollakota
First: 2026-04-21T16:31:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:31:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Edge devices such as smartwatches and smart glasses cannot continuously run even the smallest 100M-1B parameter language models due to power and compute constraints, yet cloud inference introduces multi-second latencies that break the illusion of a responsive assistant. We introduce micro language models ($μ$LMs): ultra-compact models (8M-30M parameters) that instantly generate the first 4-8 words of a contextually grounded response on-device, while a cloud model completes it; thus, masking the cloud latency. We show that useful language generation survives at this extreme scale with our models matching several 70M-256M-class existing models. We design a collaborative generation framework that reframes the cloud model as a continuator rather than a respondent, achieving seamless mid-sentence handoffs and structured graceful recovery via three error correction methods when the local opener goes wrong. Empirical results show that $μ$LMs can initiate responses that larger models complete seamlessly, demonstrating that orders-of-magnitude asymmetric collaboration is achievable and unlocking responsive AI for extremely resource-constrained devices. The model checkpoint and demo are available at https://github.com/Sensente/micro_language_model_swen_project.
中文标题/摘要
标题：微语言模型实现即时响应
由于功耗和计算能力限制，智能手表和智能眼镜等边缘设备无法持续运行即使是最小的100M-1B参数语言模型，而云端推理则引入多秒延迟，破坏了助手响应迅速的假象。我们引入微语言模型（μLMs）：超紧凑模型（8M-30M参数），可在设备端即时生成上下文相关的前4-8个词的响应，而云端模型完成剩余部分，从而掩盖云端延迟。我们证明，在这种极端规模下，语言生成仍具实用性，我们的模型性能可媲美多个70M-256M参数级别的现有模型。我们设计了一种协作生成框架，将云端模型重新定位为续写者而非回答者，实现句子中无缝交接，并通过三种错误纠正方法在本地生成部分出错时实现结构化优雅恢复。实验证明，μLMs可发起响应，由更大模型无缝完成，表明跨数量级的非对称协作是可行的，为资源极度受限的设备解锁响应式AI。模型检查点和演示可在https://github.com/Sensente/micro_language_model_swen_project获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for responsive AI on power-constrained edge devices like smartwatches, this work introduces micro language models (μLMs), ultra-compact models (8M–30M parameters) that generate the first 4–8 words of a response locally, while a cloud model completes the rest to mask latency. The method employs a collaborative generation framework where the cloud model acts as a continuator, enabling seamless mid-sentence handoffs and structured recovery via three error correction techniques when the local model fails. Experiments show that μLMs produce responses that larger models can complete without noticeable disruption, proving that asymmetric collaboration across orders of magnitude is viable and enables responsive AI on resource-limited hardware.
为解决智能手表等低功耗边缘设备无法持续运行大模型的问题，本文提出微语言模型（μLMs），参数规模仅8M–30M，能在本地瞬间生成响应的前4–8词，再由云端模型完成后续内容，从而隐藏云端延迟。方法采用协作生成框架，将云端模型视为续写者而非回答者，支持无缝句中交接，并通过三种错误纠正机制应对本地生成错误。实验表明，μLMs生成的初始内容可被大模型无缝续写，证明跨数量级异构协作可行，使资源受限设备也能实现响应式AI。
Safety-Critical Contextual Control via Online Riemannian Optimization with World Models
Authors: Tongxin Li
First: 2026-04-21T16:28:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:28:36+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern world models are becoming too complex to admit explicit dynamical descriptions. We study safety-critical contextual control, where a Planner must optimize a task objective using only feasibility samples from a black-box Simulator, conditioned on a context signal $ξ_t$. We develop a sample-based Penalized Predictive Control (PPC) framework grounded in online Riemannian optimization, in which the Simulator compresses the feasibility manifold into a score-based density $\hat{p}(u \mid ξ_t)$ that endows the action space with a Riemannian geometry guiding the Planner's gradient descent. The barrier curvature $κ(ξ_t)$, the minimum curvature of the conditional log-density $-\ln\hat{p}(\cdot\midξ_t)$, governs both convergence rate and safety margin, replacing the Lipschitz constant of the unknown dynamics. Our main result is a contextual safety bound showing that the distance from the true feasibility manifold is controlled by the score estimation error and a ratio that depends on $κ(ξ_t)$, both of which improve with richer context. Simulations on a dynamic navigation task confirm that contextual PPC substantially outperforms marginal and frozen density models, with the advantage growing after environment shifts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过在线黎曼优化与世界模型实现安全关键的上下文控制
现代世界模型过于复杂，无法显式描述动态特性。我们研究安全关键的上下文控制问题，其中规划器必须仅利用黑盒模拟器在上下文信号ξ_t条件下的可行性样本，优化任务目标。我们提出一种基于样本的惩罚预测控制（PPC）框架，其建立于在线黎曼优化之上，模拟器将可行性流形压缩为基于分数的密度估计p̂(u|ξ_t)，赋予动作空间黎曼几何结构，引导规划器进行梯度下降。屏障曲率κ(ξ_t)为条件对数密度-lnp̂(·|ξ_t)的最小曲率，同时控制收敛速率与安全裕度，替代未知动态的Lipschitz常数。主要结果为上下文安全界，表明与真实可行性流形的距离由分数估计误差及依赖κ(ξ_t)的比率决定，二者均随上下文丰富度提升而改善。动态导航任务仿真验证，上下文PPC显著优于边际与冻结密度模型，且环境变化后优势更明显。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the intractability of explicit dynamics in complex world models, this work addresses safety-critical contextual control where a planner must optimize tasks using only feasibility samples from a black-box simulator conditioned on context signals. The method, Penalized Predictive Control (PPC), leverages online Riemannian optimization by compressing feasibility manifolds into score-based densities that induce a Riemannian geometry on the action space, guiding gradient descent. The barrier curvature κ(ξ_t), derived from the conditional log-density, replaces the unknown dynamics’ Lipschitz constant and governs convergence and safety. Experimental results on dynamic navigation show that PPC achieves tighter safety bounds and superior performance compared to marginal and frozen density models, with performance gains increasing as context becomes richer.
鉴于现代世界模型难以显式建模，本文研究在黑箱模拟器下基于上下文信号进行安全关键控制的问题。方法提出一种基于在线黎曼优化的惩罚预测控制（PPC）框架，模拟器通过生成动作空间的得分密度诱导黎曼几何结构以引导梯度下降。该密度的屏障曲率 κ(ξ_t) 控制收敛速度与安全裕度，替代未知动力学的利普希茨常数。在动态导航任务中，实验表明上下文PPC显著优于边际和冻结密度模型，且环境变化后性能优势随上下文丰富度提升而增大。
SafetyALFRED: Evaluating Safety-Conscious Planning of Multimodal Large Language Models
Authors: Josue Torres-Fonseca, Naihao Deng, Yinpei Dai, Shane Storks, Yichi Zhang, Rada Mihalcea, Casey Kennington, Joyce Chai
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-21T16:27:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:27:20+00:00
Comments: Work accepted at ACL 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models are increasingly adopted as autonomous agents in interactive environments, yet their ability to proactively address safety hazards remains insufficient. We introduce SafetyALFRED, built upon the embodied agent benchmark ALFRED, augmented with six categories of real-world kitchen hazards. While existing safety evaluations focus on hazard recognition through disembodied question answering (QA) settings, we evaluate eleven state-of-the-art models from the Qwen, Gemma, and Gemini families on not only hazard recognition, but also active risk mitigation through embodied planning. Our experimental results reveal a significant alignment gap: while models can accurately recognize hazards in QA settings, average mitigation success rates for these hazards are low in comparison. Our findings demonstrate that static evaluations through QA are insufficient for physical safety, thus we advocate for a paradigm shift toward benchmarks that prioritize corrective actions in embodied contexts. We open-source our code and dataset under https://github.com/sled-group/SafetyALFRED.git
中文标题/摘要
标题：SafetyALFRED：评估多模态大语言模型的安全意识规划能力
多模态大语言模型正越来越多地被用作交互环境中的自主代理，但其主动应对安全风险的能力仍显不足。我们提出了基于具身代理基准ALFRED构建的SafetyALFRED，新增六类真实厨房安全隐患。虽然现有安全评估侧重于通过脱离身体的问答（QA）场景进行危险识别，我们则对Qwen、Gemma和Gemini系列十一款最先进模型进行评估，不仅考察其危险识别能力，更关注其通过具身规划主动进行风险缓解的能力。实验结果揭示显著的对齐差距：模型在QA场景中能准确识别危险，但在实际缓解这些危险时成功率普遍较低。我们的研究证明，仅通过QA进行静态评估不足以保障物理安全，因此我们呼吁转向更重视具身环境中纠正性行动的评估基准。我们的代码和数据集已开源于https://github.com/sled-group/SafetyALFRED.git
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess how multimodal large language models can proactively manage physical safety in embodied environments, this study introduces SafetyALFRED—a modified ALFRED benchmark enriched with six real-world kitchen hazards. It evaluates eleven state-of-the-art models from Qwen, Gemma, and Gemini families not only on hazard recognition via question answering but also on their ability to plan and mitigate risks through embodied actions. Results show that while models perform well in recognizing hazards in static QA settings, their success in actively mitigating them in embodied scenarios is significantly lower, revealing a critical gap between perception and action. The study concludes that QA-based safety evaluations are inadequate for real-world physical safety and calls for embodied benchmarks that prioritize corrective behavior.
为评估多模态大语言模型在具身环境中主动应对安全风险的能力，作者构建了SafetyALFRED基准，基于ALFRED并加入六类真实厨房安全隐患。他们对Qwen、Gemma和Gemini系列共十一款先进模型进行评估，不仅考察其在问答中识别风险的能力，更关注其在具身规划中主动规避风险的表现。实验结果表明，模型在静态问答中识别风险表现良好，但在具身环境中实际规避风险的成功率显著偏低，揭示了感知与行动之间在安全关键场景中的重大差距。
CoInteract: Physically-Consistent Human-Object Interaction Video Synthesis via Spatially-Structured Co-Generation
Authors: Xiangyang Luo, Xiaozhe Xin, Tao Feng, Xu Guo, Meiguang Jin, Junfeng Ma
First: 2026-04-21T16:25:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:25:43+00:00
Comments: The project page: https://xinxiaozhe12345.github.io/CoInteract_Project/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Synthesizing human--object interaction (HOI) videos has broad practical value in e-commerce, digital advertising, and virtual marketing. However, current diffusion models, despite their photorealistic rendering capability, still frequently fail on (i) the structural stability of sensitive regions such as hands and faces and (ii) physically plausible contact (e.g., avoiding hand--object interpenetration). We present CoInteract, an end-to-end framework for HOI video synthesis conditioned on a person reference image, a product reference image, text prompts, and speech audio. CoInteract introduces two complementary designs embedded into a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) backbone. First, we propose a Human-Aware Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) that routes tokens to lightweight, region-specialized experts via spatially supervised routing, improving fine-grained structural fidelity with minimal parameter overhead. Second, we propose Spatially-Structured Co-Generation, a dual-stream training paradigm that jointly models an RGB appearance stream and an auxiliary HOI structure stream to inject interaction geometry priors. During training, the HOI stream attends to RGB tokens and its supervision regularizes shared backbone weights; at inference, the HOI branch is removed for zero-overhead RGB generation. Experimental results demonstrate that CoInteract significantly outperforms existing methods in structural stability, logical consistency, and interaction realism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoInteract：通过空间结构化协同生成实现物理一致的人机交互视频合成
合成人机交互（HOI）视频在电子商务、数字广告和虚拟营销中具有广泛实用价值。然而，尽管当前扩散模型具备逼真的渲染能力，仍常在（i）手部和面部等敏感区域的结构稳定性，以及（ii）物理上合理的接触（如避免手与物体穿插）方面失败。我们提出CoInteract，一种基于人物参考图像、产品参考图像、文本提示和语音音频的端到端HOI视频合成框架。CoInteract在扩散Transformer（DiT）主干中嵌入两种互补设计：首先，提出一种人体感知的专家混合（MoE），通过空间监督路由将令牌分配给轻量级、区域专用专家，以最小参数开销提升细粒度结构保真度；其次，提出空间结构化协同生成，一种双流训练范式，联合建模RGB外观流与辅助HOI结构流，注入交互几何先验。训练时，HOI流关注RGB令牌，其监督调节共享主干权重；推理时移除HOI分支，实现零开销RGB生成。实验表明，CoInteract在结构稳定性、逻辑一致性和交互真实感方面显著优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically consistent human-object interaction videos in applications like e-commerce and virtual marketing, CoInteract addresses structural instability and unrealistic contact in current diffusion models. It employs a Diffusion Transformer backbone enhanced with two key innovations: a Human-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for spatially guided token routing to preserve fine-grained structural fidelity, and Spatially-Structured Co-Generation, a dual-stream training method that jointly models RGB appearance and interaction geometry to enforce physical plausibility. Experiments show CoInteract significantly improves structural stability, logical consistency, and interaction realism over existing methods without increasing inference cost.
为满足电商、虚拟营销等领域对物理一致的人机交互视频的需求，CoInteract旨在解决扩散模型在手部、面部结构稳定性及避免物体穿插方面的常见缺陷。该框架基于扩散Transformer架构，引入两项创新：一是基于空间监督的“人体感知专家混合”模块，将令牌路由至轻量级区域专用专家，提升结构细节保真度；二是“空间结构协同生成”双流训练范式，联合建模RGB外观流与交互结构流以注入几何先验。训练时结构流监督主干网络，推理时移除结构分支以实现零开销生成。实验表明，CoInteract在结构稳定性、逻辑一致性与交互真实感方面显著优于现有方法。
Towards Streaming Target Speaker Extraction via Chunk-wise Interleaved Splicing of Autoregressive Language Model
Authors: Shuhai Peng, Hui Lu, Jinjiang Liu, Liyang Chen, Guiping Zhong, Jiakui Li, Huimeng Wang, Haiyun Li, Liang Cao, Shiyin Kang, Zhiyong Wu
First: 2026-04-21T16:25:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:25:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While generative models have set new benchmarks for Target Speaker Extraction (TSE), their inherent reliance on global context precludes deployment in real-time applications. Direct adaptation to streaming scenarios often leads to catastrophic inference performance degradation due to the severe mismatch between training and streaming inference. To bridge this gap, we present the first autoregressive (AR) models tailored for streaming TSE. Our approach introduces a Chunk-wise Interleaved Splicing Paradigm that ensures highly efficient and stable streaming inference. To ensure the coherence between the extracted speech segments, we design a historical context refinement mechanism that mitigates boundary discontinuities by leveraging historical information. Experiments on Libri2Mix show that while AR generative baseline exhibits performance degradation at low latencies, our approach maintains 100% stability and superior intelligibility. Furthermore, our streaming results are comparable to or even surpass offline baselines. Additionally, our model achieves a Real-Time-Factor (RTF) of 0.248 on consumer-level GPUs. This work provides empirical evidence that AR generative backbones are viable for latency-sensitive applications through the Chunk-wise Interleaved Splicing Paradigm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过自回归语言模型的分块交错拼接实现流式目标说话人提取
尽管生成式模型在目标说话人提取（TSE）中设定了新基准，但其对全局上下文的固有依赖限制了其在实时应用中的部署。直接适配流式场景常因训练与流式推理之间的严重不匹配导致推理性能急剧下降。为弥合此差距，我们首次提出专为流式TSE设计的自回归（AR）模型。我们的方法引入分块交错拼接范式，确保流式推理的高效与稳定。为保证提取语音片段的连贯性，我们设计历史上下文优化机制，通过利用历史信息缓解边界不连续性。在Libri2Mix数据集上的实验表明，虽然AR生成式基线在低延迟下性能下降，但我们的方法保持100%稳定性且语音可懂度更优。此外，我们的流式结果与或优于离线基线。同时，模型在消费级GPU上实现0.248的实时因子（RTF）。本工作通过分块交错拼接范式，提供了AR生成式骨干网络适用于低延迟应用的实证证据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time target speaker extraction without sacrificing quality, this work introduces the first autoregressive models specifically designed for streaming scenarios. It employs a Chunk-wise Interleaved Splicing Paradigm to enable efficient and stable inference by processing audio in overlapping chunks, while a historical context refinement mechanism ensures speech coherence across boundaries. Experiments on Libri2Mix demonstrate that while standard autoregressive models degrade significantly at low latencies, this method maintains 100% stability and superior intelligibility, matching or exceeding offline baselines, with a Real-Time-Factor of 0.248 on consumer GPUs.
为实现低延迟下的高质量目标说话人提取，本文首次提出面向流式场景的自回归模型。该方法采用分块交错拼接范式，在保持音频流连续性的同时通过历史上下文精炼机制缓解边界不连续问题。实验表明，相比传统自回归模型在低延迟下性能急剧下降，本方法在Libri2Mix数据集上保持100%稳定性与更优可懂度，性能可媲美或超越离线基线，且在消费级GPU上实现实时因子0.248。
Time Series Augmented Generation for Financial Applications
Authors: Anton Kolonin, Alexey Glushchenko, Evgeny Bochkov, Abhishek Saxena
First: 2026-04-21T16:20:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:20:59+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) for complex, quantitative financial tasks is a critical and unsolved challenge. Standard benchmarks often fail to isolate an agent's core ability to parse queries and orchestrate computations. To address this, we introduce a novel evaluation methodology and benchmark designed to rigorously measure an LLM agent's reasoning for financial time-series analysis. We apply this methodology in a large-scale empirical study using our framework, Time Series Augmented Generation (TSAG), where an LLM agent delegates quantitative tasks to verifiable, external tools. Our benchmark, consisting of 100 financial questions, is used to compare multiple SOTA agents (e.g., GPT-4o, Llama 3, Qwen2) on metrics assessing tool selection accuracy, faithfulness, and hallucination. The results demonstrate that capable agents can achieve near-perfect tool-use accuracy with minimal hallucination, validating the tool-augmented paradigm. Our primary contribution is this evaluation framework and the corresponding empirical insights into agent performance, which we release publicly to foster standardized research on reliable financial AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向金融应用的时间序列增强生成
评估大型语言模型（LLM）在复杂定量金融任务中的推理能力是一个关键且尚未解决的挑战。标准基准测试往往无法隔离代理解析查询和协调计算的核心能力。为此，我们提出了一种新颖的评估方法和基准，旨在严格衡量LLM代理在金融时间序列分析中的推理能力。我们使用我们的框架——时间序列增强生成（TSAG）——在大规模实证研究中应用该方法，其中LLM代理将定量任务委托给可验证的外部工具。我们的基准包含100个金融问题，用于比较多个当前最先进的代理（如GPT-4o、Llama 3、Qwen2）在工具选择准确性、忠实度和幻觉方面的表现。结果表明，有能力的代理可实现近乎完美的工具使用准确性并最小化幻觉，验证了工具增强范式。我们的主要贡献是该评估框架及相应的代理性能实证洞察，我们公开发布以促进可靠金融AI的标准化研究。

Using a 100-question benchmark, it compares state-of-the-art models like GPT-4o and Llama 3 on metrics including tool selection accuracy, faithfulness, and hallucination rates. Results show top agents achieve near-perfect tool usage with minimal hallucinations, validating the tool-augmented approach and offering empirical insights for future financial AI research.
为评估大语言模型在复杂金融任务中的推理能力，本文提出Time Series Augmented Generation（TSAG）框架，使模型能调用外部可验证工具完成计算。作者构建包含100道金融问题的基准测试，评估多个顶尖模型（如GPT-4o、Llama 3）在工具选择准确性、忠实度和幻觉方面的表现。实验结果表明，表现优异的模型可实现近乎完美的工具调用准确率且幻觉极少，验证了工具增强范式在金融推理中的有效性。
CreatiParser: Generative Image Parsing of Raster Graphic Designs into Editable Layers
Authors: Weidong Chen, Dexiang Hong, Zhendong Mao, Yutao Cheng, Xinyan Liu, Lei Zhang, Yongdong Zhang
First: 2026-04-21T16:20:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:20:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graphic design images consist of multiple editable layers, such as text, background, and decorative elements, while most generative models produce rasterized outputs without explicit layer structures, limiting downstream editing. Existing graphic design parsing methods typically rely on multi-stage pipelines combining layout prediction, matting, and inpainting, which suffer from error accumulation and limited controllability. We propose a hybrid generative framework for raster-to-layer graphic design parsing that decomposes a design image into editable text, background, and sticker layers. Text regions are parsed using a vision-language model into a text rendering protocol, enabling faithful reconstruction and flexible re-editing, while background and sticker layers are generated using a multi-branch diffusion architecture with RGBA support. We further introduce ParserReward and integrate it with Group Relative Policy Optimization to align generation quality with human design preferences. Extensive experiments on two challenging datasets, \emph{i.e.,} the Parser-40K and Crello datasets, demonstrate superior performance over existing methods, \emph{eg.,} achieving an overall average improvement of 23.7\% across all metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CreatiParser：将位图图形设计生成可编辑图层
图形设计图像由多个可编辑图层组成，如文本、背景和装饰元素，而大多数生成模型输出的是无明确图层结构的位图，限制了下游编辑。现有图形设计解析方法通常依赖于结合布局预测、抠图和修复的多阶段流程，存在误差累积和控制性有限的问题。我们提出了一种混合生成框架，用于将位图图形设计解析为可编辑的文本、背景和贴纸图层。文本区域通过视觉-语言模型解析为文本渲染协议，实现忠实重建和灵活重编辑；背景和贴纸图层则通过支持RGBA的多分支扩散架构生成。我们进一步引入ParserReward，并结合组相对策略优化，使生成质量与人类设计偏好对齐。在Parser-40K和Crello两个具有挑战性的数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在各项指标上均优于现有方法，平均提升23.7%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable editable layer extraction from raster graphic designs for downstream editing, the authors propose CreatiParser, a hybrid generative framework that decomposes design images into text, background, and sticker layers. Text regions are parsed via a vision-language model into a renderable protocol for faithful reconstruction and re-editing, while background and sticker layers are generated using a multi-branch diffusion model supporting RGBA. The method integrates ParserReward with Group Relative Policy Optimization to align outputs with human design preferences. Experiments on Parser-40K and Crello datasets show a 23.7% average improvement over existing methods across multiple evaluation metrics.
为支持对位图图形设计的下游编辑，作者提出CreatiParser框架，将设计图像分解为文本、背景和贴纸层。文本区域通过视觉-语言模型解析为文本渲染协议，实现准确重建与灵活重编辑；背景和贴纸层则通过支持RGBA的多分支扩散模型生成。方法引入ParserReward并结合Group Relative Policy Optimization以对齐生成结果与人类设计偏好。在Parser-40K和Crello两个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在各项指标上平均优于现有方法23.7%。
MOSA: Motion-Guided Semantic Alignment for Dynamic Scene Graph Generation
Authors: Xuejiao Wang, Bohao Zhang, Changbo Wang, Gaoqi He
First: 2026-04-21T16:18:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:18:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dynamic Scene Graph Generation (DSGG) aims to structurally model objects and their dynamic interactions in video sequences for high-level semantic understanding. However, existing methods struggle with fine-grained relationship modeling, semantic representation utilization, and the ability to model tail relationships. To address these issues, this paper proposes a motion-guided semantic alignment method for DSGG (MoSA). First, a Motion Feature Extractor (MFE) encodes object-pair motion attributes such as distance, velocity, motion persistence, and directional consistency. Then, these motion attributes are fused with spatial relationship features through the Motion-guided Interaction Module (MIM) to generate motion-aware relationship representations. To further enhance semantic discrimination capabilities, the cross-modal Action Semantic Matching (ASM) mechanism aligns visual relationship features with text embeddings of relationship categories. Finally, a category-weighted loss strategy is introduced to emphasize learning of tail relationships. Extensive and rigorous testing shows that MoSA performs optimally on the Action Genome dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MOSA：用于动态场景图生成的运动引导语义对齐
动态场景图生成（DSGG）旨在对视频序列中的物体及其动态交互进行结构化建模，以实现高层次语义理解。然而，现有方法在细粒度关系建模、语义表示利用及尾部关系建模方面存在不足。为此，本文提出一种面向DSGG的运动引导语义对齐方法（MoSA）。首先，运动特征提取器（MFE）编码物体对的运动属性，如距离、速度、运动持续性及方向一致性；随后，通过运动引导交互模块（MIM）将这些运动属性与空间关系特征融合，生成运动感知的关系表示。为进一步增强语义判别能力，跨模态动作语义匹配（ASM）机制将视觉关系特征与关系类别文本嵌入对齐。最后，引入类别加权损失策略以强化尾部关系的学习。大量严谨实验表明，MoSA在Action Genome数据集上表现最优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing methods in modeling fine-grained relationships and tail interactions in dynamic scenes, this paper introduces MoSA, a motion-guided semantic alignment framework for Dynamic Scene Graph Generation. It first extracts motion features like distance, velocity, and directional consistency using a Motion Feature Extractor, then fuses these with spatial relationships via a Motion-guided Interaction Module to create motion-aware relationship representations. To improve semantic discrimination, it employs a cross-modal Action Semantic Matching mechanism to align visual relationships with textual relationship embeddings, and applies a category-weighted loss to prioritize learning of rare relationships. Experimental results demonstrate that MoSA achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Action Genome dataset.
为解决现有方法在动态场景中细粒度关系建模和尾部关系学习上的不足，本文提出MoSA框架，通过运动引导语义对齐提升动态场景图生成效果。该框架首先利用运动特征提取器获取物体对的运动属性（如距离、速度等），再通过运动引导交互模块将其与空间关系特征融合，生成运动感知的关系表示。为进一步增强语义区分能力，引入跨模态动作语义匹配机制对视觉关系与文本关系词嵌入进行对齐，并采用类别加权损失强化尾部关系学习。实验表明，MoSA在Action Genome数据集上达到最优性能。
GRAFT: Geometric Refinement and Fitting Transformer for Human Scene Reconstruction
Authors: Pradyumna YM, Yuxuan Xue, Yue Chen, Nikita Kister, István Sárándi, Gerard Pons-Moll
First: 2026-04-21T16:13:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:13:15+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://pradyumnaym.github.io/graft
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reconstructing physically plausible 3D human-scene interactions (HSI) from a single image currently presents a trade-off: optimization based methods offer accurate contact but are slow (~20s), while feed-forward approaches are fast yet lack explicit interaction reasoning, producing floating and interpenetration artifacts. Our key insight is that geometry-based human--scene fitting can be amortized into fast feed-forward inference. We present GRAFT (Geometric Refinement And Fitting Transformer), a learned HSI prior that predicts Interaction Gradients: corrective parameter updates that iteratively refine human meshes by reasoning about their 3D relationship to the surrounding scene. GRAFT encodes the interaction state into compact body-anchored tokens, each grounded in the scene geometry via Geometric Probes that capture spatial relationships with nearby surfaces. A lightweight transformer recurrently updates human meshes and re-probes the scene, ensuring the final pose aligns with both learned priors and observed geometry. GRAFT operates either as an end-to-end reconstructor using image features, or with geometry alone as a transferable plug-and-play HSI prior that improves feed-forward methods without retraining. Experiments show GRAFT improves interaction quality by up to 113% over state-of-the-art feed-forward methods and matches optimization-based interaction quality at ${\sim}50{\times}$ lower runtime, while generalizing seamlessly to in-the-wild multi-person scenes and being preferred in 64.8% of three-way user study. Project page: https://pradyumnaym.github.io/graft .
中文标题/摘要
标题：GRAFT：用于人体场景重建的几何细化与拟合变换器
从单张图像重建物理上合理的三维人体-场景交互（HSI）目前存在权衡：基于优化的方法虽接触准确但速度慢（约20秒），而前馈方法虽快速却缺乏显式交互推理，易产生漂浮和穿插伪影。 我们的核心见解是：基于几何的人体-场景拟合可被转化为快速前馈推理。我们提出了GRAFT（几何细化与拟合变换器），一种学习到的HSI先验，可预测交互梯度：通过推理人体与周围场景的三维关系，迭代性地修正人体网格参数。 GRAFT将交互状态编码为紧凑的、以身体为中心的令牌，每个令牌通过几何探针与场景几何关联，捕捉与邻近表面的空间关系。 轻量级变换器反复更新人体网格并重新探测场景，确保最终姿态同时符合学习到的先验和观测到的几何结构。 GRAFT可作为端到端重建器使用图像特征，或仅使用几何结构作为可迁移的即插即用HSI先验，无需再训练即可提升前馈方法的性能。 实验表明，GRAFT相较最先进的前馈方法交互质量提升最高达113%，在约50倍更低的运行时间下达到基于优化方法的交互质量，且无缝泛化至野外多人场景，并在三方用户研究中被64.8%的用户偏好。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between slow optimization-based methods and fast but inaccurate feed-forward approaches in human-scene reconstruction, GRAFT introduces a learned transformer that amortizes geometric fitting into fast inference. It predicts Interaction Gradients—iterative mesh updates grounded in scene geometry via Geometric Probes that encode spatial relationships into body-anchored tokens. A lightweight transformer recurrently refines human meshes and re-probes the scene, ensuring alignment with both learned priors and observed geometry. Experiments show GRAFT improves interaction quality by up to 113% over state-of-the-art feed-forward methods and achieves optimization-level accuracy at ~50x lower runtime, while generalizing well to multi-person scenes and being preferred in 64.8% of user studies.
为解决人类场景重建中优化方法慢而前馈方法精度不足的问题，GRAFT提出一种学习型变换器，将几何拟合过程转化为快速推理。它通过几何探针将人体姿态与场景几何关系编码为体锚定令牌，并预测交互梯度以迭代优化人体网格。轻量级变换器反复更新姿态并重新探测场景，确保结果符合先验和观测几何。实验表明，GRAFT相较前馈方法交互质量提升最高达113%，在约50倍更快的速度下达到优化方法精度，且能良好泛化至多人场景，并在64.8%的用户测试中被优先选择。
Bridging the High-Frequency Data Gap: A Millisecond-Resolution Network Dataset for Advancing Time Series Foundation Models
Authors: Subina Khanal, Seshu Tirupathi, Merim Dzaferagic, Marco Ruffini, Torben Bach Pedersen
First: 2026-03-17T13:23:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:12:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time series foundation models (TSFMs) require diverse, real-world datasets to adapt across varying domains and temporal frequencies. However, current large-scale datasets predominantly focus on low-frequency time series with sampling intervals, i.e., time resolution, in the range of seconds to years, hindering their ability to capture the nuances of high-frequency time series data. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel dataset that captures millisecond-resolution wireless and traffic conditions from an operational 5G wireless deployment, expanding the scope of TSFMs to incorporate high-frequency data for pre-training. Further, the dataset introduces a new domain, wireless networks, thus complementing existing more general domains like energy and finance. The dataset also provides use cases for short-term forecasting, with prediction horizons spanning from 1 millisecond (1 step) to 96 milliseconds (96 steps). By benchmarking traditional machine learning models and TSFMs on predictive tasks using this dataset, we demonstrate that most TSFM model configurations perform poorly on this new data distribution in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings. Our work underscores the importance of incorporating high-frequency datasets during pre-training and forecasting to enhance architectures, fine-tuning strategies, generalization, and robustness of TSFMs in real-world applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：弥合高频数据鸿沟：一种用于推进时间序列基础模型的毫秒级分辨率网络数据集
时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）需要多样化的现实世界数据集以适应不同领域和时间频率。然而，当前大规模数据集主要关注低频时间序列，采样间隔（即时间分辨率）在秒至年之间，限制了其捕捉高频时间序列数据细微差异的能力。为解决这一局限，我们引入了一种新型数据集，该数据集从实际部署的5G无线网络中捕获毫秒级分辨率的无线和交通状况，扩展了TSFMs的适用范围，使其能够利用高频数据进行预训练。此外，该数据集引入了新的领域——无线网络，从而补充了现有更通用的领域如能源和金融。该数据集还提供了短期预测用例，预测时间跨度从1毫秒（1步）到96毫秒（96步）。通过在该数据集上对传统机器学习模型和TSFMs进行预测任务基准测试，我们证明了大多数TSFM模型配置在零样本和微调设置下对该新数据分布表现不佳。我们的工作强调了在预训练和预测中纳入高频数据集的重要性，以提升TSFMs在现实应用中的架构、微调策略、泛化能力和鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of high-frequency time series data in existing datasets, which limits the performance of time series foundation models (TSFMs), this work introduces a novel millisecond-resolution dataset derived from operational 5G wireless networks, expanding TSFM training to include high-frequency dynamics and a new domain. The dataset enables short-term forecasting tasks with prediction horizons from 1 to 96 milliseconds. Experimental results show that most TSFM configurations underperform on this dataset in both zero-shot and fine-tuned settings, highlighting the need for high-frequency data in pre-training to improve model generalization and robustness in real-world applications.
为解决时序基础模型训练中缺乏高频数据的问题，本文提出一个源自实际5G无线网络的毫秒级分辨率新数据集，拓展了传统能源与金融等领域的覆盖范围。该数据集支持从1毫秒到96毫秒的短期预测任务，实验表明多数现有时序基础模型在该数据分布上零样本或微调后表现不佳，凸显在预训练阶段引入高频数据对提升模型泛化能力、鲁棒性及实际应用价值的重要性。
SAGE: Training-Free Semantic Evidence Composition for Edge-Cloud Inference under Hard Uplink Budgets
Authors: Inhyeok Choi, Hyuncheol Park
First: 2026-04-21T16:11:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:11:56+00:00
Comments: 11pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Edge-cloud hybrid inference offloads difficult inputs to a powerful remote model, but the uplink channel imposes hard per-request constraints on the number of bits that can be transmitted. We show that selecting transmitted content based solely on attention-based importance, the standard approach in collaborative inference, is inherently limited under hard budgets. Two findings support this claim. First, replacing high-importance units with low-importance but complementary ones improves server accuracy. This shows that what matters is not individual importance but how well the transmitted set covers diverse aspects of the input. Second, spatially uniform selection without any content information achieves competitive accuracy at moderate budgets. This confirms that spatial coverage alone carries independent value. Based on this analysis, we propose SAGE (Semantic Attention-Guided Evidence), a principled, training-free method that combines importance filtering with embedding-diversity sampling. SAGE achieves 93% of the server ceiling in offloaded accuracy while transmitting fewer than half of the available evidence units on ImageNet-1K, substantially outperforming importance-only composition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAGE：面向硬上行预算边缘-云推理的无训练语义证据组合
边缘-云混合推理将复杂输入卸载至远程强大模型，但上行信道对每请求传输比特数施加严格限制。我们表明，仅依据注意力机制重要性选择传输内容（协作推理的标准方法）在硬预算下存在固有局限。两项发现支持此观点：首先，用低重要性但互补的单元替代高重要性单元可提升服务器精度，表明关键在于传输集合对输入多样方面的覆盖能力，而非单个单元重要性；其次，无内容信息的均匀空间选择在中等预算下即可获得竞争力精度，证实空间覆盖本身具有独立价值。基于此分析，我们提出SAGE（语义注意力引导证据），一种无训练、原理化的组合方法，结合重要性过滤与嵌入多样性采样。SAGE在ImageNet-1K上仅传输少于一半可用证据单元，即可实现卸载精度达服务器上限的93%，显著优于仅重要性组合方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of transmitting limited data over constrained uplink channels in edge-cloud inference, the paper argues that attention-based importance filtering alone is insufficient under hard budget constraints. It introduces SAGE, a training-free method that combines importance-based selection with embedding-diversity sampling to ensure both semantic relevance and spatial coverage. Experiments on ImageNet-1K show SAGE achieves 93% of the server’s maximum accuracy while transmitting fewer than half the available evidence units, significantly outperforming importance-only baselines.
为应对边缘-云推理中受限上行链路带宽带来的传输挑战，论文指出仅依赖注意力机制选择重要性内容的方法在硬预算下存在局限。实验证明，用低重要性但互补的单元替换高重要性冗余单元可提升服务器精度，且无内容信息的均匀空间采样在中等预算下仍能获得竞争力的结果。基于此，论文提出SAGE方法——一种结合重要性筛选与嵌入多样性采样的无训练方案，在ImageNet-1K上仅传输少于一半的证据单元即达到服务器精度的93%，显著优于仅依赖重要性的基线方法。
EviSearch: A Human in the Loop System for Extracting and Auditing Clinical Evidence for Systematic Reviews
Authors: Naman Ahuja, Saniya Mulla, Muhammad Ali Khan, Zaryab Bin Riaz, Kaneez Zahra Rubab Khakwani, Mohamad Bassam Sonbol, Irbaz Bin Riaz, Vivek Gupta
First: 2026-03-23T22:36:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:11:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present EviSearch, a multi-agent extraction system that automates the creation of ontology-aligned clinical evidence tables directly from native trial PDFs while guaranteeing per-cell provenance for audit and human verification. EviSearch pairs a PDF-query agent (which preserves rendered layout and figures) with a retrieval-guided search agent and a reconciliation module that forces page-level verification when agents disagree. The pipeline is designed for high-precision extraction across multimodal evidence sources (text, tables, figures) and for generating reviewer-actionable provenance that clinicians can inspect and correct. On a clinician-curated benchmark of oncology trial papers, EviSearch substantially improves extraction accuracy relative to strong parsed-text baselines while providing comprehensive attribution coverage. By logging reconciler decisions and reviewer edits, the system produces structured preference and supervision signals that bootstrap iterative model improvement. EviSearch is intended to accelerate living systematic review workflows, reduce manual curation burden, and provide a safe, auditable path for integrating LLM-based extraction into evidence synthesis pipelines.

The "Small World of Words" German Free-Association Norms
Authors: Samuel Aeschbach, Rui Mata, Kaidi Lõo, Simon De Deyne, Dirk U. Wulff
First: 2026-04-21T16:07:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:07:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Free-association norms provide essential empirical data for investigating linguistic, semantic, and cultural phenomena in the cognitive sciences. Although large-scale norms exist for languages such as English, Dutch, Spanish, and Mandarin Chinese, no comparable resource has been available for German. To address this gap, we present free-association norms for 5,877 German cue words as part of the German version of the multilingual Small World of Words (SWOW) project. We describe the data collection procedures, participant characteristics, and our comprehensive preprocessing pipeline before introducing the resulting SWOW-DE data set. Using data from three established psycholinguistic paradigms, we show that SWOW-DE norms robustly predict performance in lexical decision tasks, relatedness judgments, and psycholinguistic word ratings. Furthermore, we demonstrate that SWOW-DE responses compare favorably with existing German resources and provide a preliminary cross-linguistic comparison revealing both shared and language-specific association patterns, highlighting promising directions for future research. Overall, SWOW-DE represents the largest collection of German free associations to date and offers a unique resource for linguistic, psychological, and cross-cultural research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：“词语的小世界”德语自由联想规范
自由联想规范为认知科学中研究语言、语义和文化现象提供了重要的实证数据。尽管英语、荷兰语、西班牙语和普通话等语言已有大规模规范资源，但德语尚无类似资源。为填补这一空白，我们作为多语言“词语的小世界”（SWOW）项目德语版的一部分，提供了5,877个德语提示词的自由联想规范。我们描述了数据收集流程、参与者特征及全面的数据预处理管道，随后介绍了所得的SWOW-DE数据集。通过三个已确立的心理语言学范式的数据，我们表明SWOW-DE规范能稳健预测词汇决策任务、相关性判断及心理语言学词汇评分的表现。此外，我们证明SWOW-DE的响应与现有德语资源相比表现优异，并提供了初步的跨语言比较，揭示了共同及语言特异性的联想模式，指明了未来研究的有前景方向。总体而言，SWOW-DE是迄今为止最大的德语自由联想语料库，为语言学、心理学及跨文化研究提供了独特资源。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of large-scale German free-association norms for cognitive science research, this study collected and processed free-association responses for 5,877 German cue words as part of the SWOW-DE dataset. The data was gathered through controlled experiments with native German speakers and underwent rigorous preprocessing. The resulting norms were validated across three psycholinguistic tasks—lexical decision, relatedness judgment, and word ratings—and demonstrated strong predictive power. SWOW-DE also outperformed existing German resources and revealed both universal and language-specific association patterns when compared across languages, establishing it as the most comprehensive German free-association dataset to date.
为填补认知科学领域德语自由联想规范数据的空白，本研究构建了包含5877个德语提示词的SWOW-DE数据集。数据通过针对德语母语者的实验收集，并经过严格预处理。该数据集在三项心理语言学任务（词汇判断、相关性判断和词汇评分）中表现优异，预测能力显著。SWOW-DE优于现有德语资源，并在跨语言比较中揭示了共性与语言特异性联想模式，成为目前最全面的德语自由联想语料库。
Memorize When Needed: Decoupled Memory Control for Spatially Consistent Long-Horizon Video Generation
Authors: Yanjun Guo, Zhengqiang Zhang, Pengfei Wang, Xinyue Liang, Zhiyuan Ma, Lei Zhang
First: 2026-04-20T13:00:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:05:42+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, with supplementary material
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spatially consistent long-horizon video generation aims to maintain temporal and spatial consistency along predefined camera trajectories. Existing methods mostly entangle memory modeling with video generation, leading to inconsistent content during scene revisits and diminished generative capacity when exploring novel regions, even trained on extensive annotated data. To address these limitations, we propose a decoupled framework that separates memory conditioning from generation. Our approach significantly reduces training costs while simultaneously enhancing spatial consistency and preserving the generative capacity for novel scene exploration. Specifically, we employ a lightweight, independent memory branch to learn precise spatial consistency from historical observation. We first introduce a hybrid memory representation to capture complementary temporal and spatial cues from generated frames, then leverage a per-frame cross-attention mechanism to ensure each frame is conditioned exclusively on the most spatially relevant historical information, which is injected into the generative model to ensure spatial consistency. When generating new scenes, a camera-aware gating mechanism is proposed to mediate the interaction between memory and generation modules, enabling memory conditioning only when meaningful historical references exist. Compared with the existing method, our method is highly data-efficient, yet the experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in terms of both visual quality and spatial consistency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：按需记忆：用于空间一致长时程视频生成的解耦记忆控制
空间一致长时程视频生成旨在沿预定义的相机轨迹保持时间和空间一致性。现有方法大多将记忆建模与视频生成耦合，导致场景重访时内容不一致，且在探索新区域时生成能力下降，即使在大量标注数据上训练也是如此。为解决这些局限，我们提出一种解耦框架，将记忆条件与生成分离。该方法显著降低训练成本，同时提升空间一致性并保留探索新场景的生成能力。具体而言，我们采用轻量级独立记忆分支，从历史观测中学习精确的空间一致性；首先引入混合记忆表示，捕捉生成帧中的互补时序与空间线索，再利用逐帧交叉注意力机制，确保每帧仅被最相关的历史信息条件化，该信息注入生成模型以保证空间一致性。在生成新场景时，提出一种相机感知门控机制，调节记忆与生成模块的交互，仅在存在有意义历史参考时启用记忆条件化。与现有方法相比，我们的方法数据效率高，实验表明其在视觉质量和空间一致性方面均达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
To address spatial inconsistency and reduced generative capacity in long-horizon video generation, this paper proposes a decoupled memory control framework that separates memory conditioning from the generative process. It employs a lightweight memory branch to learn spatial consistency from historical frames using a hybrid memory representation that combines temporal and spatial cues, and a per-frame cross-attention mechanism to condition each frame on the most relevant past information. A camera-aware gating mechanism further restricts memory influence to only when meaningful historical context is available, preserving generative freedom for novel scenes. Experiments show the method achieves state-of-the-art visual quality and spatial consistency while being more data-efficient and reducing training costs compared to existing approaches.
为解决长时程视频生成中空间一致性差和新场景生成能力弱的问题，本文提出一种解耦的记忆控制框架，将记忆建模与生成过程分离。该方法采用轻量级记忆分支，通过混合记忆表示学习历史帧中的时空线索，并利用逐帧交叉注意力机制，仅将最相关的过去信息注入生成器以确保空间一致性。引入相机感知门控机制，仅在存在有意义历史参考时才启用记忆，从而保留对新场景的生成能力。实验表明，该方法在视觉质量和空间一致性上达到当前最优，同时更节省数据并降低训练成本。
Autonomous UAV Pipeline Near-proximity Inspection via Disturbance-Aware Predictive Visual Servoing
Authors: Wen Li, Hui Wang, Jinya Su, Cunjia Liu, Wen-Hua Chen, Shihua Li
First: 2026-04-21T16:05:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:05:34+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 12 figures, Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable pipeline inspection is critical to safe energy transportation, but is constrained by long distances, complex terrain, and risks to human inspectors. Unmanned aerial vehicles provide a flexible sensing platform, yet reliable autonomous inspection remains challenging. This paper presents an autonomous quadrotor near-proximity pipeline inspection framework for three-dimensional scenarios based on image-based visual servoing model predictive control (VMPC). A unified predictive model couples quadrotor dynamics with image feature kinematics, enabling direct image-space prediction within the control loop. To address low-rate visual updates, measurement noise, and environmental uncertainties, an extended-state Kalman filtering scheme with image feature prediction (ESKF-PRE) is developed, and the estimated lumped disturbances are incorporated into the VMPC prediction model, yielding the ESKF-PRE-VMPC framework. A terrain-adaptive velocity design is introduced to maintain the desired cruising speed while generating vertical velocity references over unknown terrain slopes without prior terrain information. The framework is validated in high-fidelity Gazebo simulations and real-world experiments. In real-world tests, the proposed method reduces RMSE by 52.63% and 75.04% in pipeline orientation and lateral deviation in the image, respectively, for straight-pipeline inspection without wind, and successfully completes both wind-disturbance and bend-pipeline tasks where baseline method fails. An open-source nano quadrotor is modified for indoor experimentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于扰动感知预测视觉伺服的自主无人机近距管道巡检
可靠的管道巡检对安全能源运输至关重要，但受限于长距离、复杂地形及对人工巡检员的风险。无人机提供了灵活的感知平台，但可靠的自主巡检仍具挑战。本文提出一种基于图像视觉伺服模型预测控制（VMPC）的自主四旋翼无人机近距三维管道巡检框架。统一预测模型耦合四旋翼动力学与图像特征运动学，实现控制环内直接图像空间预测。为应对低频视觉更新、测量噪声及环境不确定性，开发了扩展状态卡尔曼滤波与图像特征预测（ESKF-PRE）方案，并将估计的总扰动纳入VMPC预测模型，形成ESKF-PRE-VMPC框架。引入地形自适应速度设计，在无先验地形信息情况下维持期望巡航速度，同时生成未知坡度地形的垂直速度指令。该框架在高保真Gazebo仿真与真实实验中验证。真实测试中，所提方法在无风直管巡检中，管道方向与图像横向偏差的RMSE分别降低52.63%和75.04%，并成功完成基线方法失败的风扰动及弯管巡检任务。采用开源纳米四旋翼无人机进行室内实验。
Summary / 总结
To enable safe and reliable autonomous inspection of pipelines in complex 3D environments, this paper proposes a disturbance-aware predictive visual servoing framework for quadrotors. The method integrates a unified predictive model that couples quadrotor dynamics with image feature kinematics, allowing direct image-space control within a model predictive control (VMPC) loop. To handle low visual update rates, measurement noise, and environmental disturbances, an extended-state Kalman filter with image feature prediction (ESKF-PRE) estimates lumped disturbances and feeds them into the VMPC model. A terrain-adaptive velocity strategy ensures stable cruising speed over unknown slopes without prior terrain data. Experimental results in Gazebo simulations and real-world tests show the method reduces image-based pipeline orientation RMSE by 52.63% and lateral deviation by 75.04% compared to baseline, and successfully completes challenging tasks including wind-disturbance and curved-pipeline inspections where the baseline fails.
为实现复杂三维环境中管道的自主可靠近距检测，本文提出一种基于扰动感知的预测视觉伺服框架。该方法构建了耦合飞行器动力学与图像特征运动学的统一预测模型，使控制环路可直接在图像空间进行预测。为应对视觉更新率低、测量噪声及环境不确定性，设计了扩展状态卡尔曼滤波器结合图像特征预测（ESKF-PRE），将估计的总扰动纳入模型预测控制（VMPC），形成ESKF-PRE-VMPC框架。引入地形自适应速度设计，可在未知坡度地形上维持稳定巡航速度而无需地形先验。仿真与真实实验表明，该方法在无风直管检测中，图像空间管道姿态误差降低52.63%，横向偏差降低75.04%，并在风扰动和弯管检测等基线方法失败的任务中成功完成。
Can AI-Generated Persuasion Be Detected? Persuaficial Benchmark and AI vs. Human Linguistic Differences
Authors: Arkadiusz Modzelewski, Paweł Golik, Anna Kołos, Giovanni Da San Martino
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-08T13:22:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:05:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate highly persuasive text, raising concerns about their misuse for propaganda, manipulation, and other harmful purposes. This leads us to our central question: Is LLM-generated persuasion more difficult to automatically detect than human-written persuasion? To address this, we categorize controllable generation approaches for producing persuasive content with LLMs and introduce Persuaficial, a high-quality multilingual benchmark covering six languages: English, German, Polish, Italian, French and Russian. Using this benchmark, we conduct extensive empirical evaluations comparing human-authored and LLM-generated persuasive texts. We find that although overtly persuasive LLM-generated texts can be easier to detect than human-written ones, subtle LLM-generated persuasion consistently degrades automatic detection performance. Beyond detection performance, we provide the first comprehensive linguistic analysis contrasting human and LLM-generated persuasive texts, offering insights that may guide the development of more interpretable and robust detection tools.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI生成的说服性内容能被检测吗？Persuaficial基准与AI与人类语言差异研究
大型语言模型（LLMs）可生成高度有说服力的文本，引发对其被用于宣传、操纵等有害目的的担忧。由此我们提出核心问题：LLM生成的说服性内容是否比人类撰写的更难自动检测？为此，我们对使用LLM生成说服性内容的可控生成方法进行分类，并引入覆盖六种语言（英语、德语、波兰语、意大利语、法语和俄语）的高质量多语言基准Persuaficial。基于该基准，我们对人类撰写与LLM生成的说服性文本进行了广泛实证评估。结果发现，虽然明显具有说服力的LLM生成文本可能比人类写作的更易检测，但微妙的LLM生成说服性内容会持续降低自动检测性能。此外，我们首次对人类与LLM生成的说服性文本进行了全面的语言学分析，为开发更可解释、更稳健的检测工具提供洞见。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns that AI-generated persuasive content could be misused for manipulation, this study investigates whether LLM-produced persuasion is harder to detect than human-written persuasion. The authors introduce Persuaficial, a multilingual benchmark covering six languages, and compare human-authored and LLM-generated persuasive texts using controlled generation methods. Results show that overtly persuasive LLM texts are often easier to detect, but subtle LLM-generated persuasion consistently reduces detection accuracy. The study also provides the first comprehensive linguistic comparison, revealing key differences that could inform the design of more robust detection systems.
鉴于AI生成的说服性内容可能被用于操纵和传播有害信息，本研究探讨了由大语言模型生成的说服性文本是否比人类写作更难被自动检测。研究构建了覆盖六种语言的Persuaficial多语言基准，并通过大量实证评估对比人类与AI生成的说服性文本。结果表明，明显具有说服力的AI文本往往更容易被检测，但微妙的AI说服性内容则持续降低检测准确率。研究还首次系统分析了人类与AI生成说服性文本的语言差异，为构建更鲁棒、可解释的检测工具提供了依据。
Sessa: Selective State Space Attention
Authors: Liubomyr Horbatko
First: 2026-04-20T17:59:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:04:34+00:00
Comments: v2: revised abstract for clarity; main results unchanged. Code available at: https://github.com/LibratioAI/sessa
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Modern sequence modeling is dominated by two families: Transformers, whose self-attention can access arbitrary elements of the visible sequence, and structured state-space models, which propagate information through an explicit recurrent state. These mechanisms face different limitations on long contexts: when attention is diffuse, the influence of individual tokens is diluted across the effective support, while recurrent state propagation can lose long-range sensitivity unless information is actively preserved. As a result, both mechanisms face challenges in preserving and selectively retrieving information over long contexts. We propose Sessa, a decoder that places attention inside a recurrent feedback path. This creates many attention-based paths through which past tokens can influence future states, rather than relying on a single attention read or a single recurrent chain. We prove that, under explicit assumptions and matched regimes, Sessa admits power-law memory tails $O(\ell^{-β})$ for $0 < β< 1$, with slower decay than in the corresponding Transformer and Mamba-style baselines. We further give an explicit construction that achieves this power-law rate. Under the same assumptions, Sessa is the only model class among those considered that realizes flexible selective retrieval, including profiles whose influence does not decay with distance. Consistent with this theoretical advantage, across matched experiments, Sessa achieves the strongest performance on long-context benchmarks while remaining competitive with Transformer and Mamba-style baselines on short-context language modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Sessa：选择性状态空间注意力
现代序列建模主要由两类模型主导：Transformer，其自注意力可访问可见序列中的任意元素；以及结构化状态空间模型，通过显式的递归状态传播信息。这两种机制在长上下文场景中面临不同限制：当注意力分散时，单个标记的影响会在有效支持范围内被稀释；而递归状态传播除非主动保留信息，否则会丧失长程敏感性。因此，两者在长上下文场景中均面临信息保留与选择性检索的挑战。我们提出Sessa，一种将注意力置于递归反馈路径中的解码器。这创造了多条基于注意力的路径，使过去标记能够影响未来状态，而非依赖单一注意力读取或单一递归链。我们证明，在显式假设和匹配条件下，Sessa具有幂律记忆尾部 $O(\ell^{-β})$（$0 < β< 1$），衰减速度慢于对应Transformer和Mamba风格基线。我们进一步给出实现该幂律速率的显式构造。在相同假设下，Sessa是所考虑模型类中唯一能实现灵活选择性检索的模型，包括影响力不随距离衰减的配置。与这一理论优势一致，在匹配实验中，Sessa在长上下文基准上表现最强，同时在短上下文语言建模任务上仍与Transformer和Mamba风格基线保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Transformers and state-space models in preserving selective information over long sequences—where attention dilutes token influence and recurrent chains lose long-range sensitivity—the paper introduces Sessa, a decoder that embeds attention within a recurrent feedback loop. This architecture enables multiple attention-based pathways for past tokens to influence future states, rather than relying on single attention reads or recurrent chains. Theoretically, under specific assumptions, Sessa achieves power-law memory tails decaying slower than Transformers or Mamba-style models, enabling flexible selective retrieval including non-decaying influence profiles. Experimentally, Sessa outperforms baselines on long-context benchmarks while remaining competitive on short-context tasks.
为了解决Transformer中注意力机制在长序列中信息稀释、以及状态空间模型中递归链难以保持长距离敏感性的缺陷，本文提出Sessa模型，将注意力机制嵌入递归反馈路径中，从而构建多条注意力路径以增强历史token对当前状态的影响。理论分析表明，在特定假设下，Sessa的记忆衰减遵循$O(\ell^{-β})$的幂律分布（$0 < β < 1$），衰减速度慢于Transformer和Mamba类模型，且是唯一支持灵活选择性检索（如影响不随距离衰减）的模型类别。实验结果表明，Sessa在长上下文任务上表现最优，同时在短上下文语言建模任务中仍保持与基线模型相当的性能。
Who Watches the Watchmen? Humans Disagree With Translation Metrics on Unseen Domains
Authors: Finn Schmidt, Jan Philip Wahle, Terry Ruas, Bela Gipp
First: 2026-04-19T11:42:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:02:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACL2026 (Findings)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic evaluation metrics are central to the development of machine translation systems, yet their robustness under domain shift remains unclear. Most metrics are developed on the Workshop on Machine Translation (WMT) benchmarks, raising concerns about their robustness to unseen domains. Prior studies that analyze unseen domains vary translation systems, annotators, or evaluation conditions, confounding domain effects with human annotation noise. To address these biases, we introduce a systematic multi-annotator Cross-Domain Error-Span-Annotation dataset (CD-ESA), comprising 18.8k human error span annotations across three language pairs, where we fix annotators within each language pair and evaluate translations of the same six translation systems across one seen news domain and two unseen technical domains. Using this dataset, we first find that automatic metrics appear surprisingly robust to domain-shifts at the segment level (up to 0.69 agreement), but this robustness largely disappears once we account for human label variation. Averaging annotations increases inter-annotator agreement by up to +0.11. Metrics struggle on the unseen chemical domain compared to humans (inter-annotator agreement of 0.78-0.83 vs. 0.96). We recommend comparing metric-human agreement against inter-annotator agreement, rather than comparing raw metric-human agreement alone, when evaluating across different domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：谁在监督守望者？人类对翻译评估指标在未见领域中的分歧
自动评估指标是机器翻译系统开发的核心，但其在领域迁移下的稳健性尚不明确。多数指标基于机器翻译研讨会（WMT）基准构建，引发对其在未见领域稳健性的担忧。此前研究分析未见领域时，常改变翻译系统、标注员或评估条件，导致领域效应与人类标注噪声混淆。 为解决这些偏差，我们引入系统性的多标注员跨域错误片段标注数据集（CD-ESA），包含三语对共18.8k个人类错误片段标注，每语对内固定标注员，并评估六种翻译系统在一则已见新闻领域和两则未见技术领域中的译文。利用该数据集，我们发现自动指标在段落层面表现出惊人的领域迁移稳健性（最高0.69一致性），但一旦考虑人类标注变异，该稳健性大幅消失。平均标注可使标注员间一致性提升最高+0.11。指标在未见化学领域表现逊于人类（标注员间一致性0.78-0.83 vs. 0.96）。 我们建议在跨领域评估时，应比较指标与人类的一致性与标注员间一致性，而非仅比较原始指标-人类一致性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how automatic translation metrics perform across unseen domains, motivated by concerns that metrics trained on WMT benchmarks may not generalize well. The authors introduce a controlled dataset, CD-ESA, with 18.8k human error span annotations across three language pairs and six translation systems, using fixed annotators per pair and evaluating both seen and unseen technical domains. Results show that metrics appear robust at the segment level (up to 0.69 agreement), but this robustness vanishes when human annotation variability is accounted for; averaging annotations improves inter-annotator agreement by up to 0.11. Metrics significantly underperform humans in the chemical domain (0.78–0.83 vs. 0.96 human agreement), suggesting metrics are less reliable in specialized contexts. The paper recommends evaluating metrics by comparing their agreement with humans against inter-annotator agreement, not raw metric-human scores alone.
该研究旨在评估自动翻译指标在未见领域中的表现，因现有指标多基于WMT基准训练，其泛化能力存疑。作者构建了CD-ESA数据集，包含18.8k个跨三语对的人工错误片段标注，固定每语对标注员，对六种翻译系统在一则已见新闻领域和两则未见技术领域进行评估。结果发现，指标在段落级与人类标注者的一致性高达0.69，但当考虑人类标注变异后，这种鲁棒性消失；平均标注可使标注者间一致性提升0.11。在化学领域，指标表现远逊于人类（0.78–0.83 vs. 0.96），表明其在专业领域可靠性不足。论文建议评估指标时，应比较其与人类的一致性与标注者间一致性，而非仅依赖原始指标-人类得分。
MiroThinker: Pushing the Performance Boundaries of Open-Source Research Agents via Model, Context, and Interactive Scaling
Authors: MiroMind Team, Song Bai, Lidong Bing, Carson Chen, Guanzheng Chen, Yuntao Chen, Zhe Chen, Ziyi Chen, Jifeng Dai, Xuan Dong, Wenhan Dou, Yue Deng, Yunjie Fu, Junqi Ge, Chenxia Han, Tammy Huang, Zhenhang Huang, Jerry Jiao, Shilei Jiang, Tianyu Jiao, Xiaoqi Jian, Lei Lei, Ruilin Li, Gen Luo, Tiantong Li, Xiang Lin, Ziyuan Liu, Zhiqi Li, Jie Ni, Qiang Ren, Pax Sun, Shiqian Su, Chenxin Tao, Bin Wang, Wenhai Wang, Haonan Wang, James Wang, Jin Wang, Jojo Wang, Letian Wang, Shizun Wang, Weizhi Wang, Zixuan Wang, Jinfan Xu, Sen Xing, Chenyu Yang, Hai Ye, Jiaheng Yu, Yue Yu, Muyan Zhong, Tianchen Zhao, Xizhou Zhu, Yanpeng Zhou, Yifan Zhang, Zhi Zhu
First: 2025-11-14T18:52:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:02:11+00:00
Comments: Technical Report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present MiroThinker v1.0, an open-source research agent designed to advance tool-augmented reasoning and information-seeking capabilities. Unlike previous agents that only scale up model size or context length, MiroThinker explores interaction scaling at the model level, systematically training the model to handle deeper and more frequent agent-environment interactions as a third dimension of performance improvement. Unlike LLM test-time scaling, which operates in isolation and risks degradation with longer reasoning chains, interactive scaling leverages environment feedback and external information acquisition to correct errors and refine trajectories. Through reinforcement learning, the model achieves efficient interaction scaling: with a 256K context window, it can perform up to 600 tool calls per task, enabling sustained multi-turn reasoning and complex real-world research workflows. Across four representative benchmarks-GAIA, HLE, BrowseComp, and BrowseComp-ZH-the 72B variant achieves up to 81.9%, 37.7%, 47.1%, and 55.6% accuracy respectively, surpassing previous open-source agents and approaching commercial counterparts such as GPT-5-high. Our analysis reveals that MiroThinker benefits from interactive scaling consistently: research performance improves predictably as the model engages in deeper and more frequent agent-environment interactions, demonstrating that interaction depth exhibits scaling behaviors analogous to model size and context length. These findings establish interaction scaling as a third critical dimension for building next-generation open research agents, complementing model capacity and context windows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MiroThinker：通过模型、上下文和交互式扩展推动开源研究代理性能边界
我们提出了MiroThinker v1.0，一款旨在提升工具增强推理与信息获取能力的开源研究代理。与以往仅扩展模型规模或上下文长度的代理不同，MiroThinker在模型层面探索交互式扩展，系统性地训练模型以应对更深层次、更频繁的代理-环境交互，作为性能提升的第三维度。与LLM测试时扩展不同，交互式扩展利用环境反馈和外部信息获取来纠正错误、优化轨迹，避免长推理链带来的性能退化。通过强化学习，模型实现了高效的交互式扩展：在256K上下文窗口下，每个任务最多可执行600次工具调用，支持持续多轮推理和复杂的现实研究工作流。在四个代表性基准（GAIA、HLE、BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH）上，72B版本分别达到81.9%、37.7%、47.1%和55.6%的准确率，超越此前开源代理，接近商业产品（如GPT-5-high）。我们的分析表明，MiroThinker始终受益于交互式扩展：研究性能随模型与环境交互的深度和频率增加而可预测地提升，表明交互深度的扩展行为与模型规模和上下文长度类似。这些发现确立了交互式扩展作为构建下一代开源研究代理的第三关键维度，与模型容量和上下文窗口相辅相成。

Using reinforcement learning, the agent learns to leverage environmental feedback and external information to correct reasoning errors and refine trajectories, enabling up to 600 tool calls per task within a 256K context window.
