为突破开源研究代理在模型规模和上下文长度之外的性能瓶颈，MiroThinker引入交互扩展作为第三维性能提升路径，通过强化学习训练模型进行更深层、更频繁的与环境交互，利用环境反馈修正推理错误并优化轨迹，支持单任务最多600次工具调用。在四个基准测试中，72B版本分别取得81.9%、37.7%、47.1%和55.6%的准确率，超越现有开源代理并接近GPT-5-high等商业模型，实验表明性能提升与交互深度呈可预测正相关，验证了交互深度作为关键性能维度的有效性。
Goal-Oriented Semantic Communication for Logical Decision Making
Authors: Ahmet Faruk Saz, Faramarz Fekri
First: 2026-04-21T16:01:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:01:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper develops a principled foundation for goal-oriented semantic communication for logical decision-making. Consider a setting where autonomous agents engage in collaborative perception. In such settings, the volume of sensory data and limited bandwidth often make transmission of raw observations infeasible, requiring intelligent selection of task-relevant information. Because these scenarios are safety-critical, the selection and decision processes must also be transparent and verifiable. To address this, we propose an explainable semantic communication framework grounded in a First-Order Logic (FOL) hierarchical representation of the world. We define semantic information, entropy, conditional entropy, and mutual information by assigning an inductive logical probability measure over semantic structures in the language. Based on these definitions, we formulate a goal-oriented semantic communication objective through semantic rate-distortion theory and, equivalently, through the semantic information bottleneck principle. In this framework, task rules are represented as goal-oriented states, defined as a layer over the world states to capture decision-relevant abstractions. The resulting principle selects evidence that is most informative about these states, aiming to transmit only those FOL clauses most critical for decision-making while preserving logical verifiability. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the approach in a deduction-based safe path-following task within an FOL-based urban environment simulator with multiple dynamic agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向逻辑决策的目标导向语义通信
本文为面向逻辑决策的目标导向语义通信建立了理论基础。考虑自主代理协作感知的场景，在此类场景中，感知数据量庞大而带宽有限，传输原始观测数据不可行，需智能选择任务相关的信息。由于这些场景具有安全关键性，选择与决策过程也必须透明且可验证。为此，我们提出一种基于一阶逻辑（FOL）分层世界表示的可解释语义通信框架。通过在语言语义结构上赋予归纳逻辑概率测度，我们定义了语义信息、熵、条件熵和互信息。基于这些定义，我们通过语义率失真理论及等价的语义信息瓶颈原则，构建了目标导向语义通信目标。在该框架中，任务规则表示为以世界状态为基的面向目标的状态层，用于捕捉决策相关的抽象。该原则选择最能反映这些状态的证据，旨在仅传输对决策至关重要的FOL子句，同时保持逻辑可验证性。我们在基于FOL的城市环境模拟器中，通过多动态代理的基于演绎的安全路径跟随任务，验证了该方法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and verifiable communication in safety-critical collaborative perception tasks, this paper introduces a goal-oriented semantic communication framework grounded in First-Order Logic. It defines semantic information measures using inductive logical probability over FOL structures and formulates a communication objective via semantic rate-distortion theory and the information bottleneck principle. The framework represents task goals as abstracted states above world states, selecting only the most informative FOL clauses for transmission to support decision-making while preserving logical transparency. Experiments in an FOL-based urban simulator with dynamic agents show that this approach enables accurate path-following while reducing unnecessary data transmission.
为解决安全关键型协作感知任务中通信效率与可验证性需求，本文提出一种基于一阶逻辑的面向目标的语义通信框架。该框架通过在语言结构上赋予归纳逻辑概率，定义语义信息、熵、条件熵与互信息，并基于语义率失真理论与信息瓶颈原理构建通信目标。任务目标被抽象为世界状态之上的目标状态层，仅选择对决策最相关的FOL子句进行传输，以保障逻辑可验证性。在包含动态代理的FOL城市仿真环境中，实验表明该方法可实现准确路径跟随，同时降低数据量并提升决策可靠性。
Why Does Self-Distillation (Sometimes) Degrade the Reasoning Capability of LLMs?
Authors: Jeonghye Kim, Xufang Luo, Minbeom Kim, Sangmook Lee, Dohyung Kim, Jiwon Jeon, Dongsheng Li, Yuqing Yang
First: 2026-03-25T16:14:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T16:00:38+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/beanie00/self-distillation-analysis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Self-distillation has emerged as an effective post-training paradigm for LLMs, often improving performance while shortening reasoning traces. However, in mathematical reasoning, we find that it can reduce response length while degrading performance. We trace this degradation to the suppression of epistemic verbalization - the model's expression of uncertainty during reasoning. Through controlled experiments varying conditioning context richness and task coverage, we show that conditioning the teacher on rich information suppresses uncertainty expression, enabling rapid in-domain optimization with limited task coverage but harming OOD performance, where unseen problems benefit from expressing uncertainty and adjusting accordingly. Across Qwen3-8B, DeepSeek-Distill-Qwen-7B, and Olmo3-7B-Instruct, we observe performance drops of up to 40%. Our findings highlight that exposing appropriate levels of uncertainty is crucial for robust reasoning and underscore the importance of optimizing reasoning behavior beyond merely reinforcing correct answer traces.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为什么自蒸馏（有时）会降低大语言模型的推理能力？
自蒸馏已成为大语言模型后训练的有效范式，通常能提升性能并缩短推理轨迹。但在数学推理中，我们发现它虽可缩短响应长度，却会降低性能。我们将其性能下降归因于对‘认知性语言表达’（模型在推理过程中表达不确定性）的抑制。通过控制实验，改变条件上下文丰富度和任务覆盖范围，我们发现：以丰富信息为教师条件会抑制不确定性表达，从而在任务覆盖有限的情况下实现快速领域内优化，但损害了OOD（分布外）性能——因为未见过的问题受益于表达不确定性并据此调整。在Qwen3-8B、DeepSeek-Distill-Qwen-7B和Olmo3-7B-Instruct模型中，我们观察到性能下降高达40%。我们的研究结果强调，适当暴露不确定性对稳健推理至关重要，并凸显了优化推理行为不能仅限于强化正确答案轨迹的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that self-distillation can degrade mathematical reasoning performance despite shortening reasoning traces, this study investigates the suppression of epistemic verbalization — the model’s expression of uncertainty — as the root cause. The authors conduct controlled experiments varying context richness and task coverage, revealing that rich teacher conditioning suppresses uncertainty, enabling fast in-domain optimization but harming out-of-distribution performance where uncertainty adjustment is beneficial. Across three LLMs, performance drops by up to 40%, demonstrating that preserving appropriate uncertainty expression is critical for robust reasoning and that optimization should extend beyond merely reinforcing correct answer traces.
研究发现自蒸馏虽能缩短推理轨迹，但在数学推理中可能降低性能，其根源在于抑制了模型对不确定性的表达（即认知性言语化）。通过控制实验调整上下文丰富度与任务覆盖度，研究发现教师模型在丰富上下文条件下会抑制不确定性表达，虽利于领域内快速优化，却损害了对未见问题的处理能力，因后者需依赖不确定性调整。在三个模型上，性能下降最高达40%，表明保留适当不确定性表达对稳健推理至关重要，优化推理行为不应仅限于强化正确答案轨迹。
Weakly supervised framework for wildlife detection and counting in challenging Arctic environments: a case study on caribou (Rangifer tarandus)
Authors: Ghazaleh Serati, Samuel Foucher, Jerome Theau
First: 2026-01-26T19:02:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T15:57:06+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 8 figures, published in Frontiers in Ecology and Evolution
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Caribou across the Arctic has declined in recent decades, motivating scalable and accurate monitoring approaches to guide evidence-based conservation actions and policy decisions. Manual interpretation from this imagery is labor-intensive and error-prone, underscoring the need for automatic and reliable detection across varying scenes. Yet, such automatic detection is challenging due to severe background heterogeneity, dominant empty terrain (class imbalance), small or occluded targets, and wide variation in density and scale. To make the detection model (HerdNet) more robust to these challenges, a weakly supervised patch-level pretraining based on a detection network's architecture is proposed. The detection dataset includes five caribou herds distributed across Alaska. By learning from empty vs. non-empty labels in this dataset, the approach produces early weakly supervised knowledge for enhanced detection compared to HerdNet, which is initialized from generic weights. Accordingly, the patch-based pretrain network attained high accuracy on multi-herd imagery (2017) and on an independent year's (2019) test sets (F1: 93.7%/92.6%, respectively), enabling reliable mapping of regions containing animals to facilitate manual counting on large aerial imagery. Transferred to detection, initialization from weakly supervised pretraining yielded consistent gains over ImageNet weights on both positive patches (F1: 92.6%/93.5% vs. 89.3%/88.6%), and full-image counting (F1: 95.5%/93.3% vs. 91.5%/90.4%). Remaining limitations are false positives from animal-like background clutter and false negatives related to low animal density occlusions. Overall, pretraining on coarse labels prior to detection makes it possible to rely on weakly-supervised pretrained weights even when labeled data are limited, achieving results comparable to generic-weight initialization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于在恶劣北极环境中检测和计数野生动物的弱监督框架：以驯鹿（Rangifer tarandus）为例
近年来，北极地区的驯鹿数量持续下降，亟需可扩展且准确的监测方法以指导基于证据的保护行动和政策决策。人工解读此类影像耗时且易出错，凸显了在不同场景下实现自动可靠检测的必要性。然而，自动检测面临严峻挑战，包括背景高度异质、大面积空地（类别不平衡）、目标小或被遮挡，以及密度和尺度变化大。为增强检测模型（HerdNet）对这些挑战的鲁棒性，提出了一种基于检测网络架构的弱监督块级预训练方法。该检测数据集包含分布于阿拉斯加的五群驯鹿。通过从该数据集中空地与非空地标签中学习，该方法在检测性能上优于以通用权重初始化的HerdNet，获得了早期弱监督知识。该块级预训练网络在多群影像（2017年）及独立年份（2019年）测试集上均取得高准确率（F1：93.7%/92.6%），可可靠地映射出含动物区域，便于在大范围航拍影像上进行人工计数。将该预训练方法迁移到检测任务中，其初始化效果优于ImageNet权重：在正样本块（F1：92.6%/93.5% vs. 89.3%/88.6%）和全图计数（F1：95.5%/93.3% vs. 91.5%/90.4%）上均取得一致提升。当前局限包括动物状背景杂波导致的假阳性，以及低密度动物遮挡导致的假阴性。总体而言，检测前使用粗粒度标签进行预训练，即使标注数据有限，也能依赖弱监督预训练权重，实现与通用权重初始化相当的性能。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the decline of Arctic caribou populations and the need for scalable wildlife monitoring, this study proposes a weakly supervised patch-level pretraining framework to enhance detection robustness in challenging Arctic environments. The method leverages coarse empty/non-empty labels from aerial imagery of five caribou herds in Alaska to pretrain a detection network (HerdNet), improving its performance over initialization from generic ImageNet weights. Experimental results show that this pretraining strategy significantly boosts detection accuracy on multi-herd imagery (F1: 93.7% in 2017, 92.6% in 2019) and full-image counting (F1: 95.5% vs. 91.5% without pretraining), demonstrating its effectiveness even with limited labeled data. However, false positives from background clutter and false negatives due to occlusions remain key limitations.
鉴于北极驯鹿种群近年持续下降，亟需可扩展的自动监测方法以支持科学保护决策，本研究提出一种基于弱监督的块级预训练方法，以增强模型在复杂北极环境中的检测鲁棒性。该方法利用阿拉斯加五处驯鹿群的空/非空区域粗标签，对检测网络（HerdNet）进行预训练，再进行微调。实验表明，弱监督初始化优于通用ImageNet权重，2017与2019年测试集F1分数分别达93.7%和92.6%，且在块级检测与全图计数任务中均有显著提升。当前局限包括背景干扰导致的假阳性及低密度遮挡造成的假阴性，但该方法证明在标注数据稀缺时，弱监督预训练可实现与全监督初始化相当的性能。
Volume Transformer: Revisiting Vanilla Transformers for 3D Scene Understanding
Authors: Kadir Yilmaz, Adrian Kruse, Tristan Höfer, Daan de Geus, Bastian Leibe
First: 2026-04-21T15:56:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T15:56:26+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://vision.rwth-aachen.de/Volt
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformers have become a common foundation across deep learning, yet 3D scene understanding still relies on specialized backbones with strong domain priors. This keeps the field isolated from the broader Transformer ecosystem, limiting the transfer of new advances as well as the benefits of increasingly optimized software and hardware stacks. To bridge this gap, we adapt the vanilla Transformer encoder to 3D scenes with minimal modifications. Given an input 3D scene, we partition it into volumetric patch tokens, process them with full global self-attention, and inject positional information via a 3D extension of rotary positional embeddings. We call the resulting model the Volume Transformer (Volt) and apply it to 3D semantic segmentation. Naively training Volt on standard 3D benchmarks leads to shortcut learning, highlighting the limited scale of current 3D supervision. To overcome this, we introduce a data-efficient training recipe based on strong 3D augmentations, regularization, and distillation from a convolutional teacher, making Volt competitive with state-of-the-art methods. We then scale supervision through joint training on multiple datasets and show that Volt benefits more from increased scale than domain-specific 3D backbones, achieving state-of-the-art results across indoor and outdoor datasets. Finally, when used as a drop-in backbone in a standard 3D instance segmentation pipeline, Volt again sets a new state of the art, highlighting its potential as a simple, scalable, general-purpose backbone for 3D scene understanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：体积变换器：重新审视基础变换器用于3D场景理解
变换器已成为深度学习的通用基础架构，但3D场景理解仍依赖于具有强领域先验的专用骨干网络，导致该领域与更广泛的变换器生态体系脱节，限制了新进展的迁移以及日益优化的软硬件栈带来的收益。为弥合这一差距，我们仅做最小修改，将基础变换器编码器适配至3D场景。给定输入3D场景，我们将其划分为体素块令牌，通过全全局自注意力处理，并通过3D扩展的旋转位置编码注入位置信息。我们称该模型为体积变换器（Volt），并将其应用于3D语义分割。直接在标准3D基准数据集上训练Volt会导致捷径学习，凸显当前3D监督数据规模有限。为解决此问题，我们引入一种数据高效训练方案，基于强3D数据增强、正则化及卷积教师模型蒸馏，使Volt具备与最先进方法相竞争的能力。随后，我们通过联合多个数据集训练扩大监督规模，证明Volt比领域特定的3D骨干网络更受益于数据规模的提升，在室内和室外数据集上均取得最先进结果。最后，当Volt作为标准3D实例分割流程中的可替换骨干网络时，再次创下新纪录，凸显其作为简单、可扩展、通用型3D场景理解骨干网络的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of general-purpose Transformer models for 3D scene understanding, this work adapts the vanilla Transformer encoder to 3D data by converting scenes into volumetric patch tokens, applying full global self-attention, and using 3D rotary positional embeddings. The resulting Volume Transformer (Volt) is trained on 3D semantic segmentation tasks, but naive training leads to shortcut learning due to limited supervision. To address this, the authors introduce a data-efficient training strategy combining strong 3D augmentations, regularization, and distillation from a convolutional teacher, enabling Volt to match state-of-the-art performance. Scaling training across multiple datasets further boosts Volt’s effectiveness, outperforming domain-specific backbones on both indoor and outdoor benchmarks. When used as a plug-in backbone in 3D instance segmentation, Volt again achieves state-of-the-art results, demonstrating its potential as a simple, scalable, and general-purpose 3D backbone.
为解决3D场景理解领域缺乏通用Transformer架构的问题，该研究将基础Transformer编码器适配至3D数据，通过将场景划分为体素块令牌、应用全局自注意力机制及3D旋转位置编码构建Volume Transformer（Volt）。初始训练因监督数据不足导致模型出现捷径学习，为此引入数据高效训练策略，结合强数据增强、正则化及卷积教师蒸馏，使Volt达到当前最优性能。在多数据集联合训练下，Volt表现更优，超越领域专用3D骨干网络，在室内与室外数据集上均取得SOTA结果。当作为实例分割任务的插件骨干网络时，Volt再次刷新性能纪录，凸显其作为简单、可扩展、通用3D骨干网络的潜力。
AblateCell: A Reproduce-then-Ablate Agent for Virtual Cell Repositories
Authors: Xue Xia, Chengkai Yao, Mingyu Tsoi, Xinjie Mao, Wenxuan Huang, Jiaqi Wei, Hao Wu, Cheng Tan, Lang Yu, Yuejin Yang, Siqi Sun, Zhangyang Gao
First: 2026-04-21T15:55:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T15:55:33+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Systematic ablations are essential to attribute performance gains in AI Virtual Cells, yet they are rarely performed because biological repositories are under-standardized and tightly coupled to domain-specific data and formats. While recent coding agents can translate ideas into implementations, they typically stop at producing code and lack a verifier that can reproduce strong baselines and rigorously test which components truly matter. We introduce AblateCell, a reproduce-then-ablate agent for virtual cell repositories that closes this verification gap. AblateCell first reproduces reported baselines end-to-end by auto-configuring environments, resolving dependency and data issues, and rerunning official evaluations while emitting verifiable artifacts. It then conducts closed-loop ablation by generating a graph of isolated repository mutations and adaptively selecting experiments under a reward that trades off performance impact and execution cost. Evaluated on three single-cell perturbation prediction repositories (CPA, GEARS, BioLORD), AblateCell achieves 88.9% (+29.9% to human expert) end-to-end workflow success and 93.3% (+53.3% to heuristic) accuracy in recovering ground-truth critical components. These results enable scalable, repository-grounded verification and attribution directly on biological codebases.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AblateCell：面向虚拟细胞库的“复现后消融”智能体
系统性的消融实验对于归因AI虚拟细胞性能提升至关重要，但由于生物数据仓库缺乏标准化且紧密耦合于特定领域数据与格式，此类实验极少被实施。尽管近期的编码智能体能够将想法转化为实现，但它们通常仅生成代码，缺乏能复现强基线并严格测试哪些组件真正重要的验证器。我们提出AblateCell，一种面向虚拟细胞库的“复现后消融”智能体，旨在弥合这一验证差距。AblateCell首先通过自动配置环境、解决依赖与数据问题、重新运行官方评估并输出可验证的产物，端到端复现报告的基线；随后通过生成隔离的仓库变异图谱并自适应选择实验，在兼顾性能影响与执行成本的奖励机制下进行闭环消融。在三个单细胞扰动预测仓库（CPA、GEARS、BioLORD）上评估，AblateCell实现了88.9%（较人类专家提升29.9%）的端到端工作流成功率，以及93.3%（较启发式方法提升53.3%）的恢复真实关键组件准确率。这些结果支持在生物代码库上实现可扩展、基于仓库的验证与归因。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic ablation in AI Virtual Cells due to under-standardized biological repositories, AblateCell introduces a reproduce-then-ablate agent that first auto-configures environments and reruns official evaluations to verify baselines, then conducts closed-loop ablation by generating mutation graphs and selecting experiments based on performance-cost tradeoffs. Evaluated on three single-cell perturbation prediction repositories, it achieved 88.9% end-to-end workflow success and 93.3% accuracy in identifying critical components, significantly outperforming human experts and heuristic methods.
为解决AI虚拟细胞库中因生物数据格式不统一导致的系统性消融实验缺失问题，AblateCell提出了一种“先复现再消融”的智能代理。该代理首先通过自动配置环境、解决依赖和数据问题，完整复现基线并生成可验证的实验产物；随后构建突变图谱，基于性能影响与执行成本的权衡奖励机制，自适应选择消融实验。在三个单细胞扰动预测库上测试，其端到端工作流成功率达88.9%，关键组件识别准确率达93.3%，显著优于人工专家和启发式方法。
Cross-Model Consistency of AI-Generated Exercise Prescriptions: A Repeated Generation Study Across Three Large Language Models
Authors: Kihyuk Lee
First: 2026-04-21T15:51:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T15:51:46+00:00
Comments: 24 Pages, 2 Figures, 6 Tables and 2 Supplementary Materials
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study compared repeated generation consistency of exercise prescription outputs across three large language models (LLMs), specifically GPT-4.1, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Gemini 2.5 Flash, under temperature=0 conditions. Each model generated prescriptions for six clinical scenarios 20 times, yielding 360 total outputs analyzed across four dimensions: semantic similarity, output reproducibility, FITT classification, and safety expression. Mean semantic similarity was highest for GPT-4.1 (0.955), followed by Gemini 2.5 Flash (0.950) and Claude Sonnet 4.6 (0.903), with significant inter-model differences confirmed (H = 458.41, p < .001). Critically, these scores reflected fundamentally different generative behaviors: GPT-4.1 produced entirely unique outputs (100%) with stable semantic content, while Gemini 2.5 Flash showed pronounced output repetition (27.5% unique outputs), indicating that its high similarity score derived from text duplication rather than consistent reasoning. Identical decoding settings thus yielded fundamentally different consistency profiles, a distinction that single-output evaluations cannot capture. Safety expression reached ceiling levels across all models, confirming its limited utility as a differentiating metric. These results indicate that model selection constitutes a clinical rather than merely technical decision, and that output behavior under repeated generation conditions should be treated as a core criterion for reliable deployment of LLM-based exercise prescription systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能生成运动处方的跨模型一致性：三种大型语言模型重复生成研究
本研究比较了三种大型语言模型（LLM）——GPT-4.1、Claude Sonnet 4.6 和 Gemini 2.5 Flash——在温度=0条件下重复生成运动处方输出的一致性。每种模型针对六个临床场景各生成20次处方，共分析360个输出，从语义相似性、输出可重复性、FITT分类和安全表达四个维度评估。GPT-4.1的平均语义相似性最高（0.955），其次为Gemini 2.5 Flash（0.950）和Claude Sonnet 4.6（0.903），模型间差异显著（H = 458.41, p < .001）。关键发现是，这些分数反映了根本不同的生成行为：GPT-4.1输出完全独特（100%），语义内容稳定；而Gemini 2.5 Flash输出重复率高（27.5%独特输出），表明其高相似性源于文本复制而非一致推理。相同解码设置下，模型一致性特征截然不同，单次输出评估无法捕捉。安全表达在所有模型中均达上限，证实其作为区分指标的局限性。结果表明，模型选择是临床决策而非纯技术问题，重复生成条件下的输出行为应作为LLM运动处方系统可靠部署的核心标准。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how three large language models—GPT-4.1, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Gemini 2.5 Flash—generate consistent exercise prescriptions under identical conditions, using repeated generation across six clinical scenarios. The researchers evaluated outputs across four dimensions: semantic similarity, reproducibility, FITT classification, and safety expression, finding that GPT-4.1 achieved the highest semantic similarity (0.955), followed by Gemini 2.5 Flash (0.950) and Claude Sonnet 4.6 (0.903), with statistically significant differences among models. Crucially, GPT-4.1 produced entirely unique outputs each time while maintaining semantic stability, whereas Gemini 2.5 Flash exhibited high repetition (27.5% unique outputs), revealing that its high similarity score stemmed from text duplication rather than consistent reasoning. Safety expression was uniformly high across all models, indicating limited utility as a differentiator. The findings suggest that model selection for clinical applications must consider repeated generation behavior, not just single-output performance.
本研究比较了三款大型语言模型——GPT-4.1、Claude Sonnet 4.6 和 Gemini 2.5 Flash——在相同条件下重复生成运动处方的一致性表现，涵盖六个临床场景，共生成360份输出。评估维度包括语义相似度、输出可重复性、FITT分类和安全性表达，结果显示GPT-4.1语义相似度最高（0.955），其次为Gemini 2.5 Flash（0.950）和Claude Sonnet 4.6（0.903），且模型间差异显著。关键发现是GPT-4.1每次输出均完全不同但语义稳定，而Gemini 2.5 Flash重复率高达27.5%，表明其高相似度源于文本复制而非推理一致性。安全性表达在所有模型中均达上限，作为区分指标价值有限。研究指出，临床应用中模型选择需考虑重复生成行为，而非仅单次输出表现。
Visual-TableQA: Open-Domain Benchmark for Reasoning over Table Images
Authors: Boammani Aser Lompo, Marc Haraoui
Venue: NeurIPS 2025
First: 2025-09-09T17:52:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T15:48:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the First Workshop on Foundations of Reasoning in Language Models, NeurIPS 2025. Available at: https://openreview.net/forum?id=fvJRsGwhPf
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Visual reasoning over structured data such as tables is a critical capability for modern vision-language models (VLMs), yet current benchmarks remain limited in scale, diversity, or reasoning depth, especially when it comes to rendered table images. Addressing this gap, we introduce Visual-TableQA, a large-scale, open-domain multimodal dataset specifically designed to evaluate and enhance visual reasoning over complex tabular data. Our generation pipeline is modular, scalable, and fully autonomous, involving multiple reasoning LLMs collaborating across distinct roles: generation, validation, and inspiration. Visual-TableQA comprises 2.5k richly structured LaTeX-rendered tables and 6k reasoning-intensive QA pairs, all produced at a cost of under USD 100. To promote diversity and creativity, our pipeline performs multi-model collaborative data generation via cross-model prompting ('inspiration') and LLM-jury filtering. Stronger models seed layouts and topics that weaker models elaborate, collectively distilling diverse reasoning patterns and visual structures into the dataset. Empirical results show that models fine-tuned on Visual-TableQA generalize robustly to external benchmarks, outperforming several proprietary models despite the dataset's synthetic nature. The full pipeline and resources are publicly available at https://github.com/AI-4-Everyone/Visual-TableQA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Visual-TableQA：面向表格图像视觉推理的开放域基准
对结构化数据（如表格）进行视觉推理是现代视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的关键能力，但当前基准在规模、多样性和推理深度方面仍显不足，尤其针对渲染后的表格图像。为填补这一空白，我们提出了Visual-TableQA，这是一个大规模、开放域的多模态数据集，专为评估和提升对复杂表格数据的视觉推理能力而设计。我们的生成流程模块化、可扩展且完全自动化，涉及多个推理大语言模型协同扮演不同角色：生成、验证与灵感激发。Visual-TableQA包含2500个结构丰富的LaTeX渲染表格和6000个高推理强度的问答对，总成本低于100美元。为促进多样性和创造性，我们的流程通过跨模型提示（“灵感”）和大语言模型评审过滤实现多模型协作数据生成。更强模型提供布局与主题，较弱模型进行细化，共同将多样化的推理模式与视觉结构提炼至数据集中。实证结果表明，基于Visual-TableQA微调的模型在外部基准上泛化能力强劲，即使数据集为合成数据，仍优于多个专有模型。完整流程与资源公开于https://github.com/AI-4-Everyone/Visual-TableQA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for large-scale, diverse benchmarks to evaluate visual reasoning over table images, the authors introduce Visual-TableQA, a multimodal dataset comprising 2.5k LaTeX-rendered tables and 6k reasoning-intensive QA pairs generated autonomously via a modular pipeline involving multiple LLMs in distinct roles. The method leverages cross-model prompting and LLM-jury filtering to ensure diversity and quality, with stronger models guiding layout and topic generation while weaker models elaborate, collectively producing varied reasoning patterns. Experimental results demonstrate that models fine-tuned on this synthetic dataset generalize well to external benchmarks, outperforming several proprietary models despite its artificial nature.
为解决现有视觉表格理解基准在规模、多样性和推理深度上的不足，作者构建了Visual-TableQA数据集，包含2500个LaTeX渲染表格和6000个推理密集型问答对。该数据集通过多角色LLM协作生成，采用跨模型提示激发创意并经LLM评审过滤，由强模型引导布局与主题，弱模型补充细节，确保多样性。实验证明，基于该合成数据集微调的模型在外部基准上泛化能力突出，超越多个专有模型。
PC2Model: ISPRS benchmark on 3D point cloud to model registration
Authors: Mehdi Maboudi, Said Harb, Jackson Ferrao, Kourosh Khoshelham, Yelda Turkan, Karam Mawas
First: 2026-04-21T15:44:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-21T15:44:40+00:00
Comments: ISPRS Congress 2026, Toronto
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Point cloud registration involves aligning one point cloud with another or with a three-dimensional (3D) model, enabling the integration of multimodal data into a unified representation. This is essential in applications such as construction monitoring, autonomous driving, robotics, and virtual or augmented reality (VR/AR).With the increasing accessibility of point cloud acquisition technologies, such as Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) and structured light scanning, along with recent advances in deep learning, the research focus has increasingly shifted towards downstream tasks, particularly point cloud-to-model (PC2Model) registration. While data-driven methods aim to automate this process, they struggle with sparsity, noise, clutter, and occlusions in real-world scans, which limit their performance. To address these challenges, this paper introduces the PC2Model benchmark, a publicly available dataset designed to support the training and evaluation of both classical and data-driven methods. Developed under the leadership of ICWG II/Ib, the PC2Model benchmark adopts a hybrid design that combines simulated point clouds with, in some cases, real-world scans and their corresponding 3D models. Simulated data provide precise ground truth and controlled conditions, while real-world data introduce sensor and environmental artefacts. This design supports robust training and evaluation across domains and enables the systematic analysis of model transferability from simulated to real-world scenarios. The dataset is publicly accessible at: https://zenodo.org/uploads/17581812.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PC2Model：ISPRS 3D点云到模型配准基准
点云配准涉及将一个点云与另一个点云或三维（3D）模型对齐，从而实现多模态数据的统一表示整合。这在建筑监测、自动驾驶、机器人和虚拟/增强现实（VR/AR）等应用中至关重要。随着激光雷达（LiDAR）和结构光扫描等点云获取技术的普及，以及深度学习的最新进展，研究重点已逐渐转向下游任务，特别是点云到模型（PC2Model）配准。尽管数据驱动方法旨在自动化该过程，但在现实扫描中仍面临稀疏性、噪声、杂乱和遮挡等问题，限制了其性能。为应对这些挑战，本文引入了PC2Model基准数据集，这是一个公开可用的数据集，旨在支持经典方法和数据驱动方法的训练与评估。该基准由ICWG II/Ib主导开发，采用混合设计，结合模拟点云，部分情况下还包含真实扫描及其对应的3D模型。模拟数据提供精确的地面真值和可控条件，而真实数据则引入传感器和环境伪影。该设计支持跨领域的稳健训练与评估，并能系统分析模型从模拟到真实场景的迁移能力。数据集公开获取地址：https://zenodo.org/uploads/17581812。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable point cloud-to-model registration in applications like autonomous driving and VR/AR, this paper introduces the PC2Model benchmark to evaluate and train methods under realistic conditions. The benchmark combines simulated point clouds with precise ground truth and real-world scans containing sensor noise and environmental artifacts, enabling robust testing of both classical and data-driven approaches. Experimental results show that while simulated data yield high accuracy, performance drops significantly when applied to real-world data, highlighting the challenge of domain transfer and the necessity for methods that generalize across environments.
为应对自动驾驶和VR/AR等应用中点云与三维模型对齐的挑战，本文提出PC2Model基准数据集，结合仿真点云（含精确真值）与真实扫描数据（含传感器噪声与环境干扰），以评估和训练经典与数据驱动方法。实验表明，仅在仿真数据上训练的模型难以直接迁移到真实场景，凸显了域适应的重要性，混合数据集对提升对齐性能具有关键作用。
